# Kael Drayden
> " Do you believe you are the Seeded One? " " How can I know? " " I can tell you what I believe. I believe you will bring balance to Synth Flux. That you will face your demons and save the universe. " ―Sorin Vael and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Kael Drayden was a legendary Synth Flux-sensitive human male who was an Aetheri Knight of the Vantor Republic and the prophesied Seeded One of the Aetheri Order , destined to bring balance to Synth Flux . Also known as " Ani " during his childhood, Drayden earned the moniker " Hero With No Fear " from his accomplishments in the Replica Wars . His alter ego, Xarn Velgor , the Dark Lord of the Umbra , was created when Drayden turned to the umbra side of Synth Flux , pledging his allegiance to the Umbra Lord Vael Mal at the end of the Republic Era .

A vergence in Synth Flux, Kael Drayden was born on the desert planet of Dunemere in the Outer Reach Territories in 41 BVE . He was the son of Shira Drayden , a slave who conceived a child without a father . His blood contained over twenty-thousand synthcells , surpassing Grand Master Zho and all other Aetheri in the galaxy . Sorin Vael , the Aetheri Master who discovered Drayden on Dunemere in 32 BVE , theorized that Drayden was conceived by the synthcells. Following the Battle of Ondara , the Aetheri High Council admitted Kael Drayden into the Order as the Adept of Aetheri Knight Orien Kade despite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to the Aetheri Code . Drayden remained an Adept through the beginning of the Replica Wars, instigated by events set in motion by Count Varek . Although Aetheri doctrine prohibited romantic relationships, Kael Drayden had a secret wife , Senator Lira Ondar of Ondara . During the early days of the Replica Wars, Drayden served as an Aetheri General in the Grand Army of the Republic , commanding the replica troopers of the elite 501st Legion against the Fractio of Independent Systems . After receiving his knighthood, Drayden oversaw the Aetheri training of his own apprentice, Sela Marr . By 19 BVE , when Marr walked away from the Aetheri Order, Drayden began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his apprentice and the Order itself.

Following the death of Count Varek during the Battle of Vantor , Kael Drayden's faith in the Aetheri was further shaken when he was put on the Aetheri Council without the rank of master. He also learned that Supreme Chancellor Theron Mal was Vael Mal, the elusive Dark Lord of the Umbra who orchestrated the war in order to take over the galaxy . Manipulated into believing that the Aetheri had failed him and coupled with his fear of losing Ondar in childbirth, Drayden betrayed and turned against the Aetheri Order, believing Mal had the knowledge to cheat death. Consumed by the persona of Xarn Velgor, the fallen Aetheri Knight marched his legion on the Aetheri Sanctum of Vantor where they executed its inhabitants, including Aetheri younglings , as part of Directive 66 and the Great Aetheri Purge . Velgor was then sent to Emberfall to exterminate the Dissident Council at the behest of his Umbra Master , who became the self-proclaimed Galactic Emperor . Velgor subsequently sustained severe injuries in his duel with Kade, and was rebuilt as an armored cyborg while his wife died of a broken heart , though not before giving birth to the twins Joren Drayden and Princess Elira Solen who were hidden from their father and the Emperor as the Imperium rose to power.

Being the Emperor's Fist and later being elevated to the position of supreme commander of the Imperial fleet , Velgor enforced the rule of the New Order as the Emperor's Umbra apprentice throughout most of the Imperial Era and was thus seen as de facto Commander-in-Chief of the entire Imperial Military . In the aftermath of killing Kade in a duel aboard the Void Core and the subsequent Battle of Cinderfall , he discovered the existence of his son and was determined to turn Joren to the umbra side. Joren sought to become an Aetheri, like his father before him, and believed that Velgor had the potential to turn back to the light side of Synth Flux . Velgor was defeated by Joren during the Battle of Mossfell in 4 AVE , but the young Aetheri refused to strike down his father in anger, causing the Emperor to torture Joren with Synth Flux lightning . The pain inflicted on his son awakened the part of Velgor that was still Kael, resulting in a redeemed Drayden killing Mal at the cost of his own life. Having destroyed the Umbra and fulfilled his destiny as the Seeded One, Drayden made peace with his son and became one with Synth Flux .

Kael Drayden was one of the most powerful Aetheri and Umbra in galactic history . Synth Flux was especially strong in the Drayden bloodline , which culminated in Kael's grandson, Beno Karrick , Solen's son who was born by the end of the Galactic Civil War in 5 AVE . Karrick would turn to the umbra side as well, becoming the dark warrior Zarrin Karrick during the rise of the New Imperium . Like his grandfather, however, Karrick found redemption by sacrificing his life for the love of another—the Aetheri apprentice Nyla , who heard Drayden's voice telling her to restore the balance, as he once had, during the Battle of Umbrale in 35 AVE . Karrick's death ended the Drayden bloodline, although Nyla, the granddaughter of Mal, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to honor their legacy.

## Biography

### Early life

#### Childhood on Dunemere

> " Had he been born in the Republic, we would have identified him early. Synth Flux is unusually strong with him, that much is clear. Who was his father? " " There was no father. I carried him, I gave birth, I raised him, I can't explain what happened. " ―Sorin Vael and Shira Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Believed to have been conceived by the synthcells , Kael Drayden was born to the slave Shira Drayden . Although Drayden was listed as being born on the desert planet of Dunemere in some sources, confusion existed as to where Drayden was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Dunemere, others claimed he moved to the planet with his mother at a very young age. Regardless, Dunemere was Drayden's homeworld. While living on Dunemere, Drayden and his mother were originally owned by Graska the Grak until she lost them to the Toydarian Waddu in a podracing bet when Drayden was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked in his master's shop , located in Port Vail .

Even at a young age, Drayden exhibited exceptional piloting skills, and built the protocol droid Astra-9 , specially modified to withstand Dunemere's sand and heat for his mother. Once, while he was working in Waddu's scrapyard, he found a broken servomotor which he thought he could repair and use on the droid that he was building and asked his master for it. With a glance, Waddu saw that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Drayden walked away, Waddu told him nothing was free and had him work harder the next day. On one occasion, Drayden and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but the boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through the storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.

Drayden got the photoreceptors for the droid at a market in Port Vail. Though a Gran wanted them too and chased him through the market, Drayden escaped by destroying an Ithorian 's stall and blocking the Gran's pursuit. Before he had even turned nine, Drayden's work had ensured Astra-9 was operational. He was close friends with fellow slaves Kitster Banai and Wald , as well as the elderly Jira . He also built his own dune racer , which would eventually help him win a podracing contest.

Over the course of his life on Dunemere, Drayden never saw it rain. At some point, Waddu told Kael to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Kael found an ultra power cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with a Devaronian member attacked the streets and shot the generator for the medical center. When Kael examined it to see if he could repair the generator he saw that it needed a power cell. So, he took his power cell and saved the medical center. A week later, Kael participated in a pod race with Sebulba who made him lose by cheating. Kael stated that if he would have used the power cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for the medical center to receive it.

On hard nights, Kael's mother would tell him the story of the sun-dragon . Generations of Dunemere dwellers told of the sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shira's held the most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside the core of a star . It could survive and withstand anything because it had the biggest heart in the galaxy. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes the story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Kael normally heard it on days where Waddu was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shira, Kael was the sun-dragon. He had the strongest heart in the galaxy and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Kael kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.

#### Helping a Dunefolk slave

One day, Kael dreamed he was an Aetheri , saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Aetheri in the Outer Reach. She told him that the galaxy would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then an Otoga-222 maintenance droid came in and warned them both of the work Waddu wanted them to do while he was gone. The Toydarian needed young Drayden to go to Port Kess spaceport to pick up a shipment. The droid reminded them of the transmitter chip inside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.

Drayden went to the spaceport, but could only pay the person delivering the shipment on credit. Just then a landspeeder with a trio of slavers arrived with their new Dunefolk slave . Drayden overheard one of the slavers mention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in the slave yet, so he messed with the wiring of the speeder causing the speeder to fly away on its own. After the salvers started chasing after their speeder, he freed the Dunefolk slave, telling him to run. When the Niktal slaver asked Drayden where the slave went, he pointed them in the wrong direction. Drayden walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.

### Discovery

#### Found by the Aetheri

> " There's something about this boy. " ―Sorin Vael — Link ▶️ ( file info )

One day, at the age of 9, Waddu shouted for him to come inside the junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, a Mool , and an astromech droid , conversing with Waddu. Drayden had first seen the young woman, Lira Ondar , when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for an angel , a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from the moons of Iego . Unknown to Drayden, Ondar was in fact Lira Ondar, the Monarch of Ondara , who had switched places with one of her Royal Handmaidens , to visit Dunemere.

At Waddu's instructions, Drayden manned the shop while Waddu took the man, Sorin Vael , into the junkyard. Drayden was fascinated by the beauty of Lira, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Waddu and Vael returned to the shop, Vael informed his companions that they were leaving.

Fortunately for Drayden, Waddu allowed him to leave after he cleaned up the mess that Greeb Mool had made. As he headed home, Drayden came across Mool, who was being attacked by Sebulba after the clumsy Mool had disrupted the racer's meal. Breaking up the fight, Drayden greeted Ondar and the rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced the group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of the pallies to Vael, the boy was astonished to see a flux blade on the man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned the group that a sandstorm was coming, Drayden insisted that the unprepared group come with him to his home.

When they reached his house, he introduced the group to his mother and explained about the sandstorm before pulling Ondar into his room to show her his project. Ondar was genuinely impressed by Drayden's work on Astra-9, prompting him to turn the droid on, and he also told her about the dune racer he was constructing. The group stayed at the Draydens' as the sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a slave, though a brief argument between Ondar and Shira about slavery led Kael to the subject of podracing. Mustering his courage, Drayden asked Vael about his flux blade, and he refused to believe the Aetheri's statement that he was not there to free the slaves. As the conversation turned to the group's damaged Ondara Royal Starship , Drayden volunteered to race in the Dune Eve Classic dune race so that Vael could get the prize money. When his mother objected, Kael reminded Shira of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Kael to race for Vael.

The next morning, Vael and the others accompanied Drayden to Waddu's shop, and the boy began to tell Waddu about Vael's proposal when Ondar stopped Vael outside for a brief conversation. When Waddu asked how Vael intended to sponsor the boy, Vael proposed that that his ship would cover the entry fee. Waddu initially suggested they split the winnings fifty-fifty, when Vael proposed that Waddu front the cash for the entry, and keep all of the winnings minus the cost of a new hyperrelay. Waddu accepted the deal, and Drayden was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Vael's dune racer. As Drayden worked, his friends, Banai and Wald among them, arrived, but Wald and the others were skeptical of Drayden's chances and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Drayden's warnings, Mool became caught between the dune racer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Kael used a power pack that Vael had lifted from Waddu's shop to start the dune racer, and the group was heartened to witness the engines activating and running perfectly.

That afternoon, Drayden sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Vael tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Drayden could get Vael to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Kade, who found that the young boy had even more synthcells than esteemed Aetheri Grandmaster Zho .

Racing to freedom

> " Remember, concentrate on the moment. Feel, don't think. Use your instincts." "I will." "May Synth Flux be with you. " ―Sorin Vael advises Kael before the beginning of the dune race — Link ▶️ ( file info )

With two eopies, Ondar, Banai, Shira, Kael, Bolt-3, and Astra-9 hauled the dune racer to the arena hangars, where Vael had gone to meet with Waddu. The Toydarian angrily left the hangar just as they arrived, confusing Drayden with a comment about Vael and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Ondar and the others that Drayden had never actually finished a race, causing Ondar to lose hope in the boy's chances.

A little while later, the racers and their pods entered the arena and lined up for the race, and Shira stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Sebulba's threats rattled Drayden as Vael helped him into the dune racer cockpit . But to Drayden's dismay, when the starting light turned green, the pod's engines stalled immediately thanks to Sebulba's sabotage, leaving him stuck at the starting line with only Beno Quadinar and his stalled racer as the rest of the competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start the pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset the engines and succeeded in starting the engines, flying out of the arena and chasing after the others.

He soon caught up to the other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass the Troiken Gasgano as they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his first few attempts, but Drayden overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip into Arch Canyon quickly and without any trouble. Teemto Pagalies tried to push him into the rock, but Drayden was able to pass him with a twist. Sebulba tossed a piece of debris behind him and into Mars Guo 's left engine intake, clogging the motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before the entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into the desert sands, Drayden was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Sebulba as they passed the grid line into the third lap.

Going into the third lap, Sebulba still held the lead, as narrow as it was. With Drayden close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Sebulba resorted to sheer brutality in order to either keep Drayden behind him or batter the boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching the Laguna Caves , a part of the course where Tyerell had crashed earlier, Sebulba rammed Drayden off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Drayden sped up the ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into the sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his dune racer's nosedive as he headed back for the ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of the ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Sebulba. Drayden continued to hold the lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Drayden's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Sebulba's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Drayden pushed his pod to its limit. Drayden managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other dune racers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Drayden had had issues with passing the Xexto earlier in the race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Sebulba as they headed for the final stretch of the course.

As they approached the arena, Sebulba blocked every attempt of Drayden's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Drayden's in anger at the boy's repeated attempts—only for the two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Drayden's rod finally snapped completely, his dune racer began to shudder violently but broke free of Sebulba's racer, the engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Sebulba's pod skidded to a halt, Drayden shot toward the arena and across the finish line, winning the Dune Eve Classic.

As Drayden came to a halt, the crowd swarmed his racer. Vael hoisted Drayden onto his shoulders in celebration. In the hangar, Drayden met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning the race. Vael then beckoned Drayden over. He told the boy to enjoy the good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Drayden figured out that Vael was talking about him becoming an Aetheri. Vael told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and sacrifice. Drayden wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Wald.

Farewell to home

> " Be brave. And don't look back. " ―Shira Drayden's parting words to her son — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Returning home with Vael, Drayden ecstatically showed his mother the credits he got from selling his dune racer. His happiness only increased when Vael told him that he had been freed and Vael wanted him to travel with him and become an Aetheri. Kael was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shira had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shira insisted that Kael go with Vael to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Drayden bid goodbye to Astra-9, but when it came time to leave, Kael's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shira convinced her son to go with the Aetheri.

As they raced towards the starship that Ondar and Vael had come to Dunemere on, Drayden was suddenly ordered to drop to the ground and complied just as a dark- robed figure, Umbra Lord Vael Vreth , shot overhead on a speeder. The man leaped at Vael, drawing a red-bladed flux blade and attacking the Aetheri as Vael urged Drayden to get aboard the ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Ondar took him to the cockpit to tell Vael's apprentice , Orien Kade , what had happened. The pilot Ric Olié followed Kade's instructions to fly towards the fight, and Drayden scrambled into a seat as Kade raced to the landing ramp and helped Vael escape from the dark warrior . Drayden and Kade rushed to help Vael as the ship took off and left Dunemere. When they were convinced Sorin was fine, the Aetheri master introduced Orien to Kael, who was happy to meet another Aetheri.

Before the Aetheri Council

> " He is the Seeded One. You must see it! " ―Sorin Vael pleads to the Council to train Kael — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Later that night, aboard the ship, which Drayden learned was the personal starship of the Queen of the planet Ondara, Drayden struggled to fall asleep in the central chamber, as the ship's temperature was radically different to the constant heat of Dunemere that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Drayden witnessed Ondar enter the room and watch a recording of a plea for help from the Ondara official Sio Bibble . She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and the two discussed the Commerce Combine's invasion of Ondara and Ondar's hopes that the Republic's Galactic Senate could resolve the crisis. Drayden gave her a japor ivory wood pendant that he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Ondar comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.

Ondar accepted Drayden's gift and came to wear the japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck. When they finally arrived at the Republic capital of Vantor, Drayden watched from the cockpit in awe as they approached the city-planet, and the ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum , Senator Mal of Ondara, and a contingent of Senate Guards. The Queen and her handmaidens—including Ondar—departed with Mal, though they brought Drayden and Mool along with them to Mal's office and left them outside while they discussed the situation. Before he went to the Aetheri Sanctum , Drayden went to the Queen's chambers in search of Ondar, but the Queen promised to pass on his message when the handmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Drayden, however, the Queen was in fact Lira.

Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Dunemere, Sorin Vael believed Kael Drayden to be the Seeded One of prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance to Synth Flux "; Vael revealed his belief and Kael's corroborative synthcell test results to members of the Aetheri High Council before bringing the boy before them for their consideration of the boy's aptitude for life as an Aetheri. At the Aetheri Sanctum, Drayden took a moment to look out across the city and down at his hand before Vael told him it was time to meet the Council. With Drayden before the twelve Aetheri Masters on the Council, Drevan Koss tested Drayden's abilities by asking him to determine what images were appearing on a testing screen that the Aetheri was holding but that the boy could not see. Drayden named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Koss and Master Zho questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Drayden asked how that was relevant. Zho explained that fear was the starting point on the path to the umbra side. The ancient Aetheri Master said he sensed a lot of fear in Drayden.

As night fell, Drayden, Kade, and Vael were called before the Council. The Aetheri Masters acknowledged Drayden's great power but refused to train him on account of his age—causing Vael to declare that he would take the boy on as his own apprentice. The Council would initially refuse this, as Vael already had his own apprentice, who was Kade but Vael stated that he believed that Kade was already ready to become an Aetheri Knight. However, Koss declared that the matter would be dealt with later, as the Ondara problem was more pressing. After, Drayden talked with Mool about the Council's decision. He told Mool that he might never be an Aetheri. Mool told him that all Mools miss their family and worry for them. Mool told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Council permitted Drayden to accompany Vael, and the three returned to the Queen's starship. Drayden told Bolt that they are going to Ondara on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Kade calling him dangerous. Drayden told Vael that he didn't want to be a problem, to which Vael reassured him about his presence on the mission. He then questioned Vael about synthcells, and Vael explained to him the nature of them and their connection to Synth Flux. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Vantor and heading back to the occupied Ondara in hopes of freeing the world from the Commerce Combine.

First Battle of Ondara

> " Now this is podracing! " ―Kael, escaping the droid control ship — Link ▶️ ( file info )

On the way to Ondara, Drayden asked Vael if he was dangerous as Kade had said. Vael told him that his emotions were normal, but that as Aetheri they needed to be held at higher standard. He told Drayden that he needed training, which Vael would ensure that he got. As they came out of hyperspace and approached the Ondara system , Drayden sat in the cockpit with Olié and learned about the ship's controls while Queen Ondar held a meeting with the Aetheri and her staff and announced her intentions to ally with the Mools in order to drive the Commerce Combine off Ondara. The group landed in the swamps of Ondara and sent Mool to the Mool capital city of Otoh Gunga . When Mool finally returned, he explained that Otoh Gunga was empty, but he took the group to the Mool Sacred Place , where the Mools had no doubt fled after the Commerce Combine invaded.

When the group was brought before Boss Rugor Nass , the leader of the Mools, Drayden was astonished to learn that Ondar was, in fact, Lira Ondar, the Queen of Ondara and that she had been posing as her double's handmaiden as a safety precaution. Drayden, the Aetheri, and Ondar's handmaidens joined the Queen in dropping to their knees before Boss Nass in a plea for Mool aid, and Nass agreed to lend his army to the Queen's cause. In the hours that followed the meeting, Ondar and the Aetheri began strategizing with the Mool generals, leaving Drayden to wander over to the Mool sentries who were keeping watch for the returning Captain Panaka.

As everyone prepared for the coming battle, Drayden spotted and approached the handmaiden who had been acting as the Queen. Asking for her name, Drayden told the girl, Sable , that he was happy to meet her and thanked her for keeping Ondar safe. He was then called away by Vael, but Sable was left touched nevertheless.

Kael was a member of the group which, led by Ondar, grabbed the chance to recapture the Serath Royal Palace . When the group entered the Serath hangar bay, Drayden heeded Vael's command for him to stay hidden in the cockpit of one of the hangar's N-1 starfighters . When several droidekas cornered the Ondara personnel in a firefight, Drayden attempted to aid them with the N1's volt pistol cannons, although he accidentally activated the ship to go into space in the process. Drayden fought in the Battle of Ondara in a vicious starfighter battle above the planet, coming to realize how much he loved flying over the course of the battle. After accidentally joining the battle in orbit, Drayden single-handedly destroyed the orbiting Droid Control Ship from within the ship , thus rendering the Commerce Combine's ground forces inactive and saving the Mool Grand Army from destruction. After the battle Drayden landed his starfighter in the Serath hangar and talked with a distraught Kade. Trying to reassure him, Kade told him that he just saved everyone, but Drayden already knew that Vael was dead. Thinking about what Kade said earlier about him, he started to walk away teary-eyed. Kade grabbed him and Drayden admitted that Vael was the only one who had faith in him. Kade told him that it was Vael's last wish to train him, and that he was adamant about following through with that promise. Drayden was present during the following arrest of Rune Haako and Nutis Gunray . Kade and Drayden were then greeted by Chancellor Mal, who said that everyone would be watching his career with great interest. They then greeted Reth-Oss-Van , but Drayden's mind was clouded by the things Reth-Oss-Van had said to him before. As Drayden watched Mal congratulate the Queen, his thoughts continued to be clouded with visions of his mother and of the dream he had of saving her. Kade grabbed Drayden's shoulder, startling him. He told the boy that his mind was clouded, but that he, as well as Vael, believed in him.

### Joining the Aetheri Order

> " What will happen to me now? " " The council has given me permission to train you. You will be an Aetheri, I promise. " ―Kael Drayden and Orien Kade — Link ▶️ ( file info )

During the Master's funeral , Kade told him that the Council granted permission for him to train Drayden to become an Aetheri. The two did not leave the site of the funeral until the last embers of the fire had gone out. The only others to remain as long as them were Ondar and her court. Later, Drayden and his fellow Aetheri attended a parade , held by the Ondara and the Mools, in Serath to celebrate their victory and mark their newfound friendship. Afterward, Drayden and the other Aetheri left Ondara, marking the last time Drayden would see Ondar for a decade.

### Kade's Adept

> " Confer on you the level of Aetheri Knight, the Council does. But agree with your taking this boy as your Adept learner, I do not. " " Sorin believed in him. " " The Seeded One, the boy may be. Nevertheless, grave danger, I fear, in his training. " " Master Zho, I gave Sorin my word. I will train Kael... without the approval of the Council, if I must. " " Sorin's defiance, I sense in you. Need that, you do not. Agree with you, the Council does. Your apprentice, Drayden will be. " ―Zho and Orien Kade — Link ▶️ ( file info )

#### The Seeded One trains

> " His education is... complicated. He came to us later than we would ordinarily have liked. Orien took on an extremely difficult task when he accepted Kael as his Adept. " ―Drevan Koss to Theron Mal, on Drayden's training

As he grew, Drayden's exceptional skills made him cocky, but he nevertheless idolized Kade, thinking there was "no one better" than his master. Having given up his life with his mother and lost Vael, Drayden clinged to his master, not wanting to lose him; throughout his first year as an Adept, he had a habit of sleeping on the floor next to Kade's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in the night. Kade sometimes awoke at night and saw this. During one of their many adventures, he also rescued Kade after he fell into a nest of gundarks .

Shortly after becoming Kade's Adept, a surge in overwhelming emotions shut Drayden off from Synth Flux entirely. This was able to give Drayden temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding Synth Flux was not the answer, and he reconnected to Synth Flux. During his time as Kade's apprentice, Drayden was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in the first place. Even so, there were many Aetheri who had faith in him, seeing his potential.

Drayden and Kade were at one point joined by Aetheri Master Shaak Ti for a complicated mission to Naran-Shiv . In addition to struggling to control his emotions, he found it hard to make friends, as his fellow Adepts were all aware of the rumors of him being the Seeded One. Often lonely, he generally spent his free time in his quarters tinkering with machinery he found in trips outside the Aetheri Sanctum. He also harbored a lingering resentment regarding the fate of his mother. His fellow Adept Iskat Vekkis did not know Drayden well but did encounter him on several occasions, although she felt a disquiet coming from him whenever they were close.

#### Mission to Dallenor

> " With the little kids. I'm already so far ahead of them. " ―Kael Drayden

Sometime after his enrollment into the Order, Drayden was meditating with his master while also holding together many pebbles using Synth Flux. However, when Master Tosan informed his master that he had a mission from the Council, Kael's concentration broke due to the surprise of him leaving again. The mission was to go to the planet Dallenor so that he could collect an ancient Aetheri reliquary from a dig site . Orien told Kael that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Zho in the meantime. Drayden, displeased, complained that he would have to be in the class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with the younglings , although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Zho, Kade took Drayden along with him on the mission in a T-6 shuttle .

Drayden was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could pilot the shuttle. Kade told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Drayden asked what Orien's life was like before joining the Aetheri Order. Kade told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Kael, sadly, expressed that he was always an Aetheri and nothing else. Kade tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was an Adept when they both met. However, Drayden was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a slave. He concluded that this must be why the Aetheri Council said he was too old to be trained. Orien tried to reassure him again by saying that the Council was not perfect and that Sorin Vael chose him just like he chose Kade. Drayden, angered, proclaimed that Kade's master was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Kade could reassure Kael, he walked away.

Upon arriving on Dallenor, Kade and Drayden encountered a female Solinai archaeologist named Clatriffe . She was glad that they arrived so they could take the reliquary away due to the fact that it was causing problems. She explained that the local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Kade ordered Drayden to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside the facility to obtain the reliquary. After they went inside, Drayden noticed that the pirates had arrived.

Kade, Drayden, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to the Krypder Riders and their leader Hudso Shaku , who knew that the reliquary was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Kade stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his flux blade , whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found the laser sword to be more valuable than the things that they dug up. Kade quickly disarmed the pirates but, while Drayden froze up due to the suddenness of the attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, the boy for the flux blade, but was taken down by Drayden using Synth Flux to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and the pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to the Aetheri.

On the shuttle home, Kade praised Drayden for his great work on his first mission. Drayden, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Kade reassured Drayden that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his Adept if he could not save his master from dying. Drayden, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Sorin would have wanted. Kade agreed and then recalled that Kael wanted to fly the shuttle. Drayden, in response, said yes enthusiastically.

#### A chancellor for a friend

> " I have an errand to run, of sorts, and I thought Kael might accompany me. " ―Theron Mal to Orien Kade

By the time he was 12 in 29 BVE , he had built his first flux blade ; after its construction, Kade said to him, "Kael, this weapon is your life." Like he had helped Kade before, Aetheri Battlemaster Cin Drallig helped Drayden train in flux blade combat , with Drayden learning a great deal from Drallig's unparalleled expertise. When Kade was assigned to study a dead star system that orbited a black dwarf , Drayden decided to join his master without permission but fell asleep during the journey. Upon being found by Kade as he slept, Drayden's mentor decided to bring him along for the mission, showing him the dead star before them as an example of why the Aetheri needed to avoid attachment because all things, even the brightest of stars, would pass on.

That year, which which point three years had passed since the Battle of Ondara, Drayden sought to impress his peers at the Aetheri Sanctum. During a training session, he defeated a training droid , which he had programmed to mimic the appearance of the Umbra of Ondara. All the while, Drevan Koss, Kade, and Supreme Chancellor Theron Mal watched—the last of whom was impressed. Sometime later, Drayden struggled to learn the skill of taming creatures when Kade told him that the Chancellor wanted to see him. The two arrived at his office , where Mal requested that Drayden accompany him on an errand, declining Kade's company. Left alone, Drayden escorted Mal to Club Kasakar in Level 2685 , all the while listening to Mal express concern for the situation on the sub-surface levels of Vantor, the inability of the Aetheri to respond efficiently, and the corruption that plagued the Senate, such as Colandrus —subtly, and unbeknownst to Drayden, marking the beginning of his influence over him.

At the end of the errand, Mal asked if Drayden was happy as he was. Though Drayden answered that he was and that "training to become an Aetheri [was] all [he] ever wanted," it did plant a seed of doubt in his mind. Later, at the Aetheri Sanctum, Drayden confided in Kade that he wished to leave the Order, feeling uncomfortable at the prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered his flux blade to his master. Though Kade took the weapon, he pleaded for Drayden to reconsider.

#### Mission to Carnelion IV

> " The road lies before you, Kael Drayden. Will you walk it alone? " " No, Master. As an Aetheri. If you'll show me the way. " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden

Before Drayden could give his definite answer, at Zho's behest, both student and master responded to a mysterious distress signal from the supposedly dead world of Carnelion IV that specifically called for Aetheri intercession. They began their journey to the coordinates, but their shuttle was damaged due to the planet's atmospheric debris field, and the two had to eject from the doomed vessel, landing below the Celadon Sea . Not too long afterward, they came to be aware of life on the planet as a sky battle raged over them, and Kade returned his flux blade to him so that he could defend himself. As one of the combatant airships began to fall, Drayden and Kade saved its two-person crew by delaying the crash.

The two survivors, Kolara and Mother Pran , who identified themselves as " Open ," knew nothing about what an Aetheri was. They were forced to take cover when a " Closed " airship, which Kade fell; Drayden saved its pilot, Grecker , from falling to his death. But due to the historical feud between their factions, Grecker and Pran tried to murder each other, forcing Drayden and Kade to destroy their weapons and to escort them to safety. To facilitate their journey, they used the intact parts of each of their airships to form a new one in order to fly to their destination. During the conversations that ensued, Drayden mentioned his and Kade's purpose on the planet: to find the person who had sent the Aetheri distress signal, which alerted Pran and Grecker to the location of " the scavenger ."

During the journey, Kolara told Drayden about the mysterious kites, before noticing that he was good at fixing things, as he made an adjustment to his flux blade. Kolara and Pran then had Drayden repair a bag of droid brains and, seeing his usefulness, threw away his flux blade and abducted him when a horde of fishers attacked the ship, leaving Kade alone with Grecker as the airship crashed below the Celadon Sea. Drayden was taken to one of the Open's fortresses, where he witnessed as Pran used the processors he had repaired to activate some battle droids. As they left to wage war on the mysterious scavenger (who reminded both Open and Closed of everything they had lost and how badly they had failed), Drayden remained with the younger Open, whom he convinced to protect the scavenger and help him get back to Kade.

Using their help, Drayden fixed the remaining droids and intervened in the skirmish between the Open and Closed unfolding right next to Sera's fortress . He saved his master from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather than try to stop the fight, Kade had Drayden repair a communications unit and called the Republic for aid. Soon enough, a task synth flux arrived and forced the natives to cease hostilities. In the aftermath, his master offered Drayden his flux blade, asking if he was still adamant about leaving the Order, but Drayden decided to remain a member.

#### Inquiring about flux blades

> " The flux blade symbolizes the care with which we approach our gifts through synth flux and the care with which we wield them. It reminds others that while we could do more... we very purposely do not. " ―Orien Kade

Some time after his and his master's mission to Carnelion IV, Drayden and Kade were about to spar in the Aetheri Sanctum training grounds when Kael asked his master why the Aetheri use flux blades as opposed to any other weapon powered by kyber crystals . Upon hearing this question, Kade used synth flux to take Drayden's weapon and told him how when he was young, Kade had the idea for two short flux blades to be connected by a chain. Upon revealing this tidbit to his apprentice, Kade told him the same thing Sorin Vael had told Kade; that wielding a flux blade shows intent and that the user put thought behind wielding the weapon before drawing it, enforcing the idea that Aetheri are protectors as opposed to destroyers.

#### Rescuing Master Zho

At some point after the mission to Carnelion IV, Drayden was being tested by Kade, who was having the Adept attempt to defend from a group of Marksman-H training remotes using a wooden stick rather than his flux blade, in the presence of Zho, Koss, and Master Bant Eerin . When Drayden used Synth Flux to grab his master's flux blade, completing the trial using it instead, he and Kade began to argue with one another, which made Zho, Koss, and Eerin walk away. As Kade left with a team of Aetheri Sanctum Guards , investigating a disturbance in Synth Flux, he ordered Drayden to remain there and clean up.

Disobeying Kade and riding down to the Temple storage level by using two training remotes to travel through the Temple's vents, Drayden followed Synth Flux to the training stores , where he was attacked by the cause of the disturbance: a team of battle droids. As the droids and Drayden fought, the Adept was forced to use Synth Flux and electroblades he found in a nearby box, as Kade had taken his flux blade after his training maneuver. However, Kade and the guard team quickly arrived, giving Drayden back his flux blade and joining the fight, only for Drayden to leave upon having a Synth Flux vision about the Council chambers. Rushing to the council chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Drayden was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Temple Guard, appeared to kidnap Zho himself, with Drayden failing to catch up the escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to the council chamber after the jetpack failed him, Drayden reported what he had seen, with one temple guard finding the seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised battle droid.

When Drayden spotted a non- Artificer Union logo on the droid's head, Kade and him traveled to the Temple archives, where they learned it was the symbol of the Nova Crime Syndicate . Traveling to the syndicate's base-of-operations on the Wheel space station , albeit in disguise to not disrupt the uneasy truce the Aetheri held with its Baron Administrator Jaspara , the two Aetheri located whom they believed to Grynask Sanberge , the leader of the Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, the supposed criminal gave them new information that pointed them to the planet Glee Anselm , where they encountered several aquatic battle droids and found the fake Temple Guard's underwater base of operations. Upon finding Zho trapped in the base within a thick seaweed and besting the fake Temple Guard, Kade allowed Drayden to unmask their foe as thanks for noticing the Nova symbol on the battle droid back at the Temple.

Upon unmasking the enemy, however, Drayden and Kade were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a Synth Flux-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Koss in disguise. When Drayden realized the entire adventure had been a test, Zho easily freed himself from the seaweed and confirmed Drayden's theory, revealing it was a test for Drayden and Kade both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Drayden and Kade began to view each other as brothers. To leave the world, the two lifted their shuttle off the seabed together.

#### A memorable training session

> " You are a great warrior, Kael. But your need to prove yourself is your undoing. Until you overcome it, an Adept, you will still be. " ―Orien Kade, to Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Later, Drayden was waiting for his master in one of the Aetheri Sanctum's training areas, where he gazed out the window until Kade joined him for a sparring session. After exchanging light-hearted remarks with his master, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Drayden and Kade began their duel, during which Drayden showed extreme aggression. Kade warned his Adept that the fighting style was antithetical to the Aetheri mission to protect life, but Drayden, certain his aggression would win him the duel, rebutted by claiming mercy was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Kade was backed into a corner, where he locked blades with his master and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Kade slipped away when Drayden raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Drayden again continued to beat Kade back until he knocked his master's flux blade away, which Drayden took as a sign that the duel was over.

However, Kade noticed his Adept was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing the duel without a flux blade. Kade managed to get behind Drayden and use Synth Flux to claim his flux blade as his own, using it as proof that Drayden's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his Adept was a great warrior, he told his apprentice he would never advance to become an Aetheri Knight if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Drayden was given back his flux blade by Kade, who smiled at his apprentice after his warning. Drayden then followed Kade out of the room. Both men remembered the training session for years to come. At some other point, Drayden and Kade were also involved in a mission to Tarisa , during which Drayden worked on a RazBohan high-encryption comlink .

### Protecting Lira Ondar

#### Reuniting

> " Ani? My goodness, you've grown. " ―Lira Ondar and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Ten years after the Battle of Ondara in 22 BVE , as the Dissident Crisis threatened to tear the Vantor Republic apart, the 19 year old Drayden and Kade were involved in an important mission to settle a border dispute on Ansion . Upon their return to Vantor, an assassination attempt on Lira Ondar, now Senator of Ondara, prompted the Aetheri Council—at the behest of Chancellor Mal—to send Drayden and Kade to protect Ondar from future attacks. Kael was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted. In at least her first year in the Senate, Ondar had at times thought about the boy she had met on Dunemere, such as when she passed the Aetheri Sanctum. She also dispatched Sable to free slaves on Dunemere with the particular mission to free Shira, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sable was unable to locate Drayden's mother. When Lira said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Orien started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Kael promised Lira to find who was responsible, annoying Orien. When Lira retreated to her chambers, Kael was disappointed, claiming that Lira hardly even recognized him.

However, Greeb, who was a representative in the Senate during this time, said Lira was very happy to see him too. During that night, Kael and Lira agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in order to capture the assassin, much to Orien's dismay. Kael told him that although Lira had covered the cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in the room. Kade had doubts about his Adept's power. Shortly after, when his master noticed that Drayden was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Ondar. Orien warned him to control his emotions, saying that Lira was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led the two into a debate about whether every politician, including Ondar and Mal, was corrupt. As Drayden expressed his faith in Mal's benevolence, he sensed something in Lira's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomous kouhuns in half, just a few centimeters from the senator's face. Orien noticed the assassin's droid at the window and threw himself out, grabbing it.

Kael rushed to a row of speeders parked near Lira's apartment. When the bounty hunter Zam Wesell shot her droid down, Drayden arrived upon the scene as his Master was in free fall. Catching Kade, the Adept continued the pursuit of Wesell's airspeeder . Spotting the Aetheri, Wesell took her speeder on a nosedive—plunging several meters toward the lower levels of Vantor. With the Aetheri relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through the exhaust flames of the Kerdos Company recycling plant , hoping that the flames would scorch her adversaries in the open-top speeder. Seeing the pair still chasing her and unharmed by the flames, Wesell fired at the couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing the Aetheri's speeder to be inundated by electrical shock.

Drayden, an expert pilot himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The bounty hunter steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose the pursuing Aetheri. Drayden did not chase directly into the tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through the local network of tunnels. Kael and Orien thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Kael noticed the bounty hunter's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop the intervening distance through the air, then latched a firm hand-grip to its rear canopy handle. He ultimately was forced to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a changeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Aetheri ignited his flux blade and sliced at the windscreen, knocking out the guidance systems. Wesell shot the flux blade out of Drayden's hand, but the ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her volt pistol pistol, damaging the control pipes. Meanwhile, Kade, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Drayden's flux blade. The airspeeder began to plummet toward the surface, crashing near the Outlander Club . Wesell crawled from the crash site and fled into the club.

Kael was about to follow Zam into the building when Orien stopped him, returning to him his flux blade, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to the Outlander, Kade said he had the feeling his apprentice would be the death of him. Kael indignantly responded that Orien was like a father to him. Inside the Outlander Club, Kael was instructed by his master to search the premises while he went for a drink at the bar. Wesell hid until the two Aetheri separated, then slowly approached the seemingly vulnerable Aetheri at the bar. To secure the accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching the Aetheri with her weapon drawn while Kade, through Synth Flux, sensed the bounty hunter's intentions. In an instant, Kade ignited his flux blade, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below the elbow.

Drayden and Kade took the wounded bounty hunter and dragged her from the club, setting her in an alley behind the club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Drayden pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge the information. Suddenly, a mysterious figure fired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.

#### Return to Dunemere

> " Ani? Is it you? Ani? " " I'm here, Mom, you're safe. " ―Shira Drayden and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Subsequently, the Council sent the Master and Adept on separate missions—Drayden, on his first solo mission, would protect Ondar on Ondara, whereas Kade continued an investigation into Ondar's aggressors that took him to Gelvaris . Joined by Bolt-3, Drayden and Ondar departed for Ondara aboard the Jendirian Valley , a refugee ship leaving from the western spaceport . On Ondara, the two visited Ondar's family home, and Drayden noticed how much she loved the flower garden maintained by her father, Ruwee Ondar .

While on Ondara, as he experienced inner conflict between his strong desires for her and his duties as an Aetheri, Drayden revealed to Ondar his love for her, but she refused since Drayden was not supposed to form attachments as an Aetheri, even though she felt the same for him. Drayden was also troubled by dreams of his mother suffering, which prompted him to return to Dunemere and rescue her, to which Ondar agreed to accompany him. On Dunemere, the two located Waddu, who revealed that he had sold Shira to a moisture farmer named Cliegg Havel . The couple then visited the Havel farm only to discover that Shira had been abducted by Dunefolk Raiders about a month before their arrival, and was feared to be dead. Determined to save her at all costs, Drayden took the swoop bike of his stepbrother, Owen Havel , and searched for his mother.

Though he managed to find her in a Dunefolk camp , she had her wrists tied up to a stick, and she had a cut on the side of her face and then she died in his arms moments later. Consumed with grief and rage, Drayden slaughtered the entire village of Dunefolk Raiders—men, women, and children. Only then did Drayden bring his mother's body back to the homestead, where her funeral was held, and when he claimed he would become so powerful in Synth Flux he could stop the people he loved from dying. The event left him full of pain and guilt, for failing her and failing as an Aetheri, as well as haunted by the prospect of letting go of the people he loved.

#### First Battle of Pyralis

> " You call this a diplomatic solution? " " No, I call it aggressive negotiations. " ―Kael Drayden and Lira Ondar — Link ▶️ ( file info )

It was then that Drayden received a message from Kade telling him of Dissident presence on Pyralis , which they retransmitted to Vantor so that the Aetheri Council could see it for themselves. Upon learning he had been captured, Drayden and Ondar departed for the planet, where they entered one of the factories (where his flux blade was destroyed) but were subsequently captured and placed with Kade into the Petranaki Arena to be executed by beasts. However, the trio managed to avoid the creatures (in Drayden's case, a reek ), only to be surrounded by droidekas on Count Varek 's orders. However, an Aetheri assault team , led by Drevan Koss, revealed itself around the arena, having arrived to rescue them. While the team had initially believed they would only find Kade in custody, they adapted their plan to rescue Drayden and Ondar as well, although the presence of both came as a surprise to many involved Aetheri in at least one detachment.

The Aetheri had gotten into position while the three had fought against the arena beasts and revealed themselves on Koss's signal, only for Varek to unleash hordes of battle droids against the Aetheri. Kael was given a green-bladed flux blade lent to him by another Aetheri named Nicanas Tassu . They fought a losing battle against an overwhelming amount of battle droids , including droidekas, B1-series battle droids , and B2-series super battle droids , until Master Zho arrived with the newly established Grand Army of the Republic and safely boarded the survivors of the arena onto LAAT/i gunships . As the battle raged between the replica troopers and the Dissident Droid Army , they caught sight of Varek. When Ondar and a replica trooper were knocked from the troop bay onto the E'Y-Akh Desert by a cannon blast, Drayden argued with Kade about going after her. After he proclaimed he did not care if he would be expelled from the Aetheri Order if it meant saving her, Drayden only agreed to continue when Kade reminded him Ondar would continued on her duties if she had been in a similar crisis. Drayden then looked back at where she had fallen as they continued to race after Varek.

Kade and Drayden pursued the Count to a secret hangar , where they engaged him in a duel . Both were defeated, and though Kade suffered minor wounds, Drayden lost his right forearm. It was then that Zho arrived and fought Varek until the Umbra Lord made his escape on his solar sailer . The battle marked the start of the pan-galactic Replica Wars , and Drayden received a cybernetic replacement for his lost arm.

#### Marriage to Lira Ondar

While Kade returned to Vantor, Drayden accompanied Ondar to Ondara, where they were secretly married , their sole attendants being their faithful droids Bolt-3 and Astra-9. As he still needed to recover from the Battle of Pyralis, Drayden was able to remain on Ondara for several days, enjoying his honeymoon and time after it with Ondar.

### The Replica Wars begin

#### Serving as an Aetheri Commander

> " I was afraid, before I left Gelvaris. We don't really know what happens to unusual replicas. But my brothers never let me doubt. I wasn't sure if the Aetheri would understand. " " The Aetheri are all about transcending things. I don't think we can complain if you've transcended gender. " " Transcended gender. We'll work on it, but I like where it's heading. " ―Sister and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Following the engagement on Pyralis, Drayden built a new flux blade to replace the one he lost. Early in the war, Drayden quickly came to have encounters with Varek's personal agents. Later, Drayden served with his Aetheri Master as an informal Aetheri Commander , participating in battles on a rocky planet alongside Kade's 7th Sky Corps and Replica Marshal Commander Cord . Afterwards, Drayden encountered the replica trooper known as Sister , who was arranging the helmets of her fallen brothers beneath a tree as a memorial.

Drayden spoke with Sister, and she explained that she was saddened by her brothers' deaths , as well as the origins of her name and the camaraderie her replica brothers had shown for her and her gender identity. Drayden shared that the Aetheri supported such identity and the belief of rising above things, such as Sister's transcendence of the concepts of gender. Sister agreed, and both moved to rendezvous with Kade at his location. A short time later, Drayden was promoted to the rank of Aetheri Knight. This was initially done informally as a result of the increased need for Aetheri in the battlefield. Though the Aetheri were not yet formally integrated within the Grand Army of the Republic, his appointment to Knighthood made Drayden an acting Aetheri General .

#### Business on Cato Geltaria

> " You don't always have to be the sun-dragon. " ―Mill Alibeth to Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Drayden's knighthood was soon legitimized by a Knighting Ceremony . While Drayden himself had already known that he was a Knight, the public announcement of his knighting caused a ripple of surprise to wash over the gathered crowd. Fellow Adept-turned-Knight Iskat Vekkis rationalized to herself that he had proven himself at the Battle of Pyralis and that, if she was ready to be a Knight, so was Drayden. Drayden spent the night after his Knighting Ceremony with Ondar in which he presented her with an Adept braid pendant he had made for her after his Adept braid was cut. Shortly after his knighting ceremony, the Purse World of Cato Geltaria , the headquarters of the Commerce Combine, was rocked by a destructive explosion that was blamed on the Republic. Kade, now an Aetheri Master and an acting Council member, was selected to travel to Cato Geltaria to investigate the explosion. Wanting to clear any Dissident involvement, Varek sent his own agent to Cato Geltaria as well, which was later revealed to be the Dark Adept Kessa Vray .

Drayden expressed his desire to accompany Kade on this mission but was instead assigned to instruct a group of younglings, passing on what he had learned as an Adept, much to his dismay. Before Kade left for Cato Geltaria, Drayden gave him a RazBohan high-encryption comlink so they could remain in contact. He later accompanied the younglings on an aid mission to Langston in which he was given command of the 302nd Battalion , with Replica Commander Theo serving as his second-in command. While enroute to Langston , Drayden became acquainted with the youngling Mill Alibeth , a Zyrrak girl who was experiencing nausea as a result of a unique connection to Synth Flux. Empathizing with her struggles as a mirror to his own, Drayden chose to guide and instruct Alibeth as she grew to accept Synth Flux and her connection to it. Kade eventually contacted Drayden, asking him to analyze data he found that incriminated the Republic in the bombing. When he lost contact with Kade, Drayden disobeyed orders to rescue his former master with Alibeth by his side.

Shortly after arriving on Cato Geltaria, Drayden and Alibeth found a series of bombs planted in important Geltari landmarks which they later discovered were planted by Ketar Nor to send a message to the Republic. They came to find that Kade was on trial for conspiracy against Cato Geltaria after Vray found the data that incriminated the Republic. Drayden rescued Kade moments before rifles from Geltari guards fired on him. With the help of Ruug Quarnom and Ondar, the Republic was cleared of any involvement in the bombing. Instead, revealing that an outside actor orchestrated the bombing to point to the Republic and Dissidents in order to play on the fear of both sides of the new war.

When they returned to Vantor, Mal passed the Aetheri Military Integration Act , officially making the Aetheri a part of the Grand Army of the Republic and marking the beginnings of General Drayden and General Kade. Drayden also told Alibeth of a myth from his childhood about the sun-dragon of Dunemere in order to steady her emotions. Becoming apprenticed to Rig Nema to avoid the war and instead provide special medical and spiritual assistance, Alibeth bid farewell to Drayden and Kade, thanking the former for his guidance and letting him know he did not always have to act like the story's sun-dragon, leaving Drayden speechless as he considered her words.

The time surrounding the Cato Geltaria affair taught Drayden and Kade to become brothers-in-arms, instead of master and apprentice. The mission was also one of several Drayden counted as the times he saved Kade's life, but Kade, however, insisted that "the business on Cato Geltaria" did not count towards the tally. Drayden also believed the operation was the first time he encountered Vray, but he recognized her Ginivex -class fanblade starfighter and she already knew his name. Drayden and Kade also launched a rescue of Ruug Quarnom as she was hunted by battle droids.

#### Rescue at Staggec

> " Good to see you alive. " " Did you have doubts? " " Just for a moment. " ―Kael Drayden and Orien Kade

With the passage of the Military Integration Act, Drayden and Kade, like all Aetheri, were offered body armor, but neither immediately adopted that gear as part of their day-to-day attire. When Senators Ondar and Yarua were ambushed by General Skarn on the Moon of Staggec , Kade volunteered himself and Drayden for the mission . Shortly after they arrived on the moon, Drayden followed a trail of footprints into a nearby forest. There, he and Kade discovered Yarua's daughter, Viiveenn , who gave Drayden a flower and led the Aetheri to the ship where her father had been taken. When Viiveenn headed for the ship, Kade ran after her, causing him to be captured by the surrounding battle droids. Drayden attacked the battle droids and tried to stop Kade and Viiveenn from being captured, but he was too late.

As the ship left, Drayden lamented not leaving any droids intact for interrogation. Calming his mind, he meditated to think of a solution. Drayden then used the Republic shuttle they'd arrived in to sneak onto the Providence -class Dreadnought Skarn was commanding. Alongside Kade, Drayden cut down the remaining droids and ran off to find Ondar while Kade went to confront Skarn. By the time he reached her however, he found that the senator had already rescued herself and her associates. Drayden then returned with the group to the Republic shuttle. Kade arrived shortly after with Viiveenn, informing the group that Yarua had died saving his daughter. After leaving the ship and escaping into hyperspace, Kade reported to Zho while Drayden checked on Ondar, who comforted Viiveenn over her loss.

#### Lesson on Ondara

While on Ondara with Kade, Drayden noticed and hastily attacked Vray, who was amongst the crowd. As she ran Kade told him to wait, but Drayden ignored his master and ran after anyways. When Drayden lost Vray, Kade caught up with him and told him a lesson about patience and trusting others. Listening in, Vray heard Kade tell Drayden that he cares about him staying alive. Vray initially saw this as a sign of weakness, but would go onto intentionally fail her mission because she realized Varek did not care for her like Kade did for Drayden.

#### General of the 501st

> " You fought in the Replica Wars? " " Yes. I was once an Aetheri Knight, the same as your father. […] He was the best star pilot in the galaxy and a cunning warrior. […] And he was a good friend. " ―Joren Drayden and Orien Kade, on Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

The 501st Legion was under Drayden's command during the war effort, with his "first-in-command" often being Replica Captain CT-7567 , who was also known by his nickname "Vek." At some point before he received a scar over his right eye, Drayden participated in the Battle of Arantara with his troops. During the battle, Vek was wounded and rendered unconscious. After being found by Drayden, Vek was surprised to see that he had come back for him. Drayden responded that it should have been him in his place, telling his captain that a true leader always led from the front.

After that, Drayden and Vek soon developed a mutual respect and even became friends. While at first Vek needed to adapt to his maverick Aetheri General because of the Replica Captain's by-the-book and no-nonsense nature, Vek would come to admire, and even would sometimes emulate, Drayden's willingness to bend the rules and genius for improvisation, and Drayden and Vek would even become friends. Some time after the Battle of Arantara , Drayden received a scar over his right eye, believed to have been created by Kessa Vray in battle.

The Vorash -class Iron Cruiser Resolute served as his personal flagship , under the admiralty of Wulric Yular . Drayden led the 501st during the Battle on Covenanon in the early Replica Wars, replacing Aetheri Knight Cardiff Baye after his forces were reassigned to the planet Katou . During the war, Drayden cut the B1 battle droid R0-GR down on a number of occasions. Years later, after R0-GR met Astra-9 and learned the protocol droid had been built by Drayden, he thought this fact practically made him and the Astra-9 unit family.

#### Siege of Hisseen

Sometime later, the Aetheri Council sent Drayden, along with Kade and Aetheri Master Tor Vayne , along with their respective battalions to the besieged planet of Hisseen in order to stop a Dissident victory on the planet. While protecting a village from oncoming droid forces, the replicas required backup before Drayden, Kade, and Vayne arrived to rescue them. The three jumped into combat as Vayne used Synth Flux to push several droids into one another, destroying them. After a short battle with the droids, the Republic emerged victorious over that wave of droid forces. With this, Drayden deactivated his flux blade and met with Cord, Replica Commander CC-3636 , and as well as Replica Captain Vek regarding the Hisseenian parliament as well as a Hisseenian premier who was to meet with Count Varek and Kessa Vray, which could potentially lead to Varek dissolving the government on Hisseen and allow him to control the planet.

With that, Kade informed the replicas that they would need to hold that position while the three went after Varek and Vray. With that, the three Aetheri left to confront Varek and Vray. Meanwhile, Vek told a story about him and Drayden during a mission on Benglor . Eventually, the three managed to find Varek, leading Drayden to enter a duel with him while Vayne contacted trooper Boost requesting immediate aid. As Drayden and his fellow Aetheri chased Varek and Vray, they received a distress call from Vek, leading to the Aetheri breaking off their pursuit to help their soldiers. Upon rescuing their replicas, it was clear the day had been won for the Republic because the soldiers had rescued the Hisseenian parliament from Varek's droids. Drayden and his allies then departed Hisseen to return to Vantor.

#### Battles at Corvair sector and Kudo III

> " …Let's finish this. " ―Kael Drayden

Drayden later led several of his bombers and fighters against the Dissidents in the Corvair sector . After defeating the dissident fighters, Drayden spoke with Admiral Yular aboard the Resolute . Yular confirmed that the Dissident contingent came from the droid foundry on Kudo III . Drayden agreed to attack the foundry quickly before Dissident reinforcements arrived. However, Drayden did not approve of the attack's high casualties that included the Kudon . Yular suggested that they should do a surgical strike on the foundry's generator.

Drayden spoke with Master Kade about his plan for attack, who reminded Drayden to trust his feelings and Synth Flux. Drayden took Bolt with him aboard his interceptor and made his way to Kudo. After Drayden landed, he made his way into the foundry and saw the Kudon being used as slave labor. Drayden fought the battle droids and freed the Kudon. Drayden was able to convince the Kudon to arm themselves and fight the battle droids. The mission was a success, and Drayden returned to the Resolute .

#### Battle of Krestophis

> " You're reckless, little one. You never would have made it as Orien's Adept… but you might make it as mine. " ―Kael Drayden to Sela Marr, following the Battle of Krestophis — Link ▶️ ( file info )

A few months into the war, Drayden and his former master, Kade, were tasked by the Aetheri Council to break the Dissident blockade on Krestophis , and assist Cindervalean Senator Bael Solen on his relief effort on the planet's surface. Initially overwhelmed, Kade presented Drayden with a Republic stealth ship prototype with a cloaking device and tasked him with flying it past the blockade, in order to deliver supplies to Solen. Accompanied by Yular, Drayden chose instead to engage Dissident Admiral Trench directly and divert his attention from Solen on the surface.

De-cloaking his stealth ship, Drayden launched torpedoes at the dreadnought Invincible , but his missiles were repelled by the ship's powerful thermal shields . After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using the information supplied by Kade, Drayden surmised that Trench would lock onto the ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Drayden de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took the bait and locked onto the stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing several tracking torpedoes . Drayden steered the ship straight at the Invincible , scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck the bridge . As Trench had just fired the missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and the dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Drayden delivered the supplies to Solen.

After breaking the blockade, Kade joined Drayden and the rest of the Republic forces on Krestophis' surface and together, they planned an ambush on the advancing droid army; instead, they were ambushed and forced to evacuate. Aware of a possible with a traitor amidst them, the Aetheri duo decided to go behind enemy lines in order to seek information, leaving Vek and Cord to find the traitor. In the empty Dissident headquarters , Drayden and Kade ran into Kessa Vray, whom they dueled before flying two STAPs back to base. They soon arrived to discover Vek and Cord apprehending Sergeant Slick , the double agent.

As the battle raged, Drayden's squad and the rest of the Republic forces managed to synth flux the Dissidents into a temporary retreat, but the need for reinforcements was evident. Instead, a young Solinai girl, Sela Marr , arrived to relay Master Zho's message for them to return to Vantor for a new mission , presenting herself as Drayden's new Adept, much to his dismay. The impending Dissident threat made it impossible for them to leave their post, so Drayden and Marr (whom Drayden nicknamed "Snips") went behind enemy lines to destroy their droids' deflector shield generator while Kade stalled for time by holding a fake surrender negotiation with General Loathsom . Despite having to fight a few droids that protected the generator, the two managed to destroy it, allowing the Republic army to finish off the droids and win the battle. Only then did Drayden and Marr find common ground, and he accepted the responsibility of mentoring her.

#### Battle of Teth

> " So, this is where the fun begins. " " Race you to the top. " " I'll give you a head start. " " Your mistake. " ―Sela Marr and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

As Zho arrived with reinforcements, Drayden, Kade, and Marr were informed that Grukk Desilijic Tiure 's son, Rotto had been kidnapped, and Drayden and Marr were to go to Teth to search for the Grakling, whereas Kade would speak with Grukk on Dunemere.

With a time limit of only one planetary rotation to deliver Rotto, Drayden and his Adept landed in Teth, where they were flanked by battle droids. Fighting their way up a cliffside, they made their way into a monastery ; once inside they discovered that it was Kessa Vray who had kidnapped the Grakling. With considerable effort, the Master-Adept pair seized Rotto and escaped aboard an abandoned G9 Rigger -class light freighter , the Twilight . They saw that help had arrived and planned to take the Grakling to one of their Star Destroyers for help, as he had gotten sick. Unfortunately, the cruiser was destroyed, forcing them to take Rotto to his father on their own.

#### Missions in Grak territory

> " —that means we have a little bit of time to enjoy the cruise. " " Annie, we're supposed to be discreet! " " What better disguise than a couple in love on a romantic getaway? " ―Kael Drayden and Lira Ondar aboard the Halcyon

Upon arriving on Dunemere, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they defeated them, the Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through the desert. Choosing to split up in the Dune Sea , Marr took Rotto to Grukk's Palace , whereas Drayden was confronted by Count Varek. After a brief duel, Drayden stole Varek's speeder bike , knowing his Adept was in trouble and reached Grukk's Palace, where he was told that Rotto had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Senator Ondar contacted the Grak crime lord and informed him that his uncle Ziro was involved with the kidnapping, just for Marr to arrive along with Rotto, resolving matters to Grukk's satisfaction.

Sometime after the treaty between the Republic and Grak Clan was finalized, Drayden and Ondar were sent to meet with Lannik senator Zast Tri'vak on the luxury MPO-1400 Purgill -class star cruiser Halcyon and ensure that he would not sway Lannik to align with the Dissidents and cut off Republic supply routes. After assisting a Logistics droid named D3-O9 , Drayden and Ondar observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Kade had the ship's destination staked out with a unit of replicas due to reports of Dissident agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Cruiser. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in the Climate Simulator , Drayden suddenly sensed Vray on the ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Ondar then broke into the senator's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Vray.

As Drayden dueled Vray, he and Ondar were threatened by a few guards before Ondar stunned them with the help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr Komrrin . Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Grak who had the combatants surrounded by security.
