When the Grak threatened to end the Grak Clan's treaty with the Republic, Ondar explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of the Mid Reach trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged the Grak for mercy, Vray stabbed the senator in the back and used her flux blades to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade starfighter. Ondar held onto Shorr's hand as Drayden sealed the opening with Synth Flux. She then commended the boy for his bravery while Drayden watched as Vray flew away.

Following the Battle of Hypori , Kade, Cord, and the 212th Attack Battalion were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Roon at a battle on Abrion Major . As he went to meet with Yular, Kade contacted Drayden and the Aetheri wished each other luck before Kade left for his mission.

#### The Malignar

> " That's your plan? Just fly there, land, hope they don't spot us and walk in the door? " " Basically. " " Oh. Brilliant. Let's get going. " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

When news reached them of the destruction of Aetheri Master Tor Vayne's fleet in the Abregado system , Drayden and his Adept set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Kade's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Drayden himself and Marr used the Twilight to rescue Vayne and three other replicas, who told them of a new Dissident superweapon , the Subjugator -class heavy cruiser Malignar , which was commanded by Skarn.

After yet another attack from the Malignar , Drayden decided to lead the Shadow Squadron into an attack against the heavy cruiser, only to narrowly escape the Malignar ' s ion cannon , which left too many bombers destroyed or incapacitated. Forced to change the plan, Drayden and the bombers dropped their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and causing the Malignar to retreat from its attack on a Republic medical station . While making his escape, Skarn captured Senator Ondar, who entered the system in the belief she was sent to negotiate with the InterGalactic Banking Clan Supreme Executive, rather than a trap. Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Drayden and Kade boarded the Malignar to rescue her. While on the ship, Drayden sabotaged the ship's navigation system to cause the ship to crash into a moon should the hyperrelay be engaged, before escaping the ship alongside Ondar and Kade.

Following the battle wherein a group of replicas from Rishi Station prevented a surprise attack on Gelvaris, Drayden and Kade awarded medals to two of its survivors, newcomers Echo and Fivva , with Drayden welcoming them onto the 501st Legion.

At some point, Drayden and Kade crashed their Yarrow into a shield protecting a city near the Senatorial Palace as they investigated the activities of some rogue Nightsisters on Rodanor . When the shield was abruptly activated, the starfighter was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.

#### Bolt-3 lost

> " So let me get this straight, Kael. You risked the mission, all your men, even your Adept , to save a droid? " " Bolt-3 found the listening post, and he saved our lives. We couldn't just leave him there, Orien. " " Oh, Kael, one day … " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Following numerous Dissident victories from General Skarn, and the defeat of the Falleen battle group , Drayden attempted to stop the cyborg from taking his sector and planned an ambush for the Dissident forces as they were passing through Bothawui 's asteroid field . Placing AT-TEs on the nearby asteroids , he managed to destroy the Dissident frigates and forced Skarn to retreat in the Soulless One , resulting in a pursuit by Drayden. However, debris from the destroyed Dissident frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Vek at the cost of losing Bolt-3. Though he was supplied with a new astromech droid, R3-S6 , Drayden was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the new droid, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring the battlefield; unable to find Bolt-3, they boarded a nearby scavenger ship, the Vulture's Claw , only to leave empty-handed as Gha Nachkt delivered Bolt-3 to Skarn.

With all hope lost, Drayden attempted to continue life without the droid, scouting for a possible Dissident listening outpost, until Bolt-3 sent a message revealing his location at Skytop Station . Upon arrival, Drayden ordered Marr and Vek to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost droid. Though Drayden engaged several MagnaGuards, he found Bolt-3 and met up with Vek and Denal , who told him that Marr was gone to distract Skarn alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Kael already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Skarn' spy and set several vulture droids and super battle droids against them. As they resisted, Marr rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the droid general alone; Bolt-3 opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Drayden, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.

Following Viceroy Nutis Gunray 's capture in Rodanor by Ondar, Drayden separated from Marr, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Undula . Although the two failed in their task, Drayden reunited with Marr at the rendezvous point.

#### Captured on Florrine

> " You should be more patient, Master. After all, the Count is an elderly gentlemen, and doesn't move like he used to. " " I suppose you're right." " I would kill you both right now if I did not have to drag your bodies. " ―Drayden and Kade mock Varek as the three attempt to escape — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Together with Kade, Drayden spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Count Varek, where Drayden let himself be captured by Varek's Munificent -class star frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Kade to come and rescue him. The two Aetheri then gave chase to Varek, who escaped in his solar sailer , with a Sheathipede -class transport shuttle . After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Aetheri began to hunt for Varek, but the Umbra Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Varek escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Marr and a detachment of replica troopers rescued them.

Later, as Supreme Chancellor Mal and his representatives were contacted by Hondik Hosha to receive a reward for capturing Count Varek, Drayden and Kade were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Varek, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Aetheri were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Hosha, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Aetheri mocked Varek's imprisonment, and the Umbra Lord warned them not to underestimate the Weequay . Drayden and Kade were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Varek. Despite several attempts by the Aetheri and Umbra to escape on their own, Representative Mool was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Senator Kharrus ' life and Varek's escape. Orien told Kael not to harm Hondik and left peacefully.

Later, Drayden heard about the situation on Ridlay , where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Koss, who was already there to investigate the situation . He offered his and Marr's assistance, but Koss denied their involvement.

#### Quell and Maridun

> " This battle is inevitable. You can stand by your beliefs, but let us stand by ours. " ―Kael Drayden, to Tee Watt Kaa — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Sent in as reinforcements for Aetheri Master Ciel Venn 's and Commander Bly 's forces , which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Battle of Quell , Drayden, Marr, Vek, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Venn's cruiser as it fell under heavy Dissident fire. Meeting with Venn, a raging inferno ran through the cruiser, and Drayden sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Marr and Venn dragged Drayden onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute , an ambush by several vulture droids caused the ship's hyperrelay to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star , they crash-landed on Maridun , where Drayden was left in the care of Vek, while Venn, Marr, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Vek defended Drayden from several vicious mastiff phalones until Marr arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too , before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.

Soon after their arrival, Dissident forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Aetheri of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Aetheri returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Dissidents' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator , on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of battle droids. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Drayden disabled the weapon before capturing Dissident General Lok Durd . The Aetheri then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Republic cruisers sent by Yular.

#### Orto Plutonia

> " …and this is the planet's tropical zone. " " It's not Dunemere, that's for sure. " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden, on Orto Plutonia — Link ▶️ ( file info )

After losing contact with a Republic outpost on Orto Plutonia , Drayden, his droids Astra-9 and Bolt-3, and Kade were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under the command of Captain Vek, in the company of Pantora Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chivva . Drayden and Kade investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its replica troopers , and the two Aetheri deduced that all the replicas had been killed, but not by battle droids since another nearby Dissident base had also been attacked. Drayden and Kade followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz , who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by Astra-9. Acting as a mediator, Kade proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Aetheri's efforts to bring forth peace. As a battle broke out amongst the replica troopers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The battle came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.

#### Blue Shadow Virus

> " You must move cautiously if you— " " There's no time for caution. My Adept is trapped on there, and so is Lira—I mean, Senator Ondar. " ―Gregar Typho and Kael Drayden, much to Kade's disapproving look — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Upon Ondar's petition to send Drayden and Kade to assist them in locating suspected Dissident presence on Ondara, Drayden, Kade, and Marr arrived to find that Ondar and Mool had gone to investigate the eastern swamps . Sending the young Adept with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Aetheri were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Ondar, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the galaxy. While Marr led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Kade and Drayden to enter through the hatches undetected, Drayden confronted Vindi, who electrified Ondar and Mool to make his escape. After saving the Senator, Drayden pursued the scientist , capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Kade on the swamp above.

Prepared to transport Vindi to Serath for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Drayden contacted Marr; she told him that Vindi's servant droid had set off one of the viral bombs , liberating the virus, and that she along with the replicas were trapped in the laboratory, just like Ondar was. Once in Serath, Captain Typho told the two Aetheri that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego . Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide , Drayden set off alongside Kade aboard the Twilight .

Upon arrival, Drayden and Kade were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series battle droids that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood ; he told the Aetheri that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol , who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web , the work of the Dissidents. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture droids, and Kade managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Drayden and Kade then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Ondara.

#### Battle of Selvara

> " It was a trap, Snips. It was not your fault. " " I lost so many of my pilots. " " Take heart, little one. That's the reality of command. " ―Kael Drayden and Sela Marr — Link ▶️ ( file info )

During the Battle of Selvara , Drayden gave Marr command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Kade's ground assault, encouraging Marr to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the battle, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which Drayden reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute , yet understood her good intentions. Drayden then reported his losses to Koss and Kade. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Marr because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Drayden had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender , which he would pilot by himself into the Selvara system to distract the Dissidents, while he gave Marr full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Drayden had already escaped in an escape pod and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Marr destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Drayden was rescued from his escape pod.

After Kade and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land, whereas Koss headed for Lessu with his forces, Drayden and his forces secured the space around Selvara , forcing the remaining Dissident cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Mal, Zho, Admiral Yular, Senator Orn Free Taa , and Koss. However, Dissident General Wat Tambra soon deployed Hyena -class Droid Bombers to destroy Selvara's villages and Drayden engaged them in his interceptor , accompanied by Marr and replicas. Before Lessu was destroyed on Count Varek's orders, Drayden and Marr shot them down. Subsequently, Tambra was captured, and the Republic was victorious: Selvara was finally free once more.

#### Reliquary heist

> " This could be a trap, Master. Are you sure you don't need us to go? " " Of course it's a trap, Drayden. " ―Kael Drayden and Drevan Koss — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Drayden and Replica Commander " Wolffe " fought alongside each other during the Battle of Khorm , where the Replica Officer lost his right eye to Vray. Sometime later, during the First Battle of Feldora , Drayden, Marr, and Kade fought against the Droid Army, but, while Marr was away on a jungle patrol, Drayden, Kade, and their replicas were surrounded. However, a Republic fleet commanded by Wolffe was able to break past the Dissident blockade , so Drayden and the others could be evacuated by Master Tor Vayne. However, Marr refused to retreat, and Kade and Drayden went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by droids. While Drayden and Marr argued about the retreat, as Marr believed the droids were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Aetheri watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Aetheri Sanctum, Drayden attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Council relegated her to guard duty in the Aetheri Archives to reflect on what she had done.

When Zho sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Drayden, and Kade inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo , and realized only too late that Cad Vorek had stolen a reliquary from the Vault . Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Vorek, Cato Parast revealed that Vorek's next target was Bolla Ropal , keeper of the Kyber memory crystal , which contained a list of all known Synth Flux-sensitive children in the Republic. Drayden then volunteered himself and Marr to meet with Ropal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Dissident fleet that was assisting Vorek, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the reliquary.

Drayden, Marr, and Vek boarded Vorek's Munificent -class frigate and stormed its bridge, where Bolt-3 accessed the ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Aetheri spotted Vorek and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Marr, the bounty hunter took her as a hostage, and Drayden was forced to bow to Vorek's demands and open the reliquary. Though he did so, Drayden also pulled his and Marr's flux blades to himself and attacked Vorek. However, Vorek distracted him by opening the airlock on Marr's cell and escaped, while Drayden saved his Adept and made their way to the hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Dissident ship back to the Resolute , not before letting Vorek enter, disguised in trooper armor . After landing on the Resolute , Vek later noticed Kessith blood in the shuttle, and pursued Vorek, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter . Drayden and Marr returned to the Aetheri Council to report their failure.

Drayden, along with Kade, Koss, and Zho, meditated in hope of finding the children that Vorek was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Drayden and Marr were assigned to Ondara to set a trap for Vorek in Jan-gwa city , capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page . With the real child safe, the two Aetheri took Vorek to the Resolute , where Drayden, Koss, and Kade interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick ; exhausted, Vorek gave them coordinates that Kade and Koss were to follow , while Drayden reported the details to Mal on Vantor. Soon, however, he reunited with his Adept and Bolt-3 and began to examine Vorek's Starfighter, from which Drayden and Marr deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Vorek had been to Emberfall . With nothing else to do, the Aetheri pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the droids set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Mal's orders, Drayden and Marr saved the children and returned to Vantor. Drayden admitted to the Council that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Zho advised caution on the path ahead.

#### Battle of Feldora

> " That's it. We'll just train the villagers to defend themselves. " " But we are just simple farmers. Why are we paying bounty hunters to defend us if we're just going to end up doing it ourselves? Hush. " " Trust me. We'll have you ready. " ―Kael Drayden and Dilanni — Link ▶️ ( file info )

On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Feldora Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Feldora , their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture droids deployed from an automated deployment station , forcing them to crash-land on Feldora. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sulie — Embo , Rumi Paramita , and Seripas . The village elder, Casiss Midagatis , explained to the Aetheri trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Drayden, Kade, and Marr decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.

That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondik Onaka's gang, familiar to Drayden and Kade. Hosha then told the group that he would be back, but by then Drayden had taught the Felucians how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Hosha Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Drayden dueled Hosha and his electrostaff , leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Aetheri Knight pulled him up and Hosha realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship . Alongside the bounty hunters, the Aetheri themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest Republic outpost.

#### The Zyll Beast

> " Well, you made the right choice. We got the fuel and you saved your creature. And now, he will be safe. " " Let's hope we could say the same about Vantor. " ―Kael Drayden and Drevan Koss — Link ▶️ ( file info )

During the lengthy campaign on Malastare , Drayden discussed with Mal, who contacted them via hologram, and Koss the effects of the new electro-proton bomb , just as the Dissident Droid Army prepared a new attack—with the Republic expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel . Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Koss went to investigate whereas Drayden was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus , only to be called by Koss to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to flux blades, the Zyll Beast , from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200 Falchion -class assault tanks , the Republic forces put the creature to sleep , convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Malastare now a member of the Republic, Mal ordered that the beast be shipped to Vantor for study, despite Koss's objections.

After the Zyll Beast was brought to the Republic capital, Kade and Ondar asked Drayden to speak with Mal about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Ondar met with the Chancellor, Mal and the Vice Chair Mazan Veldon tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Ondar objected to their claims, but Drayden chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Ondar, or his friend, Mal. Although Mal claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zyll beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Drayden and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle , only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Drayden sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Drayden saving Ondar from falling from the Senate Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Mal's orders.

### No end in sight

#### Another mission to Cato Geltaria

> " Wait. Now give me back my disk. " " Don't worry, Korvex. It's good to know you have a heart. I wish I could give you the disk, but I have a lot of faith in your survival skills. " ―Rush Korvex and Kael Drayden

Even as the Replica Wars continued with no end in sight, Drayden was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Drayden was recalled to the Temple by the Aetheri Council. Believing Senator Rush Korvex to be a Dissident supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Ondar, of whom Korvex was an old friend. The next day, Drayden met Ondar in the Senate and asked her about Korvex, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Korvex could be a Dissident. Drayden accompanied her to the Aetheri Council, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Geltaria, Drayden dressed as the pilot of Lira's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Korvex's attempts to get closer to his wife.

During the mission , Drayden and Bolt-3 waited for Ondar to give them a signal; when she did, Drayden sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Ondar and Korvex embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Drayden, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Korvex and gave it to Drayden, after which the Aetheri retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Ondar had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Korvex she was a spy, he helped Drayden take her back to their ship, back to Vantor, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Drayden to leave Korvex behind, stranded on the planet.

#### Second Battle of Pyralis

> " I knew they were still alive. I told you not to give up on them. " " It's not that I gave up, Drayden, but unlike you, when the time comes, I am prepared to let my student go. Can you say the same? " ―Kael Drayden and Luminara Undula

Immediately after routing Dissidents near Dorin , Drayden and Marr were sent to participate in the Second Battle of Pyralis , alongside Kade and Reth-Oss-Van, as the Republic returned to destroy the Pyralian primary droid factory . Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Drayden, Marr, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Dissident defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Kade's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Drayden's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Kade's position by foot. Though they encountered coalition, Drayden's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yular to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Yarrows to help them, they reunited with Kade and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.

They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Drayden and Marr disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades , enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Kade left for medical treatment, the Republic forces continued pushing towards the massive droid foundry, and Drayden and Marr were joined by Luminara Undula and her Adept, Barris Ovva , who met as Drayden and Marr angrily discussed Drayden's lack of trust in Marr's dependability. As Ovva and Marr were sent into the Pyralian catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Undula realized that Drayden was unable to let go of Marr and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Aetheri generals went onto distracting the droid army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.

As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Drayden and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Adepts had not returned yet. Cornered by droids coming from the factory, Drayden was contacted by Marr and she apologized, uncertain that she and Ovva would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Adept die, Drayden ordered to have them found; Undula, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Drayden was contacted by Marr; after locating them, they rescued the two Adepts.

Sending Marr and Ovva to recover out of the battlefield, Kade and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Dissident forces. For her part, Undula left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Drayden and Kade went to Undula's last known location, the Progate Temple . Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Pyralian warriors , they also encountered the captive Undula, Poggle, and the Pyralian Queen , Karina the Great . Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Drayden and Kade tried to negotiate with Karina, for Kade wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed Undula, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Vek and Cord burying the Queen in her lair.

With Poggle in Republic custody, the four Aetheri generals prepared to deliver him to Vantor, while they sent Marr and Ovva on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Drevan Koss on Steelgrove . Unbeknownst to them, one of the troopers, Scythe , was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta -class frigate , the TB-73 , forcing the two Adepts to fend off the infected replicas. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Drayden believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Marr contacted him to tell him about the situation.

Angered, Drayden went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Pyralian like him. It was then that Drayden resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Pyralian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Drayden gave in to his anger and began to Synth Flux choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Aetheri Knight, through a translator droid , that the worms were affected by the cold. Drayden relayed this information to his Adept, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Marr was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Drayden rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Marr on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.

#### Saluk

> " Where there is Kade, you will always find Drayden not far behind. " ―Skarn

Following the capture of Aetheri Master Etha Korrin in the Outer Reach, General Skarn sent a holo-transmission to the Aetheri Council, in which Skarn stated that he did not care about the Aetheri's politics , nor their Republic, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Kade decoded Korrin's hand signals that told them of their location at the Saluk system , Drayden, Kade, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yular and Kade engaged Skarn's forces over Saluk , with Kade allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Skarn. Discreetly, Drayden, Gallia, Vek, and a group of replica troopers would execute a hyperspace jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Skarn's cruiser.

Once they boarded it, Drayden and Gallia made their way to the stern of the cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical droid with a group of BX-series droid commandos . The two Aetheri destroyed all the droids and rescued Korrin, with Drayden taking the injured Korrin back to the shuttle. When Kade reported that Skarn had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Kade flank Skarn and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Drayden picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute , where he waited while Kade conducted a search mission for Skarn on the planet below alongside Cord, Vek, and other troopers.

Sometime later, Drayden and his Adept spearheaded a mission to Vantor's underworld to apprehend Car Affa , an arms dealer selling Republic weapons to the Dissidents. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Drayden instructed Marr to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Marr lost her flux blade, which prompted her to seek the help of Aetheri Master Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Drayden to find out she had lost her weapon.

#### Ferrum

> " What will you do? If you shoot me, you prove yourself a hypocrite to every pacifist ideal you hold dear. And you, Kade, you are no stranger to violence. You'd be hailed as a hero by everyone on this ship— Almost everyone. Come on, then. Who will strike first and brand themselves a cold-blooded killer? " ―Tal Merrik, to Orien Kade and Satinna Velara, before being killed by Drayden

Following allegations that Ferrum had allied with the Dissidents, and Kade was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satinna Velara , Drayden joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Vantor aboard the Coronet . On the ship, Drayden and Kade told their men to be on alert, when Velara requested their presence before her, and Drayden correctly surmised that Kade had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of their past together. When Bolt-3 picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Vek, and Drayden went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes . After destroying two of them, Drayden informed Kade that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Drayden returned to sweep the hold in search of more.

After Kade lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik , and Drayden destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Velara, now his hostage, until several B2 super battle droids sent by the Grave Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Kade to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed the droids and then went to find Kade, who like Velara, hesitated to kill Merrik; Drayden, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Vantor; as Velara complimented Kade, Drayden noted that she was quite the remarkable woman. Following the Ferrum Defense Resolution , Drayden and Kade escorted Ondar to a meeting with the Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Velara, before leaving Kade and Velara alone.

#### Trapped on Vanqor

> " That kid destroyed an entire cruiser trying to get to you, and you're just gonna let it go? " " Is there something else I should be doing, Drayden? " " How about tracking him down? " " So I should behave as this child does. I should seek revenge. " " No. How is it revenge if you stop this kid and bring him to justice? " ―Kael Drayden and Drevan Koss, on Vex Vark

Preparing to rendezvous with a Republic frigate, Drayden and Drevan Koss traveled aboard the Endurance , on which they greeted a squad of replica cadets , with an infiltrated Vex Vark among them. After Vark sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Boskan and Ayla Vossk on the Reaver One , Drayden and Koss rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod. As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance ' s crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with Bolt-3's misgivings, which prompted Koss to chastise Drayden for encouraging individuality in his astromech.

Leaving Bolt-3 and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Drayden and Koss entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Drayden noticing a Ferrik helmet . With Koss correctly surmising that it was bait set by Vex Vark, he saved Drayden from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Drayden and the unconscious Koss were soon found by Bolt-3, whom Drayden told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the astromech to come back, Koss told Drayden why Vark wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Garrick, during the first battle of Pyralis.

As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Marr, Tor Vayne, and a replica squad, thanks to Bolt-3 delivering the message at the Temple itself. After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Drayden urged Koss to track Vark down and bring him to justice. The Aetheri Master did not agree, until Vayne and Marr showed them a message from Vark and Vossk, with hostages—one of them, Ponds , killed in cold blood by Vossk. Though Koss decided to go, Vayne said that he would take the mission , alongside Marr, since both Koss and Drayden were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Vantor with Boskan and Vark in custody, Drayden was present as Vark apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Koss for killing his father, before they were taken away to the Republic Judiciary Central Detention Center .

#### Balith

> " I hope this assignment was not too boring for you. " " Ah, it had its moments. " " Huh, like the one where you convinced untrained children to help you overthrow a corrupt government? " " That was a highlight. " " It sounds pretty risky. You're lucky you didn't get hurt. " " Nothing you wouldn't have done. " ―Kael Drayden and Sela Marr

Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Ferrum, Drayden and Captain Vek escorted Marr to her solo mission on Ferrum to instruct cadets at the Royal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Velara and the Aetheri Council. Upon their arrival at Sundari , Drayden presented Marr to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec . Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto , Drayden took his Adept's flux blade to respect Ferrum's laws. After Marr exposed Almec's black market operation, Drayden reunited with his Adept, to whom he returned her flux blade.

Back in the Aetheri Sanctum, the Aetheri Council congratulated Marr on her courage and strength, but also ordered Drayden to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war . Told by the Council to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Marr pleaded to Drayden to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the battlefield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Vek.

Drayden returned to Vantor to greet, along with Zho, his Adept, Ondar, and Bael Solen after the Cindervale Refugee Conference , during which Marr captured Ayla Vossk. Wondering who would pay for Ondar's assassination, Zho had Marr use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Ondar realized who it was—Ziro the Grak. Drayden and his Adept then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Marr into confessing that he had hired Vossk for the job. Satisfied, Drayden and his Adept then left the Grak to his misery.

#### Battle of Gelvaris

> " I suppose you expect me to surrender. " " Actually, I plan to let the replicas execute you. Right now. " " Not this time. " ―Kessa Vray and Kael Drayden

Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission between Kessa Vray and General Skarn, Drayden and Kade traveled to Gelvaris to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lamir of an upcoming Dissident attack , with Vek, Cord, Echo, Fivva, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Skarn's fleet arrived and Drayden led the Republic space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident -class assault ship and AQ-series battle droids , prompting Kade to recall Drayden to the city. Soon after returning, Drayden engaged Vray, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the replicas' template, Garrick Vark. With the aid of replica trooper platoon , Drayden recovered the sample from Vray, but she, like Skarn, managed to escape yet again.

#### The blockade of Pantora

> " Should you really proceed without the Council's approval? " " We do it all the time. Don't we, Snips? " " Yup. " ―Ondar, Drayden and Marr discuss the kidnapping of Papanoida's daughters

Drayden was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped . Knowing that the Aetheri could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.

#### Senate crisis

> " Young Drayden… Not so impressive without your flux blade, are you, Aetheri? " ―Cad Vorek to an unconscious Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Supposed to be on a meditative retreat, Drayden spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Ondar anxiously arranged a party for Senator Aang , who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee . Drayden tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw Astra-9 being fuzzy about the arrangements. At Ondar's request, Drayden sent the protocol droid and Bolt-3 to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake . Although they were delayed, Ondar nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Drayden waiting for the two droids to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.

Not too long after, Drayden went to the Senate Building to convince Ondar to go away together for two weeks , to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and Aetheri. Ondar, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Senate. Drayden retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Ondar countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the Republic. However, Drayden stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Ondar his flux blade, which, Kade had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Drayden was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.

They were soon interrupted by Astra-9 and Senator Solen's voices, prompting Drayden to hide under her desk and Ondar to hide his flux blade in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill , only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Vorek and his team. Following her, Drayden saw the situation, when Vorek spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Drayden avoided them long enough to contact Mal and defeat an IG-86 sentinel droid empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Ayla Vossk and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Drayden woke up and Ondar returned his flux blade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Vorek triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.

#### Meeting Madam Synata

> " I'm still not convinced this trip is a good idea, Kael... " ―Lira Ondar

Following the crisis, Drayden went with Ondar to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship . However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Vael Krist-Ov . After they arrived, Drayden and Ondar met Synata. Drayden agreed to see her museum , despite Ondar's reluctance. Drayden took Ondar to her room. The next day, Drayden and Ondar went to the museum. There, Drayden and Ondar saw a display of Varykino , where they exchanged their vows. Drayden and Ondar came under attack by several robots from the displays. Drayden fought them off and helped Ondar escape them. Drayden and Ondar then discovered a Dissident banner and a hologram of Count Varek.

Drayden and Ondar discovered that Synata was collaborating with Varek and the Dissidents. Drayden then took one of the Dissident agents while Ondar discovered a list of anti-Dissident senators. Drayden and Ondar discovered that along with Ondar, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Drayden and Ondar fought several LEP servant droids to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from Bolt-3, Drayden and Ondar reached the theater.

As Ondar told the audience to run, Drayden borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own flux blade. Drayden fought Synata until he held her at the end of his flux blade. Drayden and Ondar then reported their trip to the Aetheri Council.

#### Intense training

> " Look, I know this is tough, but…[sigh]…I want it to be difficult. This is about life and death. And as your master, I'm responsible for you. The best way I can protect you is to teach you how to protect yourself. " ―Kael Drayden, to Sela Marr

After arriving late to one of Marr's training sessions, Drayden became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Drayden took Marr to an isolated area with Vek and the 501st. He then had the replicas surround Marr and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Marr told Drayden that the battle droids weren't nearly as good as the replicas. Drayden explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Marr would continue the sessions with Vek and the 501st throughout the war. Sometime later, Drayden was sparring with Marr, teaching her how to fight with two flux blades . She struggled to consciously utilize the other flux blade, but Drayden reassured her. When Master Zho entered to spar with her, Drayden watched as she struggled against Zho, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.

#### Unexpected Company

Sometime later, Drayden was assigned to a mission with Ondar to Cindervale . It took Drayden a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Marr ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Kade. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Drayden charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Marr warned him that the Dissident fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Drayden's ship exited hyperspace in the midst of the fleet. Drayden took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Ondar and Marr took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Ondar's side of the ship. Drayden jumped to protect Ondar, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Marr was able to take control of the ship and jumped into hyperspace. Upon their arrival on Cindervale, Drayden told her that he was glad she came. Ondar mentioned that her and Drayden made a good team, with Marr returning the compliment.

#### Trouble with Tomarian

The showman Tomarian later requested Drayden as an Aetheri escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala . Drayden thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Drayden was then surprised to see Senator Ondar dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Ondar told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Drayden, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Drayden ignited his blade. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Drayden, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.

As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs , a member of his crew , to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Drayden to escape unnoticed. He managed to catch up with Ondar, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a volt pistol from one of Telsla's goons . He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Ondar tried to contact "Checker" , who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Drayden stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Ondar snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her volt pistol against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Ondar confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Drayden fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Drayden complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Drayden glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Ondar wished him luck, and Drayden asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.

#### Aetheri Ceremony

One day, Drayden was waiting with Master Zho in the Aetheri Sanctum grounds for Marr. Once she arrived, Drayden reprimanded her for being late, but Zho praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Adept braid. Following a debate in the senate about a financial reform bill , Drayden was asked by Ondar to persuade the Aetheri Council to speak with Mal about denying the continuation of the war. Drayden allowed Marr to go with Ondar in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Dissident attack , Drayden confronted his apprentice about her and Ondar's meeting with Underra 's senator Mina Bontero on Raxun Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Drayden's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.

#### Gloomreachian threat

> " Warrior-like men, witches—this place has all kinds of fun. " ―Kael Drayden to Orien Kade, on Gloomreach

Drayden and Kade commanded Republic forces in a battle against Kessa Vray's fleet in the Garnethex system . Directly participating in the battle with their starfighters, Kade was singled out and attacked by Vray, whose Ginivex -class fanblade starfighter was, in turn, disabled by Drayden. As Kade and Vray crashed in the hangar of the Dissident command ship, Drayden soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Aetheri engage Vray in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Count Varek. The two Aetheri fled in their interceptors, presuming Vray to be doomed.

After the massacre on Devaron , Drayden and Kade were sent to Gloomreach , as footage had shown the Council of a Gloomreachian's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Gloomreach, they made their way to a village , where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Drayden took their leader hostage; the Zyrrak then told the Aetheri that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress , prompting the Aetheri to go there. Drayden and Kade questioned Mother Talzin , who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Aetheri then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko . Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Drayden and Kade caught up with their target on Varek's Dissident flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.

#### Killing a defector

> " Master, I didn't mean to-- " " It's all right, Kael. You did nothing wrong. " " Don't…hold it against me, boy. It merely seemed…the quickest way back…to the sunrise… " ―Kael Drayden, Orien Kade and Mekedrix

Following their encounter with Opress, Drayden and Kade were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yular. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at Republic and Dissident targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Roon, creating a possible link. Yular went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando . As such, Drayden and Kade were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Drayden initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Aetheri like the Dissidents. Kade assured Drayden that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Aetheri came upon empty sets of replica armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of an Umbra temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Aetheri to see their leader in exchange for their flux blades. Entering the outpost, Drayden and Kade came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a replica trooper, Kade asked him what the replica's name was. Kade went on to claim that life gave the galaxy meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Kade, but Drayden retrieved his flux blade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Drayden expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.

### Personal and wartime conflicts

#### The Destiny of the Seeded One

> " You are the Seeded One. You have brought balance to this world. Stay on this path and you will do it again… for the galaxy. But beware… your heart… " ―The Father's last words, to Kael Drayden

When the Aetheri Council received a 2,000-year-old Aetheri distress signal , Drayden, along with Kade and Marr, were sent by the High Council to the Chrelythiumn system , where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Vek and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Vek's nor the Aetheri's ship could see each other. In fact, the Aetheri's ship was guided to a mysterious planet, Mournis , where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter , who asked Drayden if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father , when Drayden was separated from Kade and Marr, and the Daughter.

Drayden tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery , where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shira, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as an Aetheri and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son , briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.

Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Seeded One. Though Drayden dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Aetheri Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny . Ordering his children to kill Marr and Kade, the Father goaded Drayden into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Drayden instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Seeded One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Drayden refused, leaving with Marr and Kade on their shuttle .

They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Marr, prompting Drayden to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Kade's reservations, Drayden went after his Adept and climbed the tower to find Marr, corrupted by the umbra side. Drayden fought her, soon to be joined by Kade, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Kade tried to hand Drayden the Dagger of Mournis , but Marr grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the umbra side user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Drayden begged the Father to save Marr, and the dying Daughter used Drayden as a conduit to channel the last of her life synth flux into Marr, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.

The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Drayden went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Drayden's offer of aid, telling the Aetheri Knight to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Drayden encountered Synth Flux spirit of his first mentor, Sorin Vael. Though Drayden sought his advice, Vael advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Umbra side to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Drayden to confront his future, one consumed by the umbra side. Horrified, Drayden submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this destiny . Soon, Kade arrived, and Drayden left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Aetheri who would stand in the way of peace.

Drayden arrived as his Adept disabled the ship and went to rescue Kade, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Drayden waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time ; he wiped Drayden's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Drayden awoke to find the Father, Kade, and Marr, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Drayden killed the Son with his flux blade. As the Father also died, he warned Drayden to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father, Drayden had brought balance to Synth Flux on Mournis through the deaths of Synth Flux wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full galaxy.

After the Father's death, the three Aetheri were transplanted back to the galaxy to finally rendezvous with Vek. Surprised to learn that, to Vek and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Iron Cruiser. They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Drayden even mentioning that he had spoken with Vael to Zho. Even though Drayden was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor. In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Vael.

#### The Citadel

> " I'm not sure what to think of your new ally. " " Well, I think we need people like him. This is a war. If we aren't willing to do what it takes to win, we risk losing everything we try to protect. " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden, on Wilhard Varrix

More than one year and nine months into the war, following the capture of Aetheri Council member Even Piele by the Dissidents, Drayden and Kade were briefed by Master Tor Vayne and sent along with their team—which included Vek, Cord, Echo, and Fivva—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piele, who held the information on the Nexus Route . Refusing to let Marr risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Drayden devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander Bolt-3 and his reprogrammed battle droid squadron.

After landing, Drayden was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Marr had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the astromech to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines , and after Marr deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger , who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck , of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot , but finally made it to Master Piele, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.

Despite the many traps laid through the fortress , the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Route coordinates, Captain Wilhard Varrix . The team was split into two—while Kade's team created a diversion, Drayden would lead the others away. In Varrix's company, Drayden and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Varrix expressed some skepticism about the Aetheri's plan, to which Drayden told Varrix that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude. However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Aetheri Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war, and Varrix developed a grudging appreciation for Drayden.

As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with Bolt-3 and the shuttle, they were attacked by droids and forced to join Kade at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Drayden and Kade contacted the Council, telling them they needed to escape, and Vayne immediately departed to pick them up. They contacted the Council again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Master Piele's life, who passed his intel on to Marr, and the group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Tor Vayne's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Vantor.

#### Adept lost

> " When I was out there, alone, all I had was your training and the lessons you taught me. And because of you, I did survive. And not only that, I was able to lead others to survive as well. " " I don't know what to say. " " I do. Thank you, Master. " " You're welcome, my Adept. " ―Sela Marr and Kael Drayden

Drayden, Marr, and 501st forces fought on Feldora alongside Tor Vayne and his replicas in taking out a reinforced Dissident outpost . Breaking their forces into three groups, Drayden and Vek's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical droid , TZ-33 , securing the outpost together with Vayne and Marr's teams.

However, Drayden then noticed that Marr had disappeared during the battle, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Drayden had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Master Vayne then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Outer Reach and assured Drayden that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Feldora was finished and that they had to return to Vantor. However, Drayden refused to abandon her, to which Vayne observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Drayden countered "I will not leave her fate up to others." Vayne insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Drayden comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.

Back at the Temple, Drayden relentlessly searched for possibilities in the star charts, which prompted Vayne to suggest that he should trust in Marr's abilities. Following the events on Wasskah , Drayden's search for his Adept came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo , in company of her Vorak allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx . Upon seeing her, Drayden profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Marr, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Master Zho.

#### Battle of Horain

> " Stand down! It's General Kade. And it looks like he's bringing a new friend. " ―Kael Drayden

Sometime later Drayden participated in the Battle of Horain , alongside Kade and Vek. Drayden was contacted by Vek, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Drayden later met Kade and Vek in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty battle droid Bats .

#### Skirmish on Retta

On Retta , Drayden ambushed a group of B1 battle droids alongside Grand Master Zho. Drayden was soon attacked by Kreeda , a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Aetheri away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Aetheri, as she was an old friend of Zho. When Drayden attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Drayden and Zho were attacked by battle droids soon after. After being separated with Zho, he met the Aetheri Master and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Replica Wars. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Drayden pretended to be using the " cloaking cap " to distract General Flebek , allowing Zho to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Zho told Drayden that he would tell Drevan Koss there was nothing of interest on Retta.

#### Battle of Mon Calvarra

> " Alright. I think I'm getting the hang of this. " [Drayden gets electrocuted] " Less talking helps. " ―Kael Drayden and Kit Fisto, restrained by Calavane eels

After the assassination of King Kolina of Mon Calvarra and new discord in the fragile peace between the Calavane and the Quarren , Drayden and Ondar, sent as representatives of the Republic, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Vorian of the Calavane Guard and protector of young Prince Liarch , but so did Riff Tamson , Dissident ambassador . Though Drayden and Ondar tried to mediate between the parties, the Quarren left the council hall, dissatisfied with the Calavane rule. Along with Vorian, they contacted Zho and Koss, who dispatched a company of replica troopers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Marr.

In the meantime, during the Quarren attack assisted by Dissident aqua droids, Drayden and Ondar accompanied Liarch, who in turn was bodyguarded by Vorian. He engaged the Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Senator Meena Tills , while leaving Marr to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Dissident/Quarren assault came to an end, Drayden reunited with Ondar, Marr, Fisto, Vorian, Liarch, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hydroid Medusas , which proved incredibly effective against the Republic-Calavane army. Forced to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.

They split into two groups: Drayden went with his wife, Vorian, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Marr, and two troopers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Republic reinforcements to come soon, Drayden and his group proceeded to the Calavane central planetary scanner facility, where the Aetheri Knight collapsed the entire structure with Synth Flux, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Republic reinforcements—the Mool Grand Army, and Representative Mool, sent at the behest of the High Council. However, Tamson used Trident -class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the Republic-Mool forces, and though Drayden managed to destroy one of them, all but Marr and Liarch were captured.

Brought before Riff Tamson, Drayden and Fisto were restrained by electric eels , whereas Ondar and Greeb Mool were imprisoned within containment devices , interrogating the two Aetheri about the whereabouts of Prince Liarch. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to synth flux them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Ondar's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water . However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Drayden and Fisto used Synth Flux to remove the water from her helmet, while Mool used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Liarch's execution, in which Nossor Ri , the Quarren leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Dissidents, resulting in the victory of the Mon Calvarra people. With the peace restored, Drayden and his friends were present for the coronation of Liarch , now recognized by both Calavane and Quarren as their new monarch.

#### Ondara

> " Lira, I'm sorry. " ―Kael Drayden, after being traded for Skarn

Following rumors that the Mools planned to aid the Dissidents in an attack on Serath, Drayden accompanied Ondar to Serath and later, to the Mool territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Mool, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie , and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace , which Drayden telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister , Rish Loo , had given it to him.

Drayden, Ondar, and Mool assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face droid commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Mool, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Mool assault on Serath. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Drayden pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Count Varek in Loo's laboratory . As Varek killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Battle of Ondara, Drayden dueled the Count, only to be overwhelmed by Varek's powers and his four MagnaGuards.

Varek then contacted Ondar and offered an exchange—the Aetheri Knight for General Skarn, who had been captured by the Mool Grand Army, thanks to Mool General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Drayden was brought and handed over to Ondar, Queen Neeyutnee and the Mools, while Skarn was allowed to remain free.

#### Battle of Umbara

> " Does my battalion have to do everything? " " You seem to always volunteer. " ―Kael Drayden and Orien Kade

During the campaign on Umbara , Drayden and Kade discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city , which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Vek and Fivva, Drayden prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Kade's joint attack with Aetheri Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell , who would be supporting Kade from the south whereas Drayden and 501st Legion would advance from the north.

Amidst heavy fire, Drayden and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Vek awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Kade's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Drayden told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Vek told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Vek observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Drayden joked that he reminded him of Vek, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.

Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Drayden and his troops, but an airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Drayden with a disconcerting message: the Council had ordered Drayden back to Vantor, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Mal, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of battle, Drayden was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Vantor. Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage the Republic effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th battalions into attacking each other, Kade was successful in taking the capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the planet for the Republic.

#### Kiros

> " Zygeran scum! I'll handle that slaver… " ―Kael Drayden, on Darts D'Nar

After ten rotations of Zho's last contact with the Solinai colonists on Kiros , Drayden, Marr, Vek, Cord, Kade, and their forces arrived at the world to investigate its silence, with Drayden particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town , they used BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower , used as the Dissident Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar , then sent a message to Kade to discuss terms of surrender. Drayden then took the lumen projector out of Cord's hand and called D'Nar " Zygeran scum." Though Drayden wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Kade told him to locate the missing colonists while he negotiated. As Drayden contacted Admiral Yular to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Kade explained Drayden's behavior to Marr.

Overhearing from Kade's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout the city, Drayden and Marr set out to disarm them. Despite sniper droids , they destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Kade to tell them D'Nar was escaping on the Tecora . With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Drayden managed to throw it off the ship, while Marr bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Drayden joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygeran into revealing the colonists' location, much to Marr's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Royal Slave Auction.

#### Zygera

> " A most curious Aetheri who cares more about his friends than his own mission. Hero. General. And now, my servant. " ―Miraj Scintel

When Drayden, Kade, Yular, and Marr told the Aetheri Council of their findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists. On the Tecora , and disguised as Zygeran, they land on the capital city of Zygera ; splitting into two teams (Kade and Vek went to located the colonists), Drayden and Marr went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel , with Drayden posing as "Havel Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri 's supposed death, and presenting Marr as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Drayden prove himself a slaver by torturing Kade.

Instead, he freed Kade and tried to escape with Bolt-3 returning their flux blades, Vek providing cover fire and Marr confronting the Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Drayden's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Varek and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Drayden brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with Synth Flux. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Drayden was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.

After a few days, Varek would arrive, and Drayden observed that she too was a slave to the Count, before she left to receive the Umbra Lord. With the help of Bolt-3, he escaped his guards and freed Marr from her cage and sent her to prep the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Kade and Vek's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec , who had betrayed Scintel, and Varek, with whom Drayden futilely dueled before Varek blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Drayden then took Scintel and escaped to the Tecora , aboard which she told him Kade was in the Kadavo system and that she, just like Drayden, was a slave, before dying.

After contacting Tor Vayne to send reinforcements, Drayden and Marr soon reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility . During the battle that ensued, Drayden disabled the turrets that were attacking Vayne and his forces, while Marr saved the Solinai colonists from falling to their death (as caused by Keeper Agruss ) by placing Admiral Bartono Coburn 's Arquitens -class light cruiser Hand of Justice underneath the facility. Along with Vek and Kade, Drayden evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Vayne's orders, before returning to the rest of Vayne's fleet.

#### Mal kidnapped

> " Drayden, a powerful Aetheri you are, yet unpredictable and dangerous you can be, to both your friends and enemies. For Orien, on your patience, everything depends. " ―Zho, to Kael Drayden

After the capture of Moralo Eval , rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Mal, Drayden, Marr, and Kade were recalled to an emergency meeting by the High Council while they walked through the nightly streets of Vantor. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted the three Aetheri to chase their aggressor. However, the sniper killed Kade before escaping on a speeder, prompting Drayden to return to his master, to find Marr crying and his old master dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond.
