Becoming withdrawn and grim, Drayden would not say a word since then, until after the funeral held to honor Kade, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer, Rako Hardeen , at Trueping's . Though Drayden would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the Republic Detention Center.

After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Drayden held a meeting with his friend, Mal, when Koss informed the Chancellor that they would remove the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Vorek; a decision did not sit well with Drayden. Mal suggested that the Council perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Drayden felt the Council was doing nothing to catch the murderer of his master—his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Mal told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Drayden and Marr to Grukh . There, two Aetheri harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia , the fugitives last known location.

After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Drayden rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Vorek and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Drayden confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Vorek interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Drayden, whispering to Drayden's ear "Kael, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Drayden passed out, only for Marr to protect him for Vorek's attempts to execute him. As they returned to Vantor, Zho summoned Drayden to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Kade was "Rako Hardeen."

Afterwards, Drayden was briefed along his Adept about the security plans the Council had for the Chancellor during the Festival of Light on Ondara. Drayden, Marr, and Koss would escort the Mal and Mazan Veldon to Serath, where Drayden was greeted by his wife. At the festival itself, the abduction was initially successful with Cad Vorek and Moralo Eval ferrying Mal away, while Drayden and Koss captured Embo and Twazzi ; however, Kade stopped Eval and Vorek, long enough for Koss and Drayden to arrive and take the two into custody.

As he reunited with his old master, he voiced his resentment for the Council's lack of trust in him as well as Kade's decision to hide the truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Council, he set out on his own to bodyguard Mal, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Drayden until they were greeted by Varek. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Drayden dueled the Count with ferocity, until Kade arrived to help him rescue the Chancellor, whereas Varek fled.

#### A phantom menace

> " I sense a disturbance. Something sinister. " " All the way out here? " " Yes. " " Do you know what…or who it is? " "[sigh] No. But whatever it is, it feels familiar. " ―Kael Drayden and Sela Marr

Sometime later, when Drayden and Marr were on their way to rendezvous with a cruiser, they decided to land at the Stobar Spaceport , as Drayden was hungry and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's , only to find police droids and an unconscious waitress . Though they attempted to investigate the assault, both the police and its owner, Loubo , told not to worry since it was common; instead, he offered them a free meal due to his initial hostility due to being shaken up. However, Drayden sensed a disturbance in Synth Flux , something sinister and familiar—which, unbeknownst to him, heralded the return of the now renegade Umbra Lord Vreth.

#### Underra Insurgency

> " I feel responsible for them. " " I know you do, Snips, but remember, purpose must come before feelings. " ―Sela Marr and Kael Drayden

Following a plea made by a insurgent cell on Underra, the Aetheri Council considered how to help them fight the Dissidents. Drayden suggested training the insurgents in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which the Aetheri would not support, the High Council decided to send advisors to the insurgent cell for the upcoming battle . On the Nura -class transport Valkyrie 2929 , Drayden, Marr, Vek, and Kade reached the planet during the night, when they met Steela Vess and were guided to their insurgent secret base, where they met her brother, Torrik . The team trained the insurgents to combat droids, until Dissident probe droids discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted the cell to enter Iziz .

After some successful operations, Drayden, Kade, and Vek decided to return to Vantor and report to the Council, while Marr stayed behind as a liaison to the insurgents, without revealing herself as an Aetheri. They kept in frequent communication with her, to provide guidance, but also to provide help by hiring Hondik Hosha to deliver missile launchers to the Underra insurgents for the final assault. Drayden attended the funeral service for Steela Vess.

#### Losing Satinna, saving Bolt

Drayden was later present when the Aetheri High Council met up with the chancellor to discuss Vreth's return, during which they decided to leave him alone even though they knew he was still alive. Drayden lent the Twilight to Kade, who used it to travel to Ferrum to rescue Satinna Velara. With all its technical problems, after landing on Ferrum, Kade vowed not to borrow another ship from Drayden. However, as he escaped, the ship was shot down by Ferrik commandos and destroyed, moments before Velara was murdered by Vreth during the Ferrik civil war . Drayden was waiting for Kade when the Aetheri Master returned to the Temple hangar, where Drayden saw through Kade's attempt to hide his sorrow. Kade explained he had lost someone important to him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Drayden knew all too well. Drayden offered to come with him for a report to the Council, which Kade happily agreed to.

Drayden was later present when the Aetheri Council sent Meebur Gascon and a team of Republic droids to steal an encryption module from the Dissidents. Later Drayden participated in the Republic strategy conference on the space station Valor , while on the station he noticed the Renown was going a little too fast. Varrix told Drayden that they are scanning Rhydonium on the ship, Drayden instantly realized it was a bomb, Drayden was later contacted and informed by Gascon of Bolt's sacrifice. However, Drayden refused to believe that his friend was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour the wreckage, who retrieved Bolt's remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Kael was happy to have him back.

### Nearing the end

#### Marr's trial

> " I know you believe in me, Kael, and I'm grateful for that. But this isn't about you. I can't stay here any longer, not now. " " The Aetheri Order is your life. You can't just throw it away like this. Sela, you are making a mistake. " " Maybe, but I have to sort this out on my own, without the Council and without you. " " I understand. More than you realize, I understand wanting to walk away from the Order. " " I know. " ―Sela Marr and Kael Drayden

During the defense of Cato Geltaria , Drayden and Marr engaged Dissident forces on their Eta-2 Actis -class interceptors , until they received an urgent mission recalling them to the Aetheri Sanctum. Its hangar had been bombed and, as the two had been off-world during the attack, Drayden and Marr were to lead the investigation to uncover the criminal—likely to be an Aetheri. As they entered the Temple's hangar, they discussed the possibilities an Aetheri was behind the attack. Marr doubted an Aetheri could bomb such a sacred location to the Aetheri, and in the process, kill their own colleagues and employees. However, Drayden told Marr to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Aetheri that did not agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Varek and Krell. As a result, everyone who was at the Temple during the bombing were potential suspects, including Aetheri.

As Drayden and Marr walked across the burning hangar, they met Russo-ISC , whom Drayden sent alongside Marr to interview the witnesses, whereas he meditated at the hangar. Unable to discover anything, Drayden met up with Marr and Russo, who told him about their missing suspect, Jackar Bowmani . With this clue, Drayden went to speak with Chief of Security Cin Drallig . As Drayden approached Drallig on the Aetheri Sanctum's steps, he witnessed a crowd of angry protestors, rallying against the Aetheri for their violence during the war. After Drayden showed Drallig the hologram of Bowmani on an imagecaster , Letta Turmond identified Bowmani in the hologram and informed the Aetheri she was Bowmani's wife . Later Drayden and Marr cornered Turmond near her home , and she under pressure admitted to feeding Bowmani nano-droids but would not reveal her motives.

Drayden was present at Zho's eulogy for the six Aetheri who died during the explosion , and later took the opportunity to speak with Varrix. Drayden and his Adept were then summoned to the war room to be briefed about a Dissident attack on Saluk, when Varrix informed them that Turmond had requested to speak with Marr. When she was framed with Turmond's death and imprisoned, he was forbidden to see her, and only returned to track her down on her escape , following Marr into the pipelines.

Alone, they revealed that their trust in each other was absolute, but as Drayden pleaded for her to surrender herself and make her case to the Council, she refused to take the blame for something she had not done and asked Drayden to trust her, just as she jumped onto a passing ship, thus escaping to the Vantor underworld.

As Varrix declared she was being accused of sedition, Zho assigned Drayden and Tor Vayne to search for Marr, with some coalition from Koss—who did not believe Drayden to be emotionally detached to do what had to be done. Nevertheless, when she was captured , Drayden escorted her to the Temple, where he was later summoned to the Chamber of Judgment along with his Adept. He was outraged to learn that the Council had already made their decision and that the meeting was simply a formality, and they proceeded to expel her from the Order. Resentful of the Council, he asked his wife, Ondar, to defend Marr at her trial before leaving to capture Vray himself.

Drayden soon found and interrogated Vray, who admitted she was about to turn in Marr to the authorities, until she realized Marr had been abandoned by Drayden and the Aetheri Order, just like she had been forsaken by Varek. She also said that whoever had attacked Marr had stolen her flux blades and that Marr had contacted Barris Ovva for help. Promising to kill Vray if she lied, Drayden left for the Temple, where he went to Ovva's quarters and questioned her, only to engage her in a duel . After subduing her, Drayden brought Ovva to the Republic military base where Marr was being judged. As the Mirialan Adept confessed, all charges against Marr were lifted.

Back at the Temple, Drayden and the Council apologized to Marr and invited her back into the Order, with Drayden offering her severed Adept braid . However, Marr apologized, leaving him in possession of her braid, and declined the offer. As she left the Temple, Drayden followed her and asked her to reconsider. Marr, however, declared that she felt she could not stay with the Order, due to their lack of trust in her. Despite Drayden's heartfelt protests and his mentioning that he himself had considered leaving the Order before, she chose to walk away. Marr's departure had a deep impact on Drayden, leaving him feeling more alone than ever in bearing the expectations of being the prophesied Seeded One. The circumstances surrounding her departure also served to intensify his frustration with the High Council, as he felt they had betrayed her by not backing her innocence. Without Marr as the counterbalance to his concerns and passions, Drayden's attentions also focused ever more upon Ondar.

#### Gelvaris conspiracy

> " He's in on it! I don't know to what extent. But I know he orchestrated much of this. He told me in the medical bay! " " He told you? When you tried to assassinate him? You have gone too far, Fivva. The Chancellor isn't capable of what you claim. " " He is! I swear to you, General. You've no idea— " ―Fivva and Kael Drayden, before being interrupted by the Vantor Guard

A short time after Marr left the Aetheri Order, Drayden, alongside Aetheri Generals Tiplar and Tiplee , and the 501st were sent to take control of Ringo Vindra 's planet-wide station back from Admiral Trench. Only after seven rotations of stalemated combat, did they manage to push Trench's forces to a breaking point. However, one of Drayden's troopers, Tupp , intentionally killed General Tiplar, forcing Drayden to order his and Tiplee's troops to fall back. He then had Tupp checked out to see if he had been brainwashed by the Dissidents, only to find nothing in him, even as he repeated nonstop "Good soldiers follow orders" as well as apparent amnesia . Though he did not find anything in him, Drayden had Tupp sent to Gelvaris for further examination.

After Tupp's shuttle was destroyed and he was taken prisoner by Trench, Drayden investigated the wreckage. Seeing that Tupp was not among the dead, Drayden decided to infiltrate the Dissident lines. Alongside Vek and Fivva, Drayden reached the Dissidents' hangar wearing an environment suit and saw that Commander Kraken had Tupp aboard his shuttle . Drayden and the others the boarded the shuttle and took out Kraken's crew, including Kraken himself, who refused to reveal why they had abducted Tupp. Drayden then had Tupp transported to Gelvaris with Fivva and Vek as escorts.

Following the end of Drayden and the 501st Legion's participation on Ringo Vindra, and the death of Tupp, Drayden and the High Council were informed by Shaak Ti that Fivva attempted to kill the Chancellor. Koss, aware that the Aetheri were not requested to participate in the manhunt, allowed Drayden and Vek to secretly search for the replica. They soon were contacted by Kix , who told them that Fivva wanted to meet the two at a warehouse in Level 1325 . As Kael and Vek entered the warehouse, they dropped their weapons on Fivva' request. After doing so, they were encircled by a ray shield, which prompted Drayden to question Fivva' actions, but he and Vek listened to the fugitive's side of the story. Drayden refused to believe that the Chancellor was involved in this conspiracy about the inhibitor chips , but their conversation was cut short by the arrival of the Vantor Guard . Drayden watched as they gunned Fivva down, dying in Vek's arms.

He would report the event to Zho, Koss, Ti, and the Chancellor, who was swift to lie to the Aetheri that all had been the product of a parasite from Ringo Vindra, one that had caused the decay of Fivva and Tupp's chips; Mal urged the Aetheri to leave the matter behind, for each day, they grew "closer and closer to victory." From that point onward, Drayden was faced with the fact that Tupp and Fivva, two of his best men, had died under mysterious circumstances, which became another weight on his emotional health.

#### Korvex returns

> " Kael, I understand to a degree what is going on. You've met Satinna. You know I once harbored feelings for her. It's not that we're not allowed to have these feelings. It's natural. " " Senator Ondar and I are simply friends. " " And friends you must remain. As an Aetheri, it is essential you make the right choice, Kael, for the Order. " ―Orien Kade and Kael Drayden

Upon his wife's mission to Scipio and subsequent reencounter with Rush Korvex, when she was accused of sabotage and her aide Teckla Minnau was killed, Drayden was sent to protect Ondar, but was upset to hear that Korvex was involved. Nevertheless, he agreed to help her, and together, they went to Korvex's residence to assist in exposing the Banking Clan's corruption. As they were about to depart along with Korvex, Drayden defended both Senators from the bounty hunter Embo and his pet anooba, Marrok . The trio were able to escape Embo, thanks to Bolt-3, and make their way to Vantor. Escorted by Zho, they met with Mal and other members of the Aetheri Council, and Senator Bael Solen, during which Drayden's animosity towards Korvex was perceived by Zho.

As Ondar was appointed by the Chancellor to work closely alongside Korvex to investigate the Banking Clan, Drayden questioned her decision, as he wanted her away from Korvex, whom he greatly distrusted. Though he demanded that she step away from her assignment, she refused and left with Korvex. Frustrated, Drayden went to his quarters, where Kade (at the behest of Zho) spoke to him, revealing that he was well aware of Drayden's feelings for Ondar, and asked his former apprentice not to let himself carried away by them before leaving. Later that night, Drayden went to his wife's apartment, where he found Korvex about to kiss her, for which Drayden briefly Synth Flux choked him—before giving him a one-sided beatdown despite Ondar's protests, willing to kill Korvex in his jealousy.

Before it was too late, he realized his mistake and stopped, only for Captain Typho to enter. Much to his surprise, Korvex lied about the attack. As they carried Korvex to Ondar's bedroom, Drayden tried to apologize to his wife, but she told him to stay away from her. Only when they were alone afterward, she questioned their marriage, too steeped in lies and deception, and decided that they take a break from one another after admitting to not feeling safe around Drayden—who, in turn, admitted to being unable to control himself, even though he did not know why.

Korvex was accepted as the new head of the Banking Clan by the Senate, and Drayden confided to the Chancellor of his distrust in Korvex, which Mal shared. Soon, when Korvex apparently joined the Dissidents the day after (which Drayden found strange), and Scipio was occupied by Dissidents, Drayden was then sent to retake Scipio. After landing, Drayden went to Korvex's office and tried to reason with Korvex to make him let go of Ondar. At that very moment, though, a vulture droid crashed into the office, making it unstable and causing Ondar and Korvex to fall, but Drayden grabbed them both, each with one hand. As he could not hold the two, Korvex told Drayden to let go of him, but he would not; Korvex apologized to Ondar and then let go of Drayden, falling to his death. Though Drayden and Ondar reconciled, the Banking Clan came under the control of the Chancellor.

#### Sifas Dyen' secret

> " The creation of the replica army, kept secret from us Sifas Dyen did. How this was done we know not, yet now a new piece of the puzzle we have. Perhaps clarity it will bring us, yes. " ―Zho

After Aetheri Master Tor Vayne intercepted a distress call from a lost shuttle and discovered Sifas Dyen 's flux blade, its late owner being responsible for the secret creation of the Replica Army. Relaunching the investigation of Sifas Dyen's death, Drayden and Kade were sent to Feldora, where they spoke to the tribal leaders to find out what happened to the late Aetheri Master. Kade was told that the Felucians had cremated his body and that another Aetheri had been with him when he was killed and reported their findings to Master Zho.

The two were then sent to Oba Diah when former Chancellor, Finis Valorum told Zho that Sifas Dyen had been sent to negotiate with the Gruv Syndicate to stop an underground war. Valorum, however, said that there was no other Aetheri sent alongside Syfo-Dyas, but rather his personal aide Silman . On Oba Diah, Kade and Drayden met VOX Gruv , who denied any involvement until Kade noticed that he was wearing the crest of Valorum and Drayden grabbed the necklace and told the Gruv to "stop playing games." VOX finally complied with the Aetheri, and told them that they had been paid by a man called Varek to shoot Sifas Dyen's shuttle down. However, as they feared that anyone who paid for the death of an Aetheri was dangerous, they kept Silman locked away for ten years as insurance.

Once they met Silman, they realized he had gone mad after being locked up for so long, and were unable to learn much from him before Varek arrived to kill him before Drayden and Kade's eyes. After engaging Varek in combat, they learned that the man called Varek was, in fact, the Count. Although Drayden pursued the count after he tried to escape on a Vantor Freighter , Varek escaped aboard his solar sailer , leaving the Aetheri without the chance to learn more about the replica army. Nevertheless having learned the identity of Varek, they informed the Council of their findings, only for them to question why Varek had been behind the creation of the replica army, and what the game of the yet-unidentified Dark Lord of the Umbra was playing.

#### Zho's journey

> " Help me to escape. Yes. " " Escape? But you're not in prison. " " No, perhaps, but perhaps I am. Already, all around us, the cage may be. A journey I must make alone. " " The Council will not want you to travel at all, alone even less. " " Disobeying the Council your expertise is. That is why I ask for your help. It is the spontaneity you find so easily which others do not. That is what sets you apart. Now, a walk you will take me on. Come, come. " ―Zho and Kael Drayden

Upon Zho's brief contact with Sorin Vael, Zho requested that Drayden meet him on the Temple's training grounds to speak about their experience on Mournis, where both he and Kade had spoken to Vael. As Drayden, too, was in disbelief of the idea that an individual could retain its identity after death, Zho left him to rest. Later, when Zho submitted himself for medical examination by Rig Nema, Drayden informed them that Chancellor Mal wanted to hold an emergency meeting with Zho, to which Koss responded, leaving the Grand Master free to undergo a deprivation ritual . Drayden, Mundi, Kade, Vayne, and Nema watched over the old master until Kade interrupted it before it was too late, and Zho claimed to have spoken with the dead.

With the Council worried for his health, Zho was placed under the surveillance of guards , so the Aetheri Master asked to Drayden to see him. Zho asked Drayden to help him escape, which Drayden did. Accompanying Zho to his fighter , Drayden even lent Bolt-3 to Zho to accompany him on a journey that would take him to Mirefall , a mysterious planet and Moraband , to learn that the Aetheri Order would not win the war, and that there was "another Drayden."

#### An Excuse to Fight

After a skirmish against Tofen's Raiders , in which Kade's starfighter malfunctioned, a Republic fleet was attacked by Dissidents. Drayden and Kade were present alongside the young vorak Viiveenn during the attack. They both argued about who should be the one to join the fight and who should be the one to deliver Viiveenn to safety. When a missile that entered the hangar bay exploded near the three, Drayden and Kade were separated, with Kade left with Viiveenn. Drayden used the wreckage that landed in his way as an excuse to join the fight. As another explosive entered the hangar, Kade reluctantly took the vorak into his starship and told her that Drayden did that sort of thing all the time.

#### Mission to Batuu

Alliance with a Chyrr "commander"

Drayden was told by Senator Ondar that one of her former handmaidens, Duja , had found something near Batuu that she and Drayden should investigate. Unfortunately, Drayden was unable to join her due to being sent to battle. Drayden allowed her to travel to Batuu by herself. Upon his return to Vantor after the battle, Drayden could find records of Ondar's travels in the Outer Reach. After another battle, Drayden received a message that Ondar's ship was found on Batuu. Drayden, along with Bolt-3 , took his interceptor to Batuu. En route to Batuu, Drayden encountered a freighter . Drayden spoke with the freighter's captain, Chyrr Defense Fleet Commander Mitth'raw'nuruodo , known simply as Vorren. Unknown to Drayden, Vorren was actually a Senior Captain . Drayden accepted Vorren's assistance in finding Ondar and Duja.

Drayden found Ondar's ship near Batuu's Black Spire Outpost . There, he stopped a group of smugglers who were angry about the ship being in their landing spot. Drayden fought them until Vorren broke up the fight. Together, they investigated Ondar's ship and learned about Duja's ship, Nomad Four. The two then went to a nearby cantina. After speaking with the bartender, Drayden and Vorren came under attack by four assailants. The two of them were able to kill the assailants. Vorren suspected the assailants were sent to kill Drayden and that there was the fifth nearby. Drayden and Vorren came up with a plan to capture the last assailant. After Drayden took one of the assailant's body, he pretended to suffer engine damage in his interceptor. After landing, Drayden came under attack by an assassin droid but was to destroy it. Vorren was able to capture the assailant and found his freighter, which belonged to the Dissidents.

Mission to Mokivj

Drayden and Vorren took out the droids guarding the freighter. Together, they found out the freighter was headed to Cermau . As they returned to Black Spire, they came under attack by several thugs. Vorren was able to use their speeder, to capture the thugs. Drayden and Vorren questioned the cargo inspector Oenti , who reveals that the cantina's bartender, Janott had ties to the Dissidents' Duke Solha . They learned that they were shipping supplies to a droid factory . After learning of Duja's death, Drayden told Vorren about the politics behind the Replica Wars. Drayden and Vorren traveled to Mokivj . There, they discovered that the Dissidents were mining Cortosis and discovered a factory.

Following Drayden's and Vorren's location of the factory, and later their change of clothing, Drayden contacted the duke, to infiltrate the factory and informed him that Captain Boroklif was indisposed, that there was trouble at Black Spire, and that the he and the others were losing. The Serennian was shocked when he heard this news and asked if the Dissident captain and his companions were dead, to which Drayden replied that he did not know as he was not there at the end of the battle.

The Aetheri Knight offered the Dissident to bring the cargo he had lost to his factory, Solha accepted and told him he'd open the shield when he was on the coordinates of the courtyard, that he was not stupid and he would not open it until it was truly necessary, just when he was in position to land. Following their arrival, the Aetheri Knight and the Chyrr commander were hailed by the Serennian duke escorted by a pair of B2-series super battle droids and two squads of B1-series battle droids, who inquired their possession of an astromech droid that belonged to the Republic, Bolt-3. In response, the Aetheri explained that he had found the droid and believed it could be worth something if he desired to purchase it.

The Chyrr commander and the Aetheri Knight asked the Serennian what happened to Ondar's former handmaiden, Duja. They also told him how they acquired Boroklif's ship, the Larkrer , that they came to Mokivj because there was a preset hyperspace course on the ship, the Serennian asked who had put in that course, to which they replied they assumed the Dissident captain did. The duke then warned the pair that they did not have anything else to bargain with and that he could have them shot. However, the pair told them there was another preset hyperspace course locked below the current one, which was locked by a two-stage encryption passcode, and that each of them had one half, Drayden then presented his Chyrr companion as "Brix." The Serennian ordered all of his droids to lower their weapons and to escort them while they entered the factory. The lights went off while the group was entering the building, and the Aetheri sliced a droid squad and threw his flux blade to the top of the building so the duke would not be aware that Drayden was in fact, an Aetheri. Then two B2s blocked the courtyard's opening to prevent any escape.

Solha knew that there was an Aetheri loose on the factory, however, he didn't know who Drayden was, he then sent Vorren and Drayden to some improvised prison cells. Calling the Aetheri a thief , the Serennian aimed at Drayden from the corridor in front of his cell with his volt pistol, and said that the Aetheri wanted to be here and that he got here thanks to Drayden, and for that alone he should be shot. He then revealed he was the Duke Solha of the Free System of Sireno, to which the Aetheri Knight replied if he should be impressed, Solha said he should be, and that he would be at some point. One B2-series super battle droid took all of their weapons and communication devices, even taking Vorren's small hold-out volt pistol located in his boot. Solha and the droids left with Bolt, who was dropping oil so Drayden knew where they had taken him. They left the room and locked the cell's doors.

The pair agreed on running a test, which would discover if Drayden's wife had been captured, if she was free or was not on the factory by waiting two hours as Ondar, and if she did not join them on the detention block, they would break out and try to locate her. Vorren asked the Aetheri that if he recognized the ship in the courtyard, telling him it was an ore carrier , which was full of ore , so this was not a staging ground but a manufacturing facility, he also told Drayden where was there more activity and where was there less activity, as Chyrr could see more infrared than humans, and he noticed the heat signatures, and that where they were in was the most active section.

Following the completion of Vorren's and Drayden's test, the Chyrr crafted a cord from the clothing he got from the Larkrer and unlocked their cells from the inside. Meanwhile, the senator had entered the building and was accompanied by a group of locals, consisting of maintenance workers of the factory, Cimy , Huga , and LebJau . They had entered the factory through the service level, which was usually unprotected and only them and the Dissidents knew of. Ondar had promised the group a fortune, which would be given to them by her Uncle Kael if they helped her access the factory. The group later arrived at the detention block, defeated the droid guards and found the Chyrr and Aetheri Knight inside.

After the Chyrr was presented to the newcomers by Drayden, the group created a plan to retrieve Drayden's flux blade, which involved Vorren and the senator going alone to the roof to retrieve the flux blade. The Aetheri was scared of her going alone with the Chyrr, but accepted it. After the Chyrr and the senator left, the Aetheri began to wander across the factory, and found a section of the factory that produced cortosis B2 super battle droids , which was guarded by the duke and his relatives and some droid sentries. The Serennians were protecting themselves with replica trooper armor covered with cortosis, Drayden tried to confuse Solha's droids but he only achieved making the Serennians aware of his presence. Drayden then left the room and began his search for the senator once again.

On the roof of the building, Ondar and Vorren retrieved Drayden's flux blade, they also witnessed some spheres which descended onto the factory, which were part of a Chyrr mission to retrieve a deflector shield generator from the factory. The senator asked the Chyrr commander to continue helping them but he insisted that his mission came first. Meanwhile, the Aetheri, disarmed, used Synth Flux to see where Solha and his droids sentries were, so he did not have to fight them, while he traveled across the maze of rooms, and corridors that the factory contained. Until he met with his wife who gave him his flux blade, and told him that Vorren had abandoned them. Their meeting also coincided with the Chyrr Ascendancy 's raid on the factory, retrieving the shield generator. Drayden fought several B2s and discovered that their armor disabled flux blades and deflected volt pistol bolts. However, he used Synth Flux and disabled them.

In the meantime, Ondar evacuated the workers with LebJau before the factory was destroyed, Vorren joined the senator and the Aetheri Knight who revealed that the ore was cortosis and that it deflected volt pistol bolts and disabled flux blades, thanks to its large energy absorption rate. They learned that the ore was mined in the river next to the factory and strengthened the battle droids' armor making them invulnerable, something that could change the Replica Wars forever. Vorren then agreed to destroy the factory after the Chyrr retrieved the shield generator.

Ondar encountered the duke, who wore replica trooper armor covered with cortosis, who protected him from his husband's flux blade, he then told her that by using the factory he was going to build his name in the Fractio. As he knew Drayden and the senator, he rejected Ondar's ultimatum to surrender and told her that he had no doubts that he'd live and that he'd never surrender.

In another section of the factory, the Aetheri and Chyrr learned the Dissidents were are also producing replica trooper armor covered with cortosis. The Aetheri thought they were planning to infiltrate the Republic by sending Serennian troopers wearing replica trooper armor to Vantor. After the battle droids protecting the room were defeated by Drayden and Vorren, and the Serennian duke by the senator, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the factory and then destroyed the factory. Drayden decided to destroy the mine too, so the Dissidents could not start again on Mokivj, though, Ondar, Vorren, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the cortosis.

Aftermath of Mokivj

The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In an effort to collapse the Dissident's efforts to produce cortosis battle droids, Drayden accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best cropland and water. Drayden did not know the cortosis in the mine would redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust, activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation, deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive synth flux doing more than merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.

The catastrophe forced LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Vorren had warned Ondar that Drayden's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Aetheri and Senator to regret their actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Vorren had also warned Drayden that his theory about the replica armor being used to infiltrate the Republic seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite Drayden's shortsighted plan, Vorren continued to respect the man and still viewed him as an effective warrior.

#### Bombing of the Temple

Drayden and Bolt-3 were in the Aetheri Sanctum prior to it being bombed by Dissident captain Rackham Sear . The bomber was foiled by Depa Billaba and Caleb Dume .

#### Ondar's departure

Prior to a mission that Ondar was taking to Clabron , Drayden insisted that he be allowed to assist Ondar, under the impression it was a diplomatic mission to Duro . After assurance from Ondar, Drayden agreed to remain on Vantor, kissing Ondar. He was startled when he realized Ondar's handmaiden, Moteé , had witnessed their display of affection, though Ondar assured Drayden that Moteé understood discretion. Ondar lied to Drayden about her mission because of his close ties to Chancellor Mal.

#### Crystal crisis on Uthara

> " How well would you sleep, knowing that I failed you? " " Not very well, I imagine. Luckily, that isn't true, and never will be. " ―Kael Drayden and Orien Kade

During the last months of the war, Kade and Drayden were sent to Uthara to investigate the mysterious death of Aetheri Master Tu-Anh . Once they landed on Pau City , Inspector Jen June led them to see the corpse at the morgue . After examining the body, they went to the place where she had been found, only to determine she had been hit in the optic nerve with a precision laser dart by a sniper. As they found the sniper's position, from the building's owner, Garri , the two Aetheri learned of the involvement of MagnaGuards and began to suspect the involvement of General Skarn, and had Inspector June analyze some slimy substance they had found—the secretion of an Amani 's skin.

Though Governor Torul Blom asked them to leave Uthara, Kade and Drayden persevered and rented two dactillions to go talk to the leader of an Amani tribe, who revealed that an Amani outcast had entered one of the caves. The two Aetheri entered it to find MagnaGuards. Despite some coalition, they managed to have the droids reveal the involvement of the Sulie and went to see Inspector June once again. Soon after, the two attended the funeral for Tu-Anh.

As they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, the Amani scattered, leaving Drayden and Kade to follow the tracks of one that appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Drayden mentioned Marr for the first time since her departure, and Kade asked if he wanted to talk about it, yet Drayden refused. Kade then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest. However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Drayden to tell Kade he missed Marr and to voice his anger at the High Council for turning its back on her. To Drayden's annoyance, Kade stood by the Council's authority, asserting that it had been Marr's decision to leave and that she had allowed her emotions to cloud her judgment, something against the Aetheri way. Then, Kade suggested he rest, but Drayden declined so that he could keep the first watch. Finally, he questioned Kade on what would happen if he had turned out to be a "major disappointment," but Kade wanted to believe it would never happen.

However, they both fell asleep and were captured by a group of Sugis led by Chong , who brought them before Endente as Kade told Chong of their "intention" to buy weapons. They learned Endente was, in fact, selling a kyber crystal , and Drayden prompted him to show it before they made the deal. Brought to the starship where the crystal was being kept, they escaped from their captors, and Drayden eliminated all the Sulie, except for Endente, who fled. After they discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship, the two Aetheri managed to transport the crystal through Uthara's plains until they reached Pau City, where Blom had the two Aetheri surrounded with his men and Skarn's droids. As the kyber crystal was loaded into a shuttle and sent to a ship on orbit , Kade and Drayden stole a freighter to recover the crystal.

During the persecution, Skarn damaged their ship, forcing them to perform a number five special ; they crashed it to disable the hyperrelay and, with their escape pods, boarded the ship where the crystal was then held. As they got separated, Kade was captured by Skarn, and Drayden recovered his flux blade, located the crystal, and freed Kade. Together, they made their way to the kyber crystal at the vault, where they were temporarily trapped until a squad discovered them. Using the crystal and Synth Flux to knock them out, he managed to get to a hangar, where Drayden stole a shuttle to escape. Meanwhile, Kade set several AATs to shoot and overload the crystal, destroying the crystal and the ship, while they escaped from the explosion in the nick of time and returned to Vantor to report to the Council.

#### Stationed on Vantor

> " Should I send a detachment, General? " " [...] No. It's nothing. " ―Yular and Drayden, as the latter senses Sela Marr

Sometime later, while on a Vorash -class Iron Cruiser, Drayden discovered an unknown ship, the Silver Angel , in military airspace. Drayden then asked Admiral Yular who was on that transport. After he closed his eyes and sensed Sela Marr's presence through Synth Flux, he decided not to send a detachment to arrest the crew and to let Sela and her companions leave.

#### Vossa and Vray

> " Vray isn't capable of anything as selfless as love. And Vossa couldn't possibly have cared about a murderess like her. Besides, she's the one who took him to the umbra side in the first place. " " Stranger things have happened. And maybe, if she loves him, she's his way back from it. " ―Drayden and Ondar discuss Vossa and Vray' relationship

Aetheri Master Daren Kelv had been tasked by the Aetheri Council to assassinate Varek with the help of Kessa Vray, but during a mission to Raxun Secundus the Aetheri turned to the umbra side due to Varek's manipulations and Vray's training. Vray staged a rescue mission , which failed and prompted her to contact the Aetheri Council. When she arrived at the Aetheri Sanctum, Drayden and Kade escorted her to the Council Chamber , where despite Drevan Koss's open disapproval, Zho dispatched Drayden and Kade to go with Vray to rescue Vossa above Tarisa . Soon enough, Kade, Drayden, and Vray were on the cockpit of her ship, the Banshee , to infiltrate the Dissident dreadnought from which Vossa, or rather "Admiral Enigma," was issuing orders.

Stealthily, the three boarded the ship and made their way to the bridge, where they found Varek, instead of Vossa. Drayden then engaged in a duel with the Count, distracting him while Kade and Vray discovered a captive Vossa. Though she knew Vossa was consumed by his hatred, they escaped to the Banshee , and Drayden joined with them to escape the enemy ship and return to Vantor. Back in the Aetheri Sanctum, Drayden saw as the Council welcomed Vossa and pardoned Vray. That same night, Drayden reunited with his wife, Ondar, for a moment of respite, during which Drayden told her of the ordeal—after which, Ondar conferred that perhaps Vray's love could be Vossa's way back from the umbra side, leaving Drayden at a loss for words.

A month later, Drayden was sent alongside Kade, Vossa, and Akar-Deshu in a mission to take over a Dissident supply storage base and redistribute the supplies to worlds in need of them. However, their mission went awry when Vossa, unbeknownst to them, arranged the base's destruction. After another failed mission to Vossa's name, he was suspected of treason, Drayden and Kade were sent to spy on Vossa and Vray as they were deployed to assassinate Varek during the second battle of Krestophis . When they saw Vossa attempt to synth flux the Count to lead him to his master, Vael Mal, rather than kill him, Drayden and Kade intervened, taking the pair by surprise and arresting them.

However, Vossa and Varek soon escaped from the Vigilance , causing the death of Akar-Deshu and Kav Bayons , and boarded Vray' ship, Banshee to escape Krestophis. Drayden had the Banshee fired on, forcing the renegades to land on the planet below. He and Kade pursued the three injured fugitives to a Dissident-controlled tower , where they surrounded them. There, Drayden and Kade witnessed what appeared to be Vray' last moments, sacrificing herself to save Vossa from Varek's fatal Synth Flux lightning . After her death, Drayden and Kade were joined by the redeemed Vossa, and they tried to capture Varek, to no avail. They then transported Vray' body and Vossa to Vantor, where the fallen Aetheri confessed his crimes and Kade defended Vray.

#### A New Droid Army

After confiscating a Munificent -class star frigate with Squad 326 , Drayden was joined by Grand Master Zho and informed about the mysterious Dissident project, Operation: Titan . Hacking into the frigate 's datacore , they were able to discover that Operation: Titan was being overseen on the planet Enthenium . Together, Zho and Drayden traveled to a Dissident base on Enthenium to uncover more information about the supposed "weapon" that was being developed under Project: Titan. Once they snuck onto the base, Zho climbed through the vents, found information on the "weapons", and confronted Skarn, who was stationed on the base. Meanwhile, Drayden and Squad 326 were battling battle droids as the base went on alert. After Zho came back and aided in the fight, the team escaped before reinforcements arrived. On the frigate once again, Drayden was informed by Zho that on Golatha the weapons being developed were a new army of megadroids four times taller than a human. Drayden quipped that they should have expected the Dissidents to eventually make their droids bigger.

Drayden and Zho then informed the Aetheri High Council via hologram about their planned mission to Golatha . Drayden believed that, because the planet was small, the forces guarding the droid factory would also be small. Zho warned against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Master Koss agreed with Zho and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Drayden was offended by the idea that his plan was overconfident, doing an impression of what Zho had said to mock him. Replica Captain Cesar interrupted their brief argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Zho and Drayden surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and that an access point must be in the valley. Zho and Drayden, along with Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no droid guards. However, volt pistol fire shot out from the trees, with the replicas unable to stop whatever was firing at them. Zho was able to stop the droid guards firing on them and find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were all killed.

They entered the facility alone, with Drayden noting that the size of an attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more droids, Drayden mentioned to Zho that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megadroids. Drayden didn't think they could destroy the whole thing, but Zho pointed out the central column holding up the factory. Drayden asked if he thought taking out the column would take out the whole facility. Zho cheekily responded with an impression of what Drayden had said earlier, with Drayden having to admit that the impression was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A single megadroid shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of Drayden and Zho. Drayden mentioned that it seems to big, to which Zho replied that size was only a matter of perspective. They try attacking it, but its durasteel coating was stronger than normal, leaving it immune to flux blade attacks. Zho climbed inside the megadroid, but just as the door closed, the droid shot itself into space. Drayden, terrified for the Grand Master's safety, got into his interceptor and chased after the droid. To his relief, Zho responded to his calls. Drayden had to stop the rotation of the droid so he could attach his ship to it, but the droid was too big and heavy. He tried asking Zho to do it, but since the droid was disabled and the cold vacuum of space weakened Zho, he could not. Zho instead instructed Drayden to do it, reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just light. Calmed down, Drayden was able to stop the droid. Zho then cut his way out from the inside and jumped to Drayden's ship, and he was able to get Zho to safety. On the ship Aspirant , Drayden told Zho that their destruction of the Golatha factory had put the Dissidents back years at least. He then asked Zho if he really was too weak to stop the rotation of the droid on his own. Zho said he'd never tell him.

#### Anaxes

> " Tell me the sequence to disarm the bomb! " " Never. Varek would kill me for losing Anaxes. " " And you think I won't ? " " You're an Aetheri. Your nobility-- " " I don't have such weaknesses! Now, let's try that again. " ―Kael Drayden and Trench

Following an assault on Cato Geltaria , Drayden was dispatched alongside Koss to participate in the Battle of Anaxes to defend the Republic shipyards on Anaxes . With mounting losses on the Republic side, Captain Vek told Drayden and Koss his theory that the Dissidents had learned his own strategy. Considering this possibility, Koss sent Vek and Commander Cord to take a squad, and Replica Synth Flux 99 , behind enemy lines to infiltrate a Dissident Cyber Center while he and Drayden held the line. The general staff was told about the possibility of sending a mission to Skako Minor , and Drayden was sure the Aetheri High Council would approve it. Replica Sergeant " Hunter " also stated The Bad Batch would go with them. Although Vek was eager to get going, Drayden informed him they had a "thing" to do, and, although Vek felt they did not have time, they both went to the barracks.

Vek remained outside on watch while Drayden, holding Vek's helmet, went in and contacted Ondar. Drayden apologized for being away for so long, but, as the two spoke, Orien Kade approached Vek. The Replica Captain knocked on the door to speed up Drayden. Drayden confided that he was worried about his captain, worrying that Vek was letting his personal feelings drive him too much on the mission, although Ondar believed Vek had learned that trait from him. Outside, Vek tried to lie about what Drayden was doing. However, Drayden and her ended their conversation, and both went on their way to Skako, although Kade revealed he knew he had been talking to Ondar by quipping that he hoped Drayden at least greeted Lira for him. In response the Aetheri stopped in his tracks, and although dismayed that his effort to be secretive had been for naught, Drayden kept his composure and only gave his master a knowing glance before walking away.

As they reached Skako Minor on the Marauder , Drayden was captured by the Poletec on their keeradaks , prompting Vek and the Bad Batch to follow them to the village and rescue the Aetheri General. They apologized and explained the situation, and the chief helped them reach Purkoll , which they infiltrated with some effort. When they reached the antechamber, Foreman Wat Tambra sent a transmission saying they had violated the Artificer Union 's neutrality, and sent droid reinforcements. While Drayden, Hunter, Wrecker , and Crossline destroyed them, Vek and Tech found Echo—alive, but as a cyborg.

Tech managed to unplug Echo, and they escaped to the ventilation system before being vaporized by Tambra's Decimator , after which Wrecker destroyed the Foreman's main computers with explosives. As they escaped, however, they were surrounded by his D1-series aerial battle droids , but thanks to Tech, they rode keeradaks to the Poletec village . There, they convinced the natives to help them against the Artificer Union's forces, including their octuptarra tri-droids , with Drayden effectively destroying two of them.

Following the skirmish, Drayden, Vek, and Replica Synth Flux 99 returned to Fort Anaxes , where Koss and Kade were to take the Dissident assembly complex by air, whereas Echo was to be escorted by the Bad Batch, Drayden, and Vek into Trench's communications vault on a dreadnought on the planet's orbit, where he would plug himself to the dreadnought to feed the Dissident's strategic movements. When Echo did so and deactivated the droids attacking the assembly complex, he realized that Trench had initiated a countdown for an explosion that could destroy most of Anaxes. While Kade assisted in the evacuation efforts, and Koss deactivated the bomb, Drayden went to the dreadnought's bridge to confront Trench, destroying his droid bodyguards. Trench refused to give the final sequence number, believing that Drayden would not harm him. Drayden amputated Trench's prosthetic attachments, prompting the Harch to immediately give in. After relaying it to Koss, who deactivated the bomb, Trench then tried to electrocute him; in response, Kael thrust his flux blade through the Harch's chest, killing him. He then took the self-destruct detonator for the dreadnought and reunited with the rest of his team.

As they left aboard the Marauder , Drayden let Wrecker destroy Trench's flagship, the Invulnerable , damaging the nearby Dissident fleet, and they went back to Fort Anaxes. Following the victory, the Republic prepared medals for the team, while Corporal Echo joined "The Bad Batch."

### Birth of Xarn Velgor

#### Battle of Yerbana

> " Sir, we've received a transmission from someone using your subspace frequency "Fulcrum"." "Torrik Vess?" "Perhaps the siege at Underra has taken a turn for the worse." "No, sir. It's not Vess. You had best take this transmission here on the ship. Both of you. " ―Yular, Drayden, and Kade

At some point before the Battle of Vantor , Drayden was on Tythe and had another encounter with Varek, yet he failed to defeat the Count. Along with the 501st and 212th, Drayden and Kade were sent to Yerbana to take down the Dissident forces on the planet. The fight caused Drayden and Kade to split their respective battalions into two fronts.

Drayden, Vek, and the 501st Legion defeated the Dissident Droid Army on their front, so they left to assist Kade and his 212th Attack Battalion, which was suffering heavy casualties from the ongoing conflict. While Kade and Commander Cord were taking cover from the blasts, Drayden ordered his men to hide under the bridge, while Bolt-3 would tell them when to launch. Next, Drayden stepped into the fight and convinced his Master to follow his plan. Drayden faked a surrender to draw out the droid commander , who saw through the lies of the Aetheri Knight; however, it was too late, as Drayden used his Synth Flux abilities to destroy the droid commander, allowing his 501st Legion led by Captain Vek to make a surprising ambush on the rest of the droid army, ending the conflict.

The 212th Attack Battalion proceeded to help the 501st while Drayden was approached by Kade who complimented him, which Drayden answered by thanking Kade for helping achieve victory. The two were soon contacted by Admiral Wulric Yular, who informed them of an upcoming transmission going under the code " Fulcrum ," while the two Aetheri thought it might have been Torrik Vess, Yular confirmed otherwise saying it was necessary for the generals to attend the call themselves.

#### The last meeting of a friendship

> " You capture Vreth. I'll take care of Skarn. With any luck, this will all be over soon. " " Master Kade always said there's no such thing as luck. " " Good thing I taught you otherwise. " [Drayden begins to leave] " Kael! Good luck. " ―Kael Drayden and Sela Marr's final words to each other as friends

At Yular's ship, Drayden and Kade were surprised to see Sela Marr and Bo-Katin Velara were the ones that had made the call. Emotionally shocked to see his former apprentice after several months of being apart, Drayden barely managed to control himself and proceeded to talk to Marr, who remarked the moment was not a reunion as Vreth had been located on Ferrum and they had an opportunity to capture him.

Marr hoped Drayden and Kade would be able to join forces with her to confront him. Marr, Velara, and her Ferriks boarded the ship, where they were greeted by Drayden and Kade. The former tried to greet Marr, but his former apprentice turned him down saying they had to act immediately if they wanted to capture Vreth. The group went to discuss the possible attack, but Kade was not willing to help Marr and her allies unless the Aetheri Council gave their permission, causing Drayden to unnoticeably scowl at him. While Kade contacted the Council, Drayden had Marr accompanying him through the complex. Drayden led Marr to a room full of the 501st replica troopers, including Vek, who had painted their helmets as a way to honor her former Commander. Drayden then prepared to give Marr her old flux blades , but before he could do so, the ship's alarm suddenly went off.

Kade rushed in and informed Drayden and Vek that they would be jumping to hyperspace immediately. When Drayden asked if this meant the attack on Ferrum was approved, Kade revealed that they were actually heading for Vantor, where a dire situation was happening: General Skarn had launched a full-scale attack on the capital of the Republic, and the Aetheri Council had lost contact with Shaak Ti, Chancellor Mal's assigned protector. Drayden was visibly uncomfortable about this news, but Kade assured him that their fleet would arrive at Vantor very soon.

Marr, however, was angered by her former Masters' decision to abandon Ferrum. Kade pointed out that they were entering a pivotal moment in the Replica Wars, but Marr countered that Kade was playing politics and that the Aetheri Order had lost their way by forgetting that their duty was to help people and not politicians, which was why the people, and Marr herself, had lost faith in them. Drayden heard the argument and weighed in; so as to help Marr in the upcoming siege while also taking part in the mission to rescue the Chancellor, Drayden promoted Vek to Commander so he could lead the a new formation of the 501st that was dubbed the 332nd Division , assigning Marr to assist as an advisor due to her being unable to hold an official military title. When Kade agreed, Marr accepted the proposal. Kade left the room to prepare their troops leaving Drayden and Marr behind. Drayden gave Marr her new flux blades complimenting them as better than before.

Drayden then headed to rescue the Chancellor, believing the defeats of Vreth and Skarn would mark an end to the war. While he was leaving, Marr stopped him by wishing him good luck in his mission. Happy to have been reunited with his apprentice, Drayden smiled and they both parted ways. Unbeknownst to either of them, this would be the last time they ever saw each other as friends.

#### Confrontation on the Invisible Hand

> " Good, Kael, good! [...] Kill him. Kill him now." "I shouldn't…" "Do it. " ―Theron Mal and Kael Drayden, on killing Varek

Having been away from Vantor for months due to the Outer Reach Sieges , Drayden and his Master finally returned to the planet after leaving Marr, to lead the rescue of the Chancellor from General Skarn before the cyborg's fleet could flee the besieged Capital . Assisted by a squadron of ARC-170 starfighters led by Replica Commander Odd Ball, the two Aetheri fought their way through a swarm of Vulture and tri-fighter droid starfighters protecting the Invisible Hand , General Skarn's flagship. Drayden and Kade headed for the ship while the replicas distracted the droids, but Kade's ship was damaged and partially disabled by Buzz-droids . Although Kade told Drayden to abandon him and complete the mission, the younger Aetheri refused and cleared his former master's ship of droids before escorting him to the Invisible Hand ' s hangar. After crashing landing, the two Aetheri disposed of the few remaining droids in the hangar while Bolt-3 located the Chancellor.

Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Drayden sensed the presence of Count Varek. Not long after they arrived at the top of the spire, the Umbra Lord appeared before them and the two Aetheri engaged him in a duel. Varek managed to gain the upper hand and knocked Kade out, but his taunting, intended to erode Drayden's morale, instead enraged him. Drayden unleashed his fury on the Umbra Lord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off both of his hands. Taking Varek's flux blade, he crossed both his and the Count's blades at the Count's throat, and Mal urged him to kill his helpless opponent. Drayden initially hesitated, but after the Chancellor's continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Varek. He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not the Aetheri way , but the Chancellor assured him that he had been right to kill the Count.

Despite the Chancellor's pleas, he carried Kade on his back to make their escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help from Bolt and the attack taking place on the Invisible Hand by a neighboring Republic cruiser, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and turning the shaft once more into well. Kade regained consciousness while they were hanging on inside the shaft and together, he and Drayden were able to escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them. However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by a ray shield and apprehended, along with Bolt, by battle droids. The two Aetheri and the Chancellor were brought before Skarn in the Invisible Hand ' s control room. The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Drayden's youth considering his reputation. Drayden retorted with a taunt about the General being shorter than he had expected, which angered the cyborg general.

Eventually, Bolt-3 caused a distraction, allowing Kade to draw his flux blade to him with Synth Flux, freeing himself and then Drayden. Once the younger Aetheri had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Skarn's MagnaGuards and the battle droids holding them and the Chancellor. However, as they attempted to apprehend Skarn, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take him. Drayden and Kade caught onto nearby command consoles until security measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him, however, Skarn escaped, jettisoning all the escape pods to prevent them from escaping. As the command crew had fled, Drayden took control of the damaged flagship, which had begun to enter Vantor's atmosphere. Although he briefly managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too great, and the Invisible Hand broke in half, picking up considerable speed as it fell towards the Republic capital. Drayden eventually succeeded in crash-landing the Invisible Hand on a deserted landing strip, returning the once-captive Chancellor to Vantor unharmed.

#### Looming fatherhood

> " Are you all right? You're trembling. What's going on? " " Something wonderful has happened. Ani, I'm pregnant. " " ...That's... That- That's won- That's wonderful. " " What are we going to do? " " We're not going to worry about anything right now, alright? This is a happy moment. The happiest moment of my life. " ―Lira announces her pregnancy to Kael

Following the ordeal, the two escorted the Chancellor to the Senate building, where they separated—while Kade went back to Aetheri Sanctum to report to the Council, Drayden remained with the Senators who had come to greet the returning Chancellor. He briefly spoke with Senator Solen, who expressed hope that Count Varek's death could bring a quick end to the war. Drayden disagreed, saying that as long as General Skarn remained as leader of the Dissident armies, the war would continue. At that moment, he noticed Ondar behind one of the columns, took his leave from the Senator and reunited with his wife for the first time in months. Having been away fighting in the Outer Reach Sieges, Drayden's single focus was reuniting with Ondar. Ondar then revealed to her husband that she was pregnant. Though they were both worried that this might expose their secret, Ondar especially, Drayden was overjoyed and told her not to worry, saying that it was the happiest moment of his life. The couple returned to Ondar's apartment, where they began to make plans for when the baby would be born, deciding that their child would be raised on Ondara, in the Lake Country . Ondar also planned to head there before the birth, to prepare the room.

However, that same night, Drayden's happiness was marred when he suffered a nightmare of his wife dying in childbirth, similar to the visions he had had of his mother before her death. Although Ondar tried to comfort him that it was only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his mother. Ondar tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they ask Kade for his assistance. Drayden refused, insisting that their baby was a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Aetheri Sanctum, he sought Zho's counsel concerning these dreams, but the Grand Master told Drayden that death was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.

#### Tensions of loyalty

> " Is it possible to learn this power? " " Not from an Aetheri. " ―Drayden and Mal discuss the power to stop people from dying

At the same time, tensions were rising between Chancellor Mal and the Aetheri Council, who were concerned with his increasing power. Matters were made worse when the Senate announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Drayden was informed of this by Kade, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the Council and Kade's warning to be wary of Mal. He then told him that the Chancellor had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the Council. So Drayden met with Mal at his office, where he told him that he was appointing the young Aetheri to be his personal representative on the Council, a decision that Drayden believed the Aetheri Masters would never accept.

When informed of this, the Council allowed Drayden to sit on the Council, yet they refused to grant him the position of Master, a decision which greatly angered Drayden. Drayden turned to Kade, who so remained silent. Angered more by this, Drayden reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on the council. At the end of the meeting, Kade acknowledged to Drayden that his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Council, he could spy on the Chancellor, which Drayden was reluctant to do. In recent weeks, Ondar and other senators concerned about Mal's expanded powers had also created the Petition of 2,000 in the name of protecting democracy, requesting he return his emergency powers to the Senate and end the war with cease-fire negotiations. Drayden was manipulated by Mal into believing Ondar could have ulterior motives that she was keeping hidden.

At the Galaxies Opera House in the Uscru District , Drayden joined Mal in his private box while he was watching Squid Lake . During the performance the Chancellor told Drayden that Replica Intelligence had located General Skarn in the Uthara system . He began to stir Drayden's distrust in the Aetheri council, claiming that the Aetheri were planning on taking control of the Republic and betraying him. Drayden felt conflicted — despite the Aetheri's mistrust in the Chancellor, he still felt that Mal was a good man. He confided in the Chancellor that he had lost trust in the Aetheri. Mal correctly guessed that it was because they had sent Drayden to spy on him, and that it made him feel dishonest. Drayden didn't know how to respond. The Chancellor claimed that the Aetheri were only perceived as good because "good is a point of view," stating that the Aetheri and the Umbra were not so different. While Mal's conflation of the two groups played to Drayden's devotion to his Order's ideals and helped set the stage for his conversion to the umbra side, Drayden rebutted by saying that the Umbra were selfish while the Aetheri were selfless. Mal then recounted the Tragedy of Vael Plagueis the Wise to Drayden framed as an "old Umbra legend". In reality, the tale was about his Umbra Master and how he killed him. Mal specifically told the story knowing that Drayden was having nightmares about his wife dying. The revelation that he might need to disobey the Aetheri Order to save Ondar opened Drayden's mind — he wanted to learn this power. Afterward, Drayden bid farewell to Kade, showing gratitude for his teachings and said to each other " May Synth Flux be with you ," before parting ways.

#### Fall to the umbra side

> " A powerful Umbra you will become. Henceforth, you shall be known as Vael… Velgor. " ―Vael Mal to his apprentice

Shortly afterwards, Kael and Koss attended a hologram meeting with Zho, Ki-Adi Mundi, and Ciel Venn in the Temple's war room . During the meeting, Cord joined and informed the group learned that Orien had engaged Skarn on Uthara. Koss then tasked Kael with informing the Chancellor and see what his reaction is in order to learn his true intentions. Drayden informed the Chancellor of Kade's forces engaging Skarn's, but during the meeting, Mal revealed that he knew the umbra side of Synth Flux , leading Drayden to realize that he was the Umbra Lord they had been searching for. Mal told Kael that if he turned him over to the Aetheri, then he would be unable to aid in saving his wife, Ondar, from her "fated" death that Kael had been dreaming of.

Despite his confusion, Drayden still was loyal to the Aetheri Order. He told his findings to Drevan Koss, who went with Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin, and Agen Kolar to arrest Mal, leaving Drayden behind. After recalling Mal's words, Drayden headed to Mal's office. During the ensuing duel , Mal killed three of the four Aetheri with ease but was disarmed by Koss just before Drayden arrived in his office. Drayden pleaded with Koss not to kill Mal, saying that he should be left for the Senate to judge, but Koss felt that Mal was too dangerous to be left alive, as he essentially controlled the Senate. Ignoring Drayden's words, Koss prepared to execute the Umbra Lord, but a desperate Drayden who needed Mal alive to save Lira, drew his flux blade and severed the unsuspecting Koss's right hand, causing Koss to drop his flux blade and allowing Mal to send him plummeting out of a shattered window with a blast of Synth Flux lightning, all of which was sensed by Marr.

Though horrified that he betrayed and contributed to the death of Koss, Drayden saw this as the final straw against the Aetheri Order, and as such there was no turning back for him. Desperate to save Lira, he reluctantly betrayed the Aetheri and pledged himself to service under his new Umbra Master, Vael Mal. Mal named him "Xarn Velgor," a title that he did not bestow lightly, and promised that, together, they would unlock the secret to immortality. Whereas earlier Mal had implied he already had that power, Drayden was so focused on the possibility that there was a chance to save Ondar that he did not notice the contradiction. After Mal declared all Aetheri enemies of the Republic, he ordered Velgor to kill the Aetheri at the Temple and execute the Dissident Council on the Emberfall System . Mal then executed Directive 66 , during which the replica troopers were forcibly brainwashed into turning on their Aetheri Generals and executing them on the spot, while Velgor led the 501st Legion to attack the Aetheri Sanctum .

During the assault, Velgor killed dozens of Aetheri, such as Master Cin Drallig. In the council chamber, he found a group of younglings trying to hide. Thinking Velgor was still an Aetheri Knight, one boy asked him what they should do, but the new Umbra Lord slaughtered the boy and the other younglings. Similarly, as he led a synth flux of replicas further into the Temple, he encountered a group of younglings who assumed he had arrived to help. Instead, however, he cut the children down, leaving only one survivor; after Velgor stabbed her and looked her in the eyes, the human youngling Revai Sevander , growing to be filled with rage, survived because she played dead amongst the corpses of her classmates. After the battle, Velgor manipulated the Temple's beacon to broadcast a signal telling any surviving Aetheri to return to Vantor where they could be murdered by the replica troopers stationed at the Temple.

Following the attack, he met with Mal, who then sent him to Emberfall to kill the remaining mebers of the Executive Dissident Council . Before his departure he went back home to Ondar and told her much to her disbelief that the Aetheri Order had tried to topple the Republic and that he was about to go to Emberfall. Telling her that he would end the war, they kissed and he left aboard his new green Eta-2 Actis -class light interceptor , which replaced the interceptor he had lost in the Battle of Vantor.

On Emberfall, Velgor slaughtered the entire Dissident Council, including Wat Tambra, Poggle the Lesser, and Nutis Gunray, along with their aides, his eyes now yellow and red with rage. Simultaneously, Mal carried out the proclamation of the New Order in an emergency session of the Senate, claiming that the Aetheri Order had committed treason against the Republic, and reorganized the Republic into the first Imperium with himself as its Emperor . During this time, Velgor was followed by Kakan , who believed he was a aetheri. Velgor showed his face, eyes to Kakan and chased after him to ensure there would be no witness. During his chase, Velgor ran into a cloud of Lava fumes . The fumes caused Velgor to see hallucinations his fellow Aetheri such as Koss, Vael, Vayne, Billaba, Zho, and his former apprentice Marr. Velgor was able to push the fumes away from himself and dismissed the hallucinations. Soon, Velgor reported his mission's completion to Mal; on his orders, Velgor sent a shutdown signal to the Confederate navy and army, effectively ending the Replica Wars. With his mission completed, and the Dissidents dead, Velgor looked on from a balcony and shed a tear at the man he had become, but knew it was too late to turn back.

### Becoming Xarn Velgor

#### Duel on Emberfall

> " You were the Seeded One! It was said that you would destroy the Umbra, not join them! Bring balance to Synth Flux, not leave it in darkness! " ―Orien Kade, to Xarn Velgor — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Soon thereafter, Velgor saw Ondar's star skiff land and ran to meet her. She told Velgor that Kade had discovered their secret and had also told her that Velgor had joined the umbra side, hoping that he'd prove Kade wrong. However, Velgor only confirmed it to be true. Driven power-hungry by the Umbra side and declaring the galaxy was at peace, Velgor tried to convince her that they could be happy together as rulers of the new Imperium, promising he could overthrow Mal. In Velgor's ideal world, Ondar would have taken his hand, believing the man before her was still her husband. In reality, however, Velgor's propositions and actions horrified his wife, who begged him to leave the umbra side behind.

At that moment, Velgor saw Kade walking out of Ondar's ship because he had secretly stowed away. Convinced that his wife had betrayed him to give his old master a chance to kill him, Velgor, ignoring her attempts to explain, Synth Flux-choked Ondar until Kade demanded he let her go, by which point she had already fallen unconscious. While what he had just done horrified him, Velgor lashed out at Kade by blaming him. Although Kade tried to reason with his former pupil, Velgor refused to listen, as the new Imperium was everything he wanted the galaxy to be. Unable to come to an understanding, with both believing themselves to be in the right, the two former friends then engaged in a fierce duel , which ripped across the Dissident facility . As they fought throughout the Dissident war room, their duel damaged the computers, causing the facility shields to deactivate and allowing the building to start falling into the lava river below.

After fighting across the crumbling building and over a lava river, the duel ended on the river banks, where Kade claimed the high ground and warned Velgor not to attack. Blinded by rage and arrogance, Velgor leaped to continue the duel, only to have his remaining limbs severed by his former master's blade, leaving only his cybernetic arm. Crippled, Velgor slid down to the riverbank while screaming his undying hatred for his former master. Kade, taking Drayden's flux blade, told him he had considered him his brother and remorsefully left him for dead as a flap of Velgor's clothing caught fire and he started burning. Kade then left the planet with Ondar, Astra-9, and Bolt-3 as Velgor lay screaming and burning on the riverbank.

#### Rebirth

> " Lord Velgor, can you hear me? " " Yes, Master. Where is Lira? Is she safe? Is she all right? " "It seems, in your anger you killed her. " " I? I couldn't have. She was alive! I felt it! NOOOOOOOO!!!!! " ―Vael Mal and Xarn Velgor, after the latter rises in his armor

Although Velgor survived, he was horrifically scarred, both physically and mentally, and had damaged lungs due to the hot ash in the air. Shortly afterward, the Emperor—having sensed his apprentice's peril—arrived on Emberfall, where he found the severely wounded Velgor and took him back to Vantor, in a medical capsule . As the Emperor could not afford to lose Velgor, he turned to the Republic's best scientists, among which was Silvar , who, in doing so, gained private access to Velgor's cybernetics. By the Emperor's orders, Velgor was put into a life-sustaining black suit of armor and outfitted with three new robotic limbs by numerous droids skilled in both cybernetic reconstruction and medical surgery. Among the droids working on Velgor was FX-6 , an FX-9 surgical assistant who previously worked on Skarn's armor. After what Mal later called a "long night" of work, he was finally sealed within his new armor. His eyes showed a brief flicker of fear before his mask and helmet locked together and he drew his first breath.

Effectively entombed within and smothered by his new life-support armor, Velgor's rebirth was complete. Upon regaining consciousness, Velgor asked the Emperor what had become of his wife, and he told Velgor that he had killed her in his anger. Unbeknownst to either of the two Umbra, Ondar gave birth to the twins Joren and Elira before her death. Velgor was overwhelmed by his despair in the belief he had killed Ondar and their unborn child, which fulfilled his prophetic dreams of her death. Remembering that he had, indeed, attacked Ondar with a Synth Flux choke on Emberfall, he believed Mal's claim. Further, he was both enraged and filled with despair that it had been his own choices that led to his current reality, of a life imprisoned within a cybernetic suit and without Ondar by his side. In his emotional turmoil, Velgor incidentally destroyed the medical droids and severely damaged the entire room through Synth Flux. He broke free of his bindings on the operating table and, despite struggling to walk under the sheer weight of his new prostheses and armor, let out a cry of anger and despair over losing everything he had loved.

Velgor's despair then turned to rage and he telekinetically slammed the Emperor against a wall in the surgical bay, snarling that his master had promised to save Ondar. The Emperor, as Velgor attempted unsuccessfully to strangle him with Synth Flux, responded that in death Ondar had provided Velgor a gift: Pain. The Emperor continued by giving Velgor a choice; to either accept and use that gift or to die. After a moment, Velgor replied that he would live.
