After finding the ship empty, he stood on the roof. Dengrik and fellow hunter Honnah fired on the SKREE fighters near Velgor's ship and destroyed them. Velgor looked in their direction as the bounty hunters took aim.

However, Varrick had a plan that involved an electro-pulse , an E.M.P. device that only worked on contact, so they withheld their fire. Velgor used synth flux to kick up dust around him so that the bunty hunters didn't have a straight shot. One of the bounty hunters, Chio Fain, charges and shoots at him. Velgor blocks his fire as well as fire from the other bounty hunters. Fain continued to fight Velgor, intent on avenging his partner Rone. As the Ardennian taunted him, Velgor chopped his arms off. Fain then asked Velgor how good he was against a Gamorrean battle-ax , to which Honnah started to attack. Dodging the Gamorrean 's swings, Velgor took a hit from Fain, using his last remaining arm to fire at him. Velgor then used synth flux to push Fain into Honnah's battle-ax, killing him. After taking a shot from Gita, Velgor incapacitated her and killed Honnah. Varrick took the opportunity and tried to stick the electro-pulse to Velgor but was unable to. He was then shocked by Dengrik, revealing himself to the crew as being a double agent for the Imperium and Velgor.

Velgor brought Varrick on his Iron Cruiser Vengeance along with Dengrik. He gave the bounty hunter his reward and asked abut the Hidden Hand. Dengrik said that he had told Velgor everything he knew, and that Varrick would not tell him anything more. Velgor dismissed him and told him that he knew where to find him, if the Imperium ever required his services again. In the interrogation room, Varrick had been questioned for days, to no avail. Velgor arrived, dismissed interrogation staff and started torturing Varrick for answers until he passed out. Once awake, Varrick escaped his constraints and killed two guards. Exiting out using their armor and weapons, he was greeted by his ship and Velgor. Velgor took him to Chorin and Varrick told him how both he and the Imperium failed to protect Chorin, creating the Hidden Hand. Velgor told him that what remained of his people would be safe. Knowing his history as an Imperial cadet, Velgor welcomed him back to the Imperium.

Varrick was able to locate the Hidden Hand's hideout and lead Velgor and his troops there. Velgor fought through the Hidden Hand's forces until reaching its leader. Gwi, the Tarsunt that initially hired Varrick to kill Velgor, was now held at gunpoint by him. Velgor used synth flux to lift Gwi to safety, he was ordered to bring the Emperor's prize to him alive. As shock troopers dragged Gwi away, Velgor detailed to the Tarsunt how he and each member of his cabal would be hunted and killed. He then asked a shock trooper to bring him Varrick. When the trooper told him that Varrick was missing, Velgor synth flux choked him and sent troops to Chorin to raze Varrick's settlement and kill everyone in it. When troops arrived, all the inhabitants had been evacuated. Varrick had successfully escaped Velgor and saved his people.

#### Another attempt to resurrect Lira

> " She was almost within reach. Now she is lost to me again—because of you! " ―Xarn Velgor, to the Corvax descendent

Despite his previous failure to restore his wife, Velgor was eventually tempted to try again when he discovered the Aeon Engine and with it the tale of Lady Corvax , who had attempted to restore her husband to life when he fell in battle using the most sacred artifact in Emberfall, Bright Star , to power the Engine. Although the process was a failure, Velgor, believing Corvax failed due to her lack of power, was confident that if he could get the Bright Star, he could successfully use the Bright Star and the Aeon Engine, and thus made efforts to find the Bright Star, but discovered Lady Corvax had protected her devices so only those with her blood could access it.

Undeterred, Velgor searched for any survivors in Lady Corvax's bloodline, which he eventually discovered with the help of Imperial Admiral Gable Karius use an Interdictor vessel to pull a starship named Windfall out of hyperspace above Emberfall and capture the smuggler who was the descendant he had been looking for. Velgor proceeded to demand the smuggler to open a configuration box to confirm their relation to Lady Corvax, and once they succeeded, Velgor left with a reminder that he would need their services later. He communicated with the Black Bishop , who had been his ally in his search for the Aeon Engine, where they discussed about how the Bright Star would give Velgor the incredible powers needed to even resurrect the dead and that with Corvax's descendant in his grasp, the final component needed to achieve his goals was now Velgor's. Dismissing the Bishop once he sensed Lira's soul echo through the Aeon Engine, Velgor let out a wail of fury and sorrow once she quickly disappeared after announcing her pregnancy. Catching up to the smuggler and the ZO-E3 in their attempts to escape, Velgor tossed aside the droid before getting the smuggler to open the lock to the sanctum beneath his fortress. Successful in activating the lift to access the lower levels of Fortress Velgor where his ultimate prize lay, his Shock troopers were killed by a number of ancient droids , but Velgor and the smuggler swiftly cut down the droids before he confiscated the smuggler of their flux blade and telling them that they had handled their own well. Seeing that the smuggler needed training in the ways of Synth Flux, Velgor decided to provide instruction to the smuggler.

Reaching the inner sanctum of Corvax Fortress , Velgor demonstrated the power of Synth Flux on a nearby broken statue while proclaiming Synth Flux as powerful beyond their imagination, as it "discloses the enigmas that will cause the fragile and broken to turn away in horror and agitation", and could grant them access to the secrets of both life and death, and that the smuggler had power, but would need to learn more about Synth Flux to overcome the challenges ahead. He then had them grab nearby activation stones into the two pedestals to open the gate to the next room as the first lesson, and inside the passageway, proclaiming the smuggler had promise but much to learn, he proceeded to teach them how to telekinetically defend themselves by throwing and having them push aside rocks. Seeing the smuggler had glimpsed the power of Synth Flux, Velgor decided to resume their training later and viewed a message left behind by Lady Corvax for one of her descendants, dismissing Lady Corvax's plea to fix her mistake as her never understanding the true extent of that which she had created. Treading down the pathway, the third test for the smuggler came when they came across a large room populated by nests of Lava nymphs , Velgor demanding them to tap into the umbra side and exterminate the vermin. Unimpressed by their difficulties in doing so, Velgor stated their abilities were hardly acceptable and lacking in much regards. but before he could work on the Smuggler more, activating the pedestal with a key caused a large portion of the floor begins to break apart, and despite Velgor's efforts to keep the Smuggler under his watch, lifting up the floor, a darkghast attacked him, forcing him to let go of the Smuggler to fight the creature. Despite the separation, Velgor caught up to the Smuggler just as they had retrieved the Bright Star with a tipoff from the Mole. Seeing that the Smuggler had intended to backstab him, Velgor beat them to the punch, retrieved the Bright Star, and left them and their friends stranded on the cave after coldly making it clear their betrayal meant nothing as they had outlived their usefulness.

Placing the Bright Star on the Aeon Engine, Velgor began the ritual to resurrect Lira, determined to save his wife at the cost of everyone else, willing to even sacrifice other planets if necessary. Lira's face began to emerge with her voice repeating her same words from long ago, indicating that there was the possibility that the ritual could work, but the Smuggler reemerged to stop the ritual, knowing the potential catastrophe that the device could cause. Although surprised by the smuggler's survival, Velgor believed it didn't matter, and engaged his brief apprentice in a duel as he vowed that he would succeed where Lady Corvax had failed. As the smuggler managed to resist Velgor's attacks, the Bright Star's energies coalesced, finally causing a bluish projection of Ondar in full-bodied form to appear, as she began to utter some of the last words that Velgor had heard her tell him long ago. Velgor then paused the duel in its tracks, holding the smuggler in a choke hold, as he realized that their presence was affecting the Aeon Engine. However, it was too late as her projection faded. Realizing the ritual had failed, Velgor became furious, blaming his failure on the smuggler, and proceeded to easily overpower him. However, before he could kill his opponent, the Black Bishop intervened, stopping Velgor temporarily and giving the smuggler the opening needed to destroy the Aeon Engine. By the time Velgor was free, he could only beheld the Engine's destruction in horror, and before he could strike down the smuggler, the Engine exploded, sending the two far away from one another.

#### Downed by Red Four

During a battle , Velgor's ship was downed when a Vector intentionally crashed into it. Using the callsign Red Four , Micha 's sacrifice is momentarily thought to have killed the Dark Lord, that is until Velgor emerges from the wreckage alive. He activated his flux blade, facing the Insurgent troops.

#### Visit on Emberfall

> " Be careful not to choke on your aspirations, Director. " ―Xarn Velgor to Orren Callan Vossik — Link ▶️ ( file info )

When Cass Virel managed to enter the World Between Worlds , he heard Kael Drayden's voice when he was on Krestophis. Velgor's voice was then heard telling an individual to not underestimate the power of the umbra side. As had been suggested by Grand Admiral Vorren shortly before his, the Chimaera ' s, and Virel's disappearance in the Liberation of Lorath , Velgor served as part of the Void Core project despite his dislike of the battle station, as Vorren had noted he was the one individual in the Imperium who could recognize and deal with all threats to the station. Vorren had also used this appointment to synth flux Assistant Director Brierly Ronan to join the Chyrr Ascendancy, as this Imperial official would not have been able to hide his contempt for the Emperor from the Umbra Lord.

Shortly after the destruction of Embermoor City , while floating in the tank, Velgor was approached by his servant Vainé . He informed Velgor that Director Vossik, the Imperial in charge of the construction of the Void Core, had arrived as summoned to explain a number of recent problems involving the battle station. The most pressing of these problems was the discovery that Galen Arsa, an engineer who had played a pivotal role in the creation of the Void Core's superlaser, had been a traitor and that there was a distinct possibility that he had leaked information about the weapon to the Insurgency.

Vossik had been recently informed by Governor Varrix that he was no longer in command of the Void Core project, and was keen to impress upon Velgor his need for an audience with the Emperor, ostensibly to discuss the weapon's destructive capabilities. Velgor instead chastised Vossik for the destruction of Embermoor City, the city where the Imperium had been mining the kyber crystals needed for the Void Core's primary weapons systems to function. Vossik attempted to shift the blame for the city's destruction to Varrix, but Velgor was unmoved. He informed Vossik that the Imperial Senate had been told that Embermoor had been destroyed in a mining disaster and that the Void Core did not exist. He then impressed upon the director in no uncertain terms that he was to make certain that Galen Arsa had not compromised the Void Core in any way. Taking this as confirmation from Velgor that he was still in command of the project, Vossik began to ask Velgor if he would speak to the Emperor on his behalf. But before he could finish his question, Velgor cut him off by telekinetically closing his throat, with a sardonic warning to not "choke" on his aspirations.

#### Stolen plans

> " The insurgent flagship is disabled, my Lord. But it has received transmissions from the surface. " " Prepare a boarding party. " ― Shaef Corssin and Xarn Velgor

Shortly afterwards, Insurgent spies attacked the Imperial security complex on the planet Halcyon, stole the technical readouts of the Void Core, and transmitted a copy to the Insurgent flagship Profundity . As the Insurgents began to flee to hyperspace, Velgor arrived at the scene of the battle in the Iron Cruiser Reckoning and quickly crippled the already damaged Insurgent flagship. He and a small group of shock troopers then boarded the vessel and began searching for the plans. As the Insurgent crew began to evacuate the Profundity , a small group of Insurgent troopers copied the Void Core schematics onto a data disk and attempted to board the Korrathi corvette Tantiva IV and escape. Mere meters away from one of the corvette's airlocks, the Profundity experienced a power loss, jamming an automatic door in the path of the Insurgent soldiers.

Unable to get the door to open more than a few centimeters, the Insurgents were intercepted by Velgor, who unleashed his fury in the enclosed space to slaughter the helpless Insurgents. As Velgor reached the trooper with the plans, the Insurgent managed to pass the data disk to a compatriot of his behind the door before Velgor impaled him and telekinetically wrenched the door open. While Velgor finished eliminating the remaining soldiers, the trooper to whom the plans had been passed dove through the open airlock to the Tantiva IV , and the Insurgents managed to launch the ship mere moments before Velgor was able to board it. Once the ship was away, the plans were given to Velgor's estranged daughter, Princess Elira Solen, who had been tasked by her adoptive father to find Orien Kade on Dunemere.

#### Pursuing the plans

> " I don't know what you're talking about. I'm a member of the Imperial Senate on a diplomatic mission to Cindervale. " " You are part of the Free Coalition, and a traitor! Take her away! " ―Xarn Velgor and Elira Solen

Despite their daring escape, the Insurgents aboard the Tantiva IV did not get far. After failing to capture the plans aboard the Profundity , Velgor had immediately re-boarded the Reckoning and pursued the Insurgent corvette. The Reckoning intercepted the Tantiva IV in orbit above Dunemere, and quickly disabled and captured it. Velgor and his troops boarded the ship, killing many Insurgent troopers and gaining complete control of the blockade runner. Velgor interrogated its captain, Raymus Rask , before killing him for not revealing the location of the plans. Velgor's troops captured the princess, but not before she placed the plans inside Bolt-3 and sent him in an escape pod to the planet's surface. Although the Princess tried to use her diplomatic immunity as a member of the Imperial Senate, Velgor had her arrested as a insurgent and a traitor.

As she was escorted to the Reckoning , Velgor was informed that an escape pod had been jettisoned during the fighting but that no life forms were aboard. Believing that the Princess had hidden the plans there, Velgor sent a battalion to Dunemere to recover the plans while he took the Princess to the Void Core for interrogation. In hyperspace, Velgor would catch Solen when she had escaped her guards and made her way to one of the Reckoning ' s shuttles, and announced that the Emperor was disbanding the Senate as they spoke, leaving her defenseless. Velgor then had her follow him to the ship's bridge shortly before arriving at the Void Core, where he had her sent to her cell in Detention Block AA-23 .

Meanwhile, Velgor accompanied Varrix to attend a meeting with the Joint Chiefs , including General Cassio Targ and Admiral Conan Antonio Motti , who mocked Velgor's reliance on an "ancient religion," only to have Velgor Synth Flux-choke him. Before he could kill him, Varrix intervened and instructed Velgor to find the location of the Insurgent base from Solen. Subsequently, Velgor went to torture her with a mind probe -equipped interrogator droid , but as she resisted, he and Varrix turned to coercion—by bringing the Void Core to the Cindervale system , they threatened to destroy her homeworld if she refused to disclose the location of the Insurgent base. Eventually, she told him that the Insurgents were on Steelgrove, yet Varrix ordered Cindervale to be destroyed anyway, to make an effective demonstration of the Void Core's power. Velgor restrained the Princess as the Void Core's superlaser destroyed her planet. Velgor then took Solen back to her cell to await her execution.

### Enter Joren Drayden

#### Duel on the Void Core

> " I've been waiting for you, Orien. We meet again at last. The circle is now complete. When I left you, I was but a learner. Now I am the Master. " " Only a master of evil, Vael . " ―Xarn Velgor and Orien Kade, before their fourth and final duel

After the scout ships sent to Steelgrove found the base abandoned, Varrix ordered Velgor to kill her immediately, but the Corsair Wing was captured in the ruins of Cindervale by the Void Core's grapple beam. Identified as a suspicious ship from Port Kess , Velgor concluded that they were trying to return the stolen plans to the Princess and went to inspect the freighter himself. Told that the crew had apparently abandoned ship after take-off, Velgor gave orders for the ship to be scanned to be sure. At the same time, Velgor sensed the presence of his old master, Orien Kade. When news reached him that the Princess had been released from her cell, Velgor decided to confront Kade alone.

Velgor found Kade after he had deactivated a grapple beam terminal to allow the Corsair escape the battle station, and the two engaged in one final duel. During the fight, Velgor noted that Kade's age had made him weak. However, Kade told his former apprentice that he would not win because he would become more powerful than Velgor could ever imagine, even if he managed to kill him. As they fought, the two reached Bay 327 just as Kade's companions and the Princess reunited to board the Corsair , only for Kade to see his latest apprentice, Joren Drayden, Velgor's son.

As his former master gave him a mysteriously knowing smile, Velgor struck him down when Kade raised his blade in surrender. Much to Velgor's surprise, Kade's body disappeared, for his spirit had become one with Synth Flux, and Velgor tried to find remains in his robes but found none. As he did so, General Moradmin Bast asked who he was, and Velgor answered "An old man who thought he could help gifted children. He was mistaken." Velgor then tried to stop the Insurgents from escaping but was prevented from doing so by the closing of the hangar doors by Joren's shot. After the Corsair ' s escape, Velgor met with Varrix and confirmed that a homing beacon had been placed upon the ship and that they could follow the ship to the Insurgent base.

Kade's warning to Velgor came true; his consciousness survived his death, and he became a Synth Flux spirit . Meanwhile, still remaining hidden on Mirefall , Zho felt Kade and Velgor's duel through Synth Flux, especially noticing, for the first time, the loneliness in the man who had once been Kael Drayden. Zho noticed that Velgor's loneliness only grew after he struck down his former mentor, which nearly made Zho pity the former Aetheri and wish he could counsel him. As he continued his journey, Zho reflected on his past, blaming himself for not noticing Drayden's path to the umbra side.

#### Cinderfall 4

> " Synth Flux is strong with this one. " ―Xarn Velgor, about Joren Drayden during the Battle of Cinderfall

Velgor's plan worked and the Void Core followed the Corsair to the hidden Insurgent base on Cinderfall 4 . In the Void Core's conference room , Velgor awoke Varrix from a vision about his father just as the battle station approached Cinderfall Prime , with an officer informing them that Cinderfall 4 was located on the far-side of the planet. As the Void Core prepared to destroy the moon, Insurgent Vectors and Yarrows starfighters attacked the Void Core, having identified a weakness: a thermal exhaust port which led to the reactor systems; a direct hit on the port would cause a chain reaction and the destruction of the station. Although Varrix remained convinced in the station's invulnerability, Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Tanbris that the thirty attacking Insurgent starfighters were small enough to avoid the pulse lance defenses. As such, Velgor ordered fighters to launch. When Gold Squadron broke off from the main group and begun a trench run towards the exhaust port, Velgor boarded his SKREE Advanced and, flanked by two SKREE fighters, managed to terminate three of the last four Gold Squadron pilots. He continued to destroy most ships of Red Squadron , killing Red Leader Garven Dreis .

As the last members of Red Squadron engaged in a trench run while the Void Core was preparing the destroy the moon, Velgor damaged Torb Rask 's Vector, forcing him to break off formation, and killed Biggs Darklighter , leaving only the leader of the group, Joren. Velgor quickly realized that Synth Flux was powerful in the young pilot, but nevertheless proceeded to get him into range. When he finally succeeded, he was just about to destroy the young pilot when the newly arrived Corsair Wing intervened and destroyed one of Velgor's escorts. Before the Corsair could destroy Velgor, his other escort accidentally bumped into Velgor's craft, destroying the other escort and causing Velgor's craft to veer off course and send him plunging into space. This prevented him from stopping the Void Core's destruction, which killed Varrix and was a major defeat for the Imperium.

Shortly after the battle, Velgor sent a distress signal to the Reckoning to send a ship to pick him up and bring him back from the Cinderfall system to the Iron Cruiser. Sometime later, he would fly just beyond the ever-expanding debris field that once was the Void Core, when Imperial pilots Ciena Ree and Berisse Sai arrived in a Gozanti -class cruiser . Docking his SKREE Advanced x1 with the freighter, Velgor passed through the airlock door and met Ree. Then, he ordered Ree and Sai to remain in the hold for the remainder of the voyage while he took command of the freighter until they returned to the Reckoning . As the insurgents quickly abandoned their base in the wake of the battle, Velgor joined the Imperial retaliatory attack, but the insurgents were largely able to escape. Velgor ordered for a blockade of the Cinderfall system to begin at one point.

#### Post-Cinderfall ventures

Sometime later, an Imperial officer managed to obtain one of two flux blades formerly owned by the Umbra Vael Atrio . Velgor arrived on Hradreek , where the officer welcomed him, supported by a contingent of shock troopers. Velgor looked at the flux blade and asked where the other one was. The officer was confused by his question, and Velgor Synth Flux-choked him. As he gagged, he claimed that he bought the flux blade from a smuggler and that she must have tricked him. Velgor told a shock trooper to divert all divisions to finding the smuggler and the other flux blade. Velgor then killed the officer as the shock troopers scattered to begin the search.

A squad of shock troopers found the smuggler, Sana Starric , and they chased her down the streets. Starric paid a gang of Aquari to stall them, and they got in the way of the shock troopers. However, Velgor appeared and cut them down using his flux blade and Vael Atrio'. Velgor then followed Starric' path to a cargo bay, and he used Synth Flux to move the crates and block her from a clear exit. Starric then shot at Velgor, and he used Synth Flux to disperse the plasma bolts. Starric rigged one of the crates and then waited for Velgor to follow before detonating it, throwing Velgor to the side and burying him. Velgor soon freed himself and made his way to the nearby podracing track. Velgor entered the seating area, and the audience scattered at his presence. Velgor noticed one of the dune racers, who was grabbing the attention of the commentary. The human, despite being biologically unequipped for a race like this, was winning the race, and Velgor used Synth Flux to cause one of the engines to malfunction, crashing the dune racer. The pilot of the racer was none other than Joren Drayden, who had obtained the other of Atrio' flux blades. Joren decided to destroy the flux blade, as it overwhelmed him with anger whenever he needed it. Velgor watched him escape in his Vector, and he decided to crush Atrio' other flux blade.

According to a tale later told by Vainé, at another point after the Battle of Cinderfall, Velgor's Iron Cruiser was contacted by Cranwell , the assistant to Varrix Initiative scientist Restin , to warn them that Doctor Restin had betrayed the Imperium. Restin, who wanted a weapon to use against Velgor as revenge for the destruction of Cindervale, had created a genetic serum that would turn those injected with it into rampaging Gamorreans, who could then spread the infection by touch. Velgor decided to deal with Restin himself, so he arrived at Restin's base of operations with two shock troopers and pretended to not know he was a traitor. After witnessing the doctor turn Cranwell into a Gamorrean with the serum, Velgor suggested the serum could be used against the Insurgency. When Restin turned the infected Gamorrean against the Umbra Lord, he effortlessly lifted it and his infected shock troopers into the air with Synth Flux. He did the same with Restin, forcing the scientist to confess his treachery before revealing he had already known he was a traitor. Intending to be a firsthand witness to his destruction, Velgor forced the three Gamorreans and Restin to touch, transforming the doctor into one of his creations. Leaving the infected scientist to the mercy of the Gamorreans, Velgor left aboard his Vectis -class shuttle. He had never feared Restin's experiments, as Velgor already viewed himself as a different kind of monster.

#### Cymoon 1

> " He's here. The Insurgent pilot who destroyed the Void Core. I can feel his presence. He will not escape me again. " ―Xarn Velgor

Several weeks after the Battle of Cinderfall, Xarn Velgor was called to Weapons Factory Alpha on Cymoon 1 . He was to negotiate , or rather synth flux an arrangement with an envoy of Grukk the Grak about supplying raw materials through Grak-controlled space to the factory. Velgor arrived shortly after the envoy (secretly the Corsair Wing ' s captain, Dax Karrick ) and landed on a platform. As he was about to enter the facility, Velgor was shot at by a sniper, Brevok , but he deflected the volt pistol bolt and used two shock troopers as human shields as the Vorak rained down dozens of shots upon him. Unscathed, Velgor located Brevok's position and used to Synth Flux to collapse his vantage point, but the Vorak survived.

Velgor then ordered the arriving shock trooper reinforcements to hunt him down. One of them informed the Dark Lord that Overseer Aggadeen was out of contact and the factory's main reactor was on meltdown. As he surmised these occurrences were the work of Insurgents, Velgor put the entire moon on alert but also felt the presence of the pilot who had destroyed the Void Core, Joren Drayden. Although unaware of the boy's identity, Velgor made his way into the factory at once and came face-to-face with the Aetheri apprentice. Velgor soon realized that the boy had been cursorily trained by Kade, and after the briefest of duels, snatched Joren's flux blade. As he prepared to execute Joren for his refusal to disclose information about the Insurgency, he realized that the boy's flux blade had once been his own. Before he could question Joren further, the foot of an Insurgent-hijacked Ironclad Walker walker came hurtling down into the middle of their standoff, knocking the two apart.

In the ensuing chaos, Velgor sought to prevent the Insurgents' escape, and mowed down the factory's runaway slaves, commanding his shock troopers to execute all the escapees. Once Joren got away on a stolen 74-Z speeder bike and attacked Velgor's troops (leading the Dark Lord to reconsider his initial dismissal of the boy and wonder exactly what Kade had been up to before his death), Velgor personally saw to the walker's defeat. Karrick and Solen, commanding the Ironclad Walker, tried to step on him, but Velgor used Synth Flux to hold the walker's descending foot several meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.

He was foiled, however, when Karrick and Solen unleashed a barrage of the Ironclad Walker's laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old flux blade (which the current owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of rubble, a shock trooper approached; upon seeing Velgor's scarred face, they tried to apologize. However, Velgor killed them by twisting their head around using Synth Flux, right before Aggadeen reached him via comlink and asked for the facility's evacuation. Velgor vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and ordered to kill all the Insurgent invaders, except for the boy, whom Velgor would deal with personally. As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully defused the reactor, Velgor personally felled the Insurgent walker by slashing its legs with his flux blade.

As reinforcements arrived, Velgor sent them after the Insurgents on the trash fields, while he pursued the lone Insurgent who was returning to the factory, Joren. Aboard a combat speeder , Velgor chased Joren into the facility, but the Insurgent managed to destroy the power core with the speeder bike, leaving Velgor as the only survivor among the factory's rubble following its explosion. The Umbra Lord then saw Joren's abandoned speeder and the fleeing Corsair Wing ; as the Adjudicator ' s captain, Kronn , informed him that they had been unable to catch the freighter, Velgor Synth Flux-choked the captain. Then, as if he were talking to Kade, Velgor vowed to find the boy's identity and to turn him to the umbra side, so that the boy became his weapon, and not Kade's.

#### Cinderfall's aftermath

> " But you are the only one living to suffer my anger. You, an isolated survivor of the greatest military disaster in all my Imperium's history? Oh, you are truly the Seeded One, Lord Velgor. Chosen to be the one responsible. " " I will make it right. I will crush the Insurgents. " " No. I think not. " ―Mal and Velgor discuss Velgor's failings

Thereupon, Velgor returned to the Imperial Palace on Vantor to report to the Emperor, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Emperor bid Velgor to explain the events of Weapons Factory Alpha, which along with the Void Core's destruction, had put the Imperium very near disaster. Although Velgor considered Aggadeen to be responsible for the failure on Cymoon 1 (and the Overseer was tortured accordingly), the Emperor claimed much of the responsibility lay in Velgor's head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster in the Imperium's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag the Corsair with a homing beacon and let it escape with the Void Core plans. He then informed Velgor that Cassio Targ, now Grand General of the Imperial Army , would be his commanding officer for some time.

The Emperor then ordered Velgor to continue the unfinished Cymoon 1 negotiations with Grukk the Grak on Dunemere, right before going to Targ. As they returned to his office, Mal greeted one of his agents, Silvar, and Velgor asked for his identity, but the Emperor denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Velgor if there was anything else to report, but Velgor chose not to tell him about Kade's mysterious disappearance, nor the Synth Flux-sensitive Insurgent pilot who had destroyed the Void Core and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded an Imperial Iron Cruiser and immediately left for Dunemere, sending the Imperial vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

#### Dunemere negotiations

> « Hmmm. I see you know enough to tread carefully. But tell me... are you brave or foolish. » " That is not the question, Grak. The question is: Are you? " « Oh, Aetheri... always making everything so difficult. » ―Grukk the Grak and Xarn Velgor

Velgor soon arrived at Grukk's Palace where, during the meeting, he had the Grak dismiss his court. However, Grukk would rather claim the bounty on Velgor's head and had his people shoot at him, but Velgor killed them and forced Grukk to reconsider his position. After being Synth Flux choked, the Grak admitted he found it hard not to respect him. The two then came to an agreement: Grukk would provide Velgor with two of his best bounty hunters. Velgor left the Grak's palace and sought out a Dunefolk Raider camp , wherein he mercilessly slaughtered its inhabitants. He was then approached by Grukk's envoys: Vex Vark and Zazzik . While he sent Vark to find and capture the Insurgent pilot from Cinderfall alive, Velgor assigned the Vorak to bring Mal's agent to him. Both mercenaries agreed, and Velgor departed Dunemere for his Iron Cruiser.

The following day, Velgor returned in his official capacity as the Emperor's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Grukk welcomed Velgor and claimed that his resources belonged to the Imperium as long as he was paid. Velgor then turned to leave, leaving his shock troopers to tell Grukk what was required, but the Grak invited him to a feast and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. Aboard the sail barge Khetanna , Velgor and Grukk watched as the latter's guards killed wild striders for the sport until Grukk's court jester , Salacious B. Crumb , let out a loud cackle. Velgor warned the Grak to not let the Kowakian monkey-lizard do that again in his presence if he valued Crumb's life; Grukk merely laughed and told Velgor he now regarded him as a "good friend." The Grak then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Velgor berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Grak that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Imperial agent and a boy. Grukk argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Void Core, and had possibly been trained by Kade. The name prompted Velgor to request that Grukk tell him all he knew of Kade's exile on Dunemere.

Grukk first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to pilot the Khetanna to the Great Pit of Karkoon . He expected Velgor to be impressed by the devourer , which he believed was one of the few treasures that Dunemere had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note (like the Cinderfall pilot) could have ever been born on Dunemere. In silence, Velgor watched his homeworld's sunset.

#### Servitude unto Targ

> " You should think of yourself as your flux blade. A singular weapon from an older time—and one that is dangerous to everyone around them without a skilled hand to wield it. In time you will understand that I am the skilled hand you've been waiting for. " ―Cassio Targ to Xarn Velgor

After leaving Dunemere, Velgor met with Targ aboard his Sovereign -class Star Dreadnought , Annihilator . During a time of many raids against ships carrying Grukk's weapons and equipment supplies, Velgor was sent to protect an Imperial shuttle, CZ-246 , from a pirate attack . With his SKREE Advanced, Velgor went to their aid, alongside Black Two and Black Three , and successfully disabled the automated CR90 corvette attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to the Annihilator , Velgor reported to Targ that the pirate vessel had been captured and that he suspected a security breach.

As he said so, Targ disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Void Core as "Varrix's folly." Velgor noted that Varrix at least had a vision, prompting Targ to admit his plans were not as grand as Varrix's or Velgor's, but they still were effective; he believed that the Imperium could not be based around a single asset, like the Void Core or Velgor himself. Targ then informed Velgor that he intended to repair the corvette and use it to attack its base of origin , sending Velgor to head the attack, although with an adjutant , Lieutenant Oon-ai .

Velgor left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Velgor's personal communications to provide information to Targ. Although it annoyed Velgor, as it was the Emperor's command that he serve under Targ, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into a black astromech so as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Velgor, Oon-ai, and six shock troopers boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by a Velari pirate , whom he killed, Velgor ordered the troopers to secure the core, while his astromech made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.

They found themselves under the attack of two customized droidekas who brandished missile launchers, prompting Velgor to use Synth Flux to throw the incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the droids and fatally injuring the pirates. A Calavane pirate survived long enough to activate the self-destruct, and Velgor ordered the surviving troopers to return to the corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Velgor went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the corvette just before the station exploded.

Back on the Annihilator , Velgor reported to Targ, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syndicate), but also claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Targ should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Velgor isolated the black astromech under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the droid had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the droid through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.

Following an Imperial-Insurgent confrontation , Velgor went to the Palace of Grukkar the Grak to meet with Sergeant Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion "Velgor's Fist," Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grukkar' operations was a success, but that the Cinderfall Insurgent pilot had escaped them yet again. Velgor inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.

#### A droid army

> " There was a time I had armies at my beck and call. That time has passed. I need resources of my own. Private resources. " ―Xarn Velgor to Dr. Vexa, on why he needed her services

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Velgor learned that a Doctor Chelli Lona Vexa had supplied the pirates with the customized droidekas. As he wanted similarly effective units in the personal army he sought to build, Velgor set out to find Vexa, conducting a manhunt for her in many locations, gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of her presence on Quarantine World III in Kallidahin Space . He landed there just as Vexa was arrested by Utani Xane and a squad of B2 super battle droids; although Xane warned him that he was violating treaties, Velgor began destroying her droids and saved Vexa as she tumbled over a ledge. Velgor stabbed Xane through the back with his blade before telling the doctor he needed her services. He helped her up and she agreed.

Together, they boarded Vexa's ship, the Ark Angel II , where she admitted to being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Velgor to the Triple-Zero protocol personality matrix and estimated it would take hours to unlock the matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Velgor a few seconds to unlock it, which let Vexa install the personality matrix into a protocol droid's body right away. As the droid introduced himself as 0-0-0 , she imprinted herself and Velgor as his masters, and used him to wake up VT-1 , a blastomech prototype . But as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do any harm.

Velgor then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Vexa (who had originally acquired the two droids for the Gotra ) instead gave them to Velgor after realizing Velgor was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of unquestionable loyalty: battle droids. Vexa then suggested that they acquire an unusual droid foundry on Pyralis, one in possession of Queen Karina , who had survived the Replica Wars and the planet's sterilization . As they landed the Ark Angel , and she sent 0-0-0 and VT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that she and Velgor could follow, Vexa asked Velgor if he had ever been to Pyralis before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Ondar, shortly before the First Battle of Pyralis.

Velgor ordered Vexa to cease her probing, and entered the catacombs; she followed him. They reunited with VT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Velgor immediately amputated her from the womb droid factory. The queen, injured but alive, ordered her "children" to stop the intruders; at Velgor's command, VT-1 opened fire on the droids, and he and Velgor began to overwhelm her forces. Meanwhile, Vexa set a beacon on the ceiling with Velgor's help and ordered her Ark Angel to barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Velgor protected himself and Vexa by creating a Synth Flux barrier . His J-Type 327 Nubian then lowered a crane-platform, which Vexa attached to the factory while Velgor fought off the advancing droids, and the ship carried them and the factory to the skies.

They would later board the Ark Angel , where Vexa successfully activated the factory and told him he would have his private droid army. She then asked Velgor if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a flux blade right through the neck, with no warning. Velgor finally told her she had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform Velgor that he had a signal from Black Zazzik: he was bringing the Emperor's agent to him.

#### Silvar's enforcers

> " Synth Flux is obsolete. These are its successors. " " This is... blasphemous! " " This has nothing to do with Synth Flux. Much like you, Lord Velgor. I look at you, more machine than man, and I see a bridge between the old world and mine. In many ways, these are your children. " " Enough. " ―Silvar introduces Xarn Velgor to his enforcers

As Zazzik arrived, Velgor confronted the Emperor's agent, Silvar-IV. Velgor demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from the Emperor and the location of his headquarters . As he refused, Velgor assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve all relevant information from him. After an interrogation that resulted in the agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Velgor that he was Dr. Silvar-IV, that his research base was located in an Outer Reach nebula , and that his commission was to create replacements for Velgor. After learning this, they immediately departed for the base, aboard his J-type 327 Nubian . While Vexa remained on the ship, Velgor and two platoons of droid commandos infiltrated it. Velgor sent a platoon to "the dojo" and had the other one follow him to the barracks . As they killed the soldiers in there, Vexa reported that they had lost the other platoon, prompting Velgor to go to the dojo, where he met the twins Morit and Aiolin Astarte .

They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each other, Silvar-V appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Velgor why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not making apprentices for the Emperor but enforcers, as he believed Synth Flux to be obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Velgor attempted to strike Silvar down when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Velgor's children and successors. The Emperor then revealed his presence to Velgor and stopped him, claiming he wanted to see Silvar's demonstration without delay.

Velgor was immediately made to fight the Astarte twins, Tulon Voidgazer , the Calavane Karbin , and a Trandoshan warrior . As neither party was injured, the Emperor ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Velgor fought the Cyberanimate Trandoshan , but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Morit to intervene and kill his fellow enforcer. The Emperor then ordered the fighting to cease; Velgor warned Morit that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the Trandoshan's life was his to take. The Emperor then told the enforcers and Velgor that their mission was to strike down all who opposed the Imperium and that they were not to slay each other. As per his master's orders, Velgor followed him.

In private, the Emperor, discovering the remains of some of Velgor's commando droids, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enforcers. Velgor criticized his dealings with Silvar, as he saw his enforcers as a heresy to Synth Flux, but the Emperor berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Velgor who had disappointed him on Emberfall and had forced him to contact Silvar. As he left, he observed that perhaps, Velgor would prove himself worthy of the name he had given him back then.

#### Parenthood

> " Did you bring me anything of value, bounty hunter? " " Not much. Just his name. Drayden. " ―Xarn Velgor and Vex Vark

Troubled, Velgor returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Vexa told him that Vark wanted to report in during Velgor's absence. Subsequently, Velgor traveled to an Iron Cruiser to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark Lord was disappointed to hear that Vark had lost the boy, but before leaving, Vark told him his name: Joren Drayden. In his solitude, Velgor remembered Ondar telling him about her pregnancy, and Mal's half-truth, and cracked the viewport in his anger. Thereupon, he used his meditation chamber's lumen projector to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son; rather, Velgor assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission. Nevertheless, he accepted Joren as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.

Determined to find more about Joren, Velgor returned to Dunemere on the Ark Angel , with Vexa, 0-0-0 and VT-1 in his company. They landed at the Havel homestead, which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Imperial troops, and Velgor tried to find traces of Joren's presence. Unable to find anything of importance, Velgor left the moisture farm and went to Kade's home where he indeed felt the remnants of Joren's usage of Synth Flux during his duel against Vark, but nothing else. As they left, Vexa activated a molecular purge bomb to remove all remnants of their presence, and Velgor dismissed Vexa, for he was to return to his duties for the time being.

#### Anthan Prime

> " I'm trying to ascertain if you are... dangerous. " " Oh, I am, Lord Velgor... But only to the enemies of the Imperium. " ―Xarn Velgor and Inspector Thanoth

As part of Targ's crackdown on Outer Reach criminal interests who had gone unchecked in the past, Velgor took part in a mission to terminate a criminal organization known as Son-tuul Pride . He and his troops destroyed the Pride's base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Imperial light cruiser, but he also secretly sent Vexa all the information needed to steal the credits cargo. Following the theft , Velgor reunited with her on Anthan 13 , where he sent her to confirm some information about Commodex Tahn .

Velgor then attended a meeting between Targ and Silvar's enforcers, wherein the Astarte twins were assigned to deal with the Plasma Devils , and wherein Velgor let Targ know he wanted the mission of finding the Cinderfall Insurgent pilot (which was Karbin's mission). Targ felt he was too personally invested, and sent Velgor to punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-tuul Pride's fortune in the Anthan system ; he also informed Velgor that Inspector Thanoth was his replacement adjutant. While Thanoth went to the Anthan Prime Orbital Dockyard to investigate the theft's circumstances, Velgor returned to Anthan 13 and killed the entirety of a small Insurgent cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with Thanoth on Anthan Prime , where they sought Doowan , an employee of " the Dragon "—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosives needed for the theft—and interrogated him. The Raadan promptly revealed the location of the Dragon in his mansion , before the twins killed him in a show of synth flux.

Thanoth then noticed Velgor's passivity, but Velgor dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if Thanoth was dangerous or a traitor. The two then went to the Dragon's mansion, which Velgor entered on his own, killing the people inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Velgor everything to save himself, Velgor killed the Ortolan , and claimed he would have detonated the building once Thanoth reached the office. The inspector then opened the Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Velgor to ready a strike synth flux for when he located the suspects.

Velgor then used the opportunity to meet with Vexa, who had just come back from a raid on Commodex Tahn's villa on Ondara. She confirmed his sources: Ondar had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the Aetheri. Velgor then tasked Vexa with finding Joren before Karbin could; subsequently, she resorted to The Ante . Velgor then returned to the Iron Cruiser, where Thanoth they would travel to the Spire to find The Ante. When they found him, he told them the location of the Plasma Devils to show cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Vexa. Before the Givin could identify her by name, Velgor telekinetically forced an unlucky stray shot and killed him. Velgor then went to find Vexa before anyone else.

As they encountered each other, Velgor ordered her not to struggle and Synth Flux choked her, but Vexa claimed she knew of Joren's location. Velgor released her and ordered Vexa to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using Synth Flux to drop debris above him as she escaped on the Ark Angel . He got out of the rubble as Thanoth and the shock troopers arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her, Velgor and Thanoth boarded their Arquitens -class light cruiser. As they waited for their SKREE/sa bombers to catch Vexa, Velgor and Thanoth agreed to use their ready strike synth flux to kill the Plasma Devils before they could escape.

The two had their light cruiser go to the Thanteen Substrata on Anthan 1 , where the Insurgent cell was located. As per their plan, their light cruiser bombarded the westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Velgor went to the east route and felled incoming Insurgent ships by throwing his flux blade until the cell was annihilated. He then took three of the Plasma Devils' flight helmets and, alongside Thanoth, presented them to Targ, who agreed with their course of action given the tight situation. As Karbin reported that the Cinderfall Insurgent pilot was free, Velgor and Thanoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.

#### Shu-Torun

> " As a princess, you served your father. As a queen, you serve your people. This is a small reminder of what happens to worlds who resist the Emperor. This is what remains of Cindervale. " ―Xarn Velgor to Queen Triova

Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun with a message to reinforce their cooperation and synth flux its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and VT-1 killed the king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet the King of Shu-Torun , he instead met Princess Triova upon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu-Torun assassins that wished death to the Imperium and the king, but Velgor successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. Triova complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevators were being watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Velgor recognized Triova as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as lava poured into the tunnels.

However, he used his flux blade to make a round platform to float above the lava and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious Triova with him, Velgor made it to one of the corridors where he killed the guards. Triova awoke, only to point a volt pistol at Velgor. He, however, cut her hand off and entered the king's chambers to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Velgor's assassin droids. With Triova as the new Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered the Emperor's message; a piece of Cindervale, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Velgor then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but that she was to not forget whom she served.

#### Vrogas Vas

> " Xarn Velgor! Lay down your weapons! You are surrounded!" "All I am surrounded by is fear. And dead men. " ―Xarn Velgor responds to an Insurgent commando company ordering his surrender

Velgor met with Vexa and the droids, and demanded that he tell her Joren's location: Vrogas Vas . She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe droid from Karbin followed him. In his SKREE Advanced, he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of hyperspace, he found three Alliance starfighter squadrons orbiting the planet. As he sensed Joren's presence, he remained and charged against the starfighters, who dismissed his SKREE as a mere Imperial scout.

During the battle , Velgor engaged the Vector starfighters , and managed to take out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron , as he lost contact with Vexa. Velgor was then confronted by Joren, who was flying as Red Five , and flew his Vector into Velgor's starfighter, hoping to kill him. During the collision, their stabilizers were damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface. There, Velgor was hunted down by the Alliance forces, starting with Gray Squadron , but he promptly destroyed them by use of Synth Flux. Afterward, a company of Insurgent commandos moved in to attack him, but he activated some of their thermal detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Insurgent forces.

Velgor also took the volt pistol array of his fallen SKREE Advanced x1 and used it to take down further ships, this time from Cyan Squadron . Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Solen was leading them herself. Velgor, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Joren, Solen used the opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Velgor's location. Instead, Commander Karbin arrived with a task synth flux, and tried to take his victory—both his son and the princess' execution—from Velgor. Subsequently, Velgor engaged the enforcer in a duel, during which Solen escaped, and during which they entered the Aetheri Sanctum in ruins.

Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Vael's, Kade's, and Zho's voices), but continued the duel. Vexa then contacted him from aboard the Ark Angel and informed him that Joren had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Velgor had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Cinderfall pilot and Velgor's relationship than met the eye. Velgor then finished him and contacted Vexa to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Joren's capture. With Synth Flux, he brought down the shuttle transporting Joren, and Vexa tried to capture Joren only to be captured herself by the Insurgents, leaving Velgor to watch as his son on the Corsair Wing fled from Vrogas Vas.

#### Shu-Torun War

> " The queen of Shu-Torun. Did you choose well? " " There was no other choice. " ―Queen Triova and Xarn Velgor

Velgor then returned to the Imperial Palace and brought Karbin's body to the Emperor, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The Emperor then told Velgor to return to Shu-Torun as the ore-barons were rebelling and he required a full military intervention, and that Silvar (per Targ's request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Velgor returned to Shu-Torun, alongside Silvar, the Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Triova, Velgor led an attack against a rebellious delving citadel , and destroyed it as a demonstration of synth flux. He also accorded to treat Triova with the illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Vexa and return her to him, either dead or alive.

After sending a broadcast to the barons, Velgor, Triova, and Silvar and his enforcers went, per the queen's suggestion, to the ancestral retreat of the Shu-Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked by Baron Rubix 's Lava Leviathan , and Velgor used Triova' escape vessel to reach the Leviathan and dared the twins to follow him. With their flux blades, they boarded it, and from the inside, the three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with magma and be destroyed. Upon returning, Triova suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she had identified the ship's owner) and so, Velgor told Silvar to prepare his enforcers. In the meantime, he killed Beebox for trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.

Soon enough, they carried out the siege to Rubix's delving citadel , using the Shu-Torun Loyalist/Imperial Delving Fleet 's combat drills , with Velgor heading the first wave alongside the Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Silvar, and his drill was redirected into a lithoporoite seam, where they were surrounded by Rubix's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Velgor left his troops as well as droid platoons to protect the drill and had the Astarte twins follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him, but Aiolin saw they could not win against Velgor and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Silvar; Velgor then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Silvar's deceit before finishing her off.

Velgor then returned to his forces, who had secured the enemy lines thanks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Triova' assault to the delving citadel was already underway. With no opposition, Velgor's combat drill resumed his way to citadel, and Velgor himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Triova soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending the insurgency. Velgor then left the planet and informed the Emperor of Silvar's treachery, and the Emperor simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector Thanoth saying that he had located Vexa and that they needed to talk.

#### Hunt for Silvar

> " I often wondered whether what rose from our operating table was a machine or a man... Good to have an answer. " ―Silvar, to Xarn Velgor

At the shipyards of Kuat , Velgor met with Mal aboard an unfinished Sovereign -class Star Dreadnought, the Sovereign . The Emperor explained that in order to save Velgor after Emberfall, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to the Umbra, among them Silvar. Silvar eventually grew too powerful within the Varrix Initiative and so Mal was forced to make him overplay his hand. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Velgor met with Thanoth on Anthan 13; the Inspector, aware of Velgor's real identity, revealed that he would support Velgor's treason against the Emperor and that he would reveal Vexa's location on the Cosmatanic Steppes . However, Thanoth understood that Velgor would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell the Umbra Lord in person so Velgor would not waste time looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Vexa's location and thanked "Kael" for their work together, Velgor killed him with his flux blade.

Outside and ignoring the droids' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Velgor ordered 0-0-0 and VT-1, who were joined by Zazzik and his commando droids, to return Vexa to the Sovereign or kill her as their priority orders. Velgor also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Cosmatanic Steppes to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Vexa had told anyone about her work with Velgor, they were silenced. Velgor then departed aboard his SKREE Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Silvar back alive, and returned to the Sovereign . Aboard, he confronted Professor Thlu-Ry , a colleague of Silvar's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in the Crushank Nebulae . Velgor took the Reckoning to the nebulae, where his crew confirmed Silvar's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be thanked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Velgor led the assault on Silvar's fleet from his SKREE Advanced. After Silvar's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable the Reckoning and moved to escape, Velgor fired into the whale ship and crashed into the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to hyperspace

Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into hyperspace and entering the labs, Velgor was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a cyberanimate rancor as a test of her technology just as Silvar's fleet arrived at Kuat. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Velgor killed it by throwing his flux blade through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned one of Voidgazer's defense droids against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with the Sovereign ' s systems to incapacitate the crew; Velgor finished her off and went to the empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Silvar and Morit as they had fled in the escape pods, to find that Silvar's flagship was to collide with the Sovereign . Before it did, he ejected and landed on the Sovereign ' s outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to the Sovereign ' s bridge, where he confronted Silvar. The scientist simply disabled Velgor's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Velgor to kneel and drop his flux blade.

While Velgor was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former master, Orien Kade. This time, however, Kade threw him into the lava. Velgor then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old master. However, like their duel on the Void Core, Kade's body disappeared. Velgor then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Drayden fought until Velgor came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a vision of his deceased wife, Lira Ondar. Velgor then found himself chained to the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Lira pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Velgor, however, choked the illusion while at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her husband was dead, Velgor used his flux blade to impale Silvar, who was astounded that Velgor was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Velgor had regained the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his success. The Emperor then ordered Velgor to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.

Velgor took control of his SKREE Advanced, flying into Silvar's command ship. After cutting his way through Silvar's forces, the Dark Lord found his way to the doctor's replication room. After destroying Silvar's spare bodies, Velgor made it to the command bridge. Velgor then confronted the doctor one last time. He mind-tricked the whale-ship into flying into a nearby star, destroying Silvar once and for all. After returning to the Sovereign , Velgor met with his master, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Vexa was present: while 0-0-0 and VT-1 had successfully brought Vexa to the Sovereign and were planning on giving Velgor suggestions on how to kill the doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Vexa realized Velgor's priority order only mandated the droids bring her to the Sovereign . As she realized as such and was dragged away by two shock troopers to await Velgor's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her escape. Reaching Mal's chamber, Vexa told Mal all of Velgor's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding Joren Drayden.

Instead of being angry with Velgor, however, the Emperor was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Emperor then left his apprentice to deal with Vexa as he pleased. Velgor then ordered Vexa to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Velgor would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Zazzik, 0-0-0, and VT-1. Making his way to the bridge, the Emperor informed him that Grand General Targ was to be demoted and that command of the Sovereign and the fleet would be transferred to Velgor, who Mal named supreme commander of the Imperial fleet . The Emperor then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Velgor then executed Targ and ordered Admiral Kendal Ozzel to have the ship ready in two weeks. Velgor then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.

#### Slaying the unworthy

After the insurgency began encroaching in the Mid Reach, The Emperor let Velgor loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the Void Core's destruction, allowing him to kill Imperial officials including Moff Coovern and Minister Khemt .

At some point after the Sovereign became his flagship, Velgor summoned Lieutenant Sulaco to the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a hangar bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Vectis-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission to Sarjenn 3 , the third moon in the Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Imperials landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Insurgent outpost as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats. Velgor was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Insurgents of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Velgor deflected a shot from a missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building with Synth Flux. Now at Sulaco’s house, Velgor identified the officer as an Insurgent spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Velgor ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.

#### Skirmish on the Scorca Retreat

> " I am the alpha and the omega of the Ordu Aspectu! I am the eternal Rur, Aetheri Knight " " I am Xarn Velgor, Umbra Lord. I killed the Aetheri…and none shall escape me " ―The eternal Rur confronts Velgor

Sometime later, Vexa began an auction of a large kyber crystal, which contained a copied conscience from an ancient Aetheri named Rur , on the Sorca Retreat . However, Velgor was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by 0-0-0, who specifically left out the fact that Vexa had organized it as blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him and VT-1 to her will, they would inform Velgor of her survival. By the time Velgor arrived aboard an Imperial -class Iron Cruiser, the auction had turned into a massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a droid from the Cyban Front to be taken over by the conscious kyber crystal, making it hunt down the factions on the space station. As Velgor roamed the hallways, he found another Cyban Front droid , who had noticed him and his shock troopers moving about. The droid attempted to run, but Velgor used Synth Flux to smash it to pieces. Velgor ordered his troopers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.

After the freed 0-0-0 and VT-1 made their escape, and as some of the shock troopers crossed paths with the droid being controlled by Rur, Velgor cut down a member of the Dominion of the Ezaraa . Some of his shock troopers approached him, reporting coalition in the top dome and the droid. Velgor walked down the halls and found the droid standing over a pile of dead shock troopers. The droid had two blue-bladed flux blades, generated from the kyber crystal inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed. As they fought, Velgor deduced that Rur was not an Umbra. Rur claimed to be the last of the Ordu Aspectu and an Aetheri. As they deduced more about each other, Velgor Synth Flux-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Velgor charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut the droid's right hand off.

Velgor Synth Flux-pushed him again into a wall, but the droid began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of the defensive guns in the ceiling and used them on Velgor. Rur then tried to take control of Velgor's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Velgor told him that he was not the first to attempt that and used Synth Flux to repeatedly smash Rur against the walls. The three collisions with the walls demolished the droid and Velgor took the crystal. After the duel, a shock trooper reported to Velgor that some small ships had slipped his blockade. Velgor sensed Vexa's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the blockade. Velgor took the crystal back to Mal on Vantor and then archived it with the rest of his master's collection.

#### Tureen VII

After the death of Silvar, Velgor ordered Sergeant Kreel and his SCAR Squadron to hunt down Joren Drayden. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking the Imperial -class Iron Cruiser Harbinger . The vessel made its way to the blockaded planet Tureen VII in an attempt to aid the people there. Velgor and his SKREE forces engaged the attacking insurgent Vectors. While fighting the insurgent fighters, Velgor questioned Kreel if he had located Joren. At that moment, Kreel was engaging the insurgent in a flux blade duel. The Dark Lord then warned the shock trooper not to injure his "prize." However, Joren used Synth Flux to incapacitate Kreel, much to Velgor's anger. The insurgents eventually were able to break the siege and give relief to the people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron, however, was not left empty-handed, capturing the protocol droid Astra-9.

Soon after, Velgor was contacted by Kreel and informed that Astra-9 had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Velgor then told him that the droid was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him. Shortly after, Bolt-3 managed to infiltrate the Iron Cruiser where Astra-9 was held and broke the droid out. Velgor was notified of this and intercepted their Vector in his SKREE advanced. Bolt-3 knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Velgor's frustration, Bolt-3 gained the support of Joren, the Corsair Wing and the Volt Cobra , who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.

#### Confronting Kreel

Sometime later, Velgor approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen training droids . Velgor told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become an Umbra. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not an Umbra who saved him from the pits of Chagar IX , but the 501st Legion. Claimed to be a shock trooper to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a flux blade. Velgor then took his flux blade and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard the Harbinger . Kreel told Velgor that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Velgor and bury that flux blade in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Velgor gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible insurgent outpost on Horox III , bringing back Joren if he was present. Velgor then handed back Kreel's flux blade and told him to be worthy of it. Mal then entered the room and summoned Velgor. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Mal's presence and he thanked him. Mal then brought Velgor out of the room. Kreel then traveled to the Horox system and destroyed the insurgent outpost there, killing all insurgents present.

#### Sculpting a trap

Sometime later, Velgor was at his castle with Admiral Kendal Ozzel and General Maximilian Veers . There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Imperial projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing coalition. He argued that, although he could strike back, the insurgents were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Velgor's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that the insurgency was like weeds, while the Imperium was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the insurgency was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Velgor of when Elira Solen told Varrix: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers." Velgor told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set out with a new strategy: give the Alliance time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.

Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Triova in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapons. Triova asked Velgor how she could help him. Velgor revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of Embermoor. After she was to supply information to the insurgents that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining efforts. Finally, she had to infiltrate the insurgency, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Velgor said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Velgor then threatened her that he'd make Cindervale look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Velgor that he could not threaten Triova like that. Triova shot him as Velgor was their honored guest. She then allowed Velgor to kill the rest of the people in the room.

#### Velgor's master stroke

To Velgor's pleasure, Triova was successful in her mission, being able to earn Elira Solen's trust at Embermoor and helping them prompt a mass mutiny of Calavane merchant ships. As the insurgents armed their new cruisers at the Mako-Ta Space Docks , she gave Velgor the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing. As the insurgents prepared to scatter their new fleet, their hyperrelays failed to work, rendering the cruisers immobile from the system. Velgor arrived soon after with Death Squadron and Ozzel asked Velgor if they should begin a bombardment. Velgor told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As the insurgent fighters prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the hanger doors not opening, but neither were the defense turrets able to fire or were their engines able to activate. This was because Solen had ensured that the cruisers were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Triova could sabotage them. The cruisers were useless against Velgor's fleet, and Velgor ordered the nearest cruiser be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on General Vanden Willard's flagship Cinderfall's Hope and destroyed it with ease, killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Velgor of their next target, and Velgor said hope.

The Star Destroyers began engaging the other Calavane cruisers, and Triova attempted to get off the spacedocks on her ship. However, insurgent forces boarded the vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape pod. The insurgents discovered they could get the cruiser's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it. Bandwin Cor volunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards the cruisers. The shuttle was reported to Velgor, but the SKREE fighters dealt with it before Velgor could make any decisions. Velgor's fleet soon destroyed another cruiser, sending it into a fireball. The Corsair Wing then arrived at the system, and Dax Karrick was told to attempt the same thing by Solen. Velgor was notified of the Corsair ' s arrival and quickly went to his SKREE advanced. Karrick flew the Corsair through the fleet and took on Skrees before flying towards General Jan Dodonna 's Republic . Velgor then engaged the Corsair and hit the ship's stabilizer, sending it spiraling. Karrick set it to manual, and Velgor prepared to fire on the Corsair when it got back on course.

Velgor fired missiles at the Corsair as Karrick attempted to land on Commander Lajaie 's cruiser . Velgor was to synth flux Lajaie's crew to abandon ship and ordered the Sovereign to destroy the escape pods. He continued to pursue the Corsair until his SKREE was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field. Velgor was able to resume his attack on the insurgent fleet's starfighters. Velgor then ordered the Imperial fleet to close in on the Insurgent fleet. After being informed that Triova was returning to the Sovereign , Velgor returned to the ship where he intended to meet her.
