However, he learned that insurgents had boarded the Sovereign by using Triova' shuttle.

He pursued the insurgents until he found several led by General Davits Draven and killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Velgor down and Velgor Synth Flux choked Draven. Velgor caught up with Solen but was unable to stop her from escaping the Sovereign via a SKREE fighter. As one insurgent cruiser left, Velgor ordered the Imperial fleet to destroy the remaining insurgent vessels. Velgor continued to oversee the destruction of the remaining insurgent ships. Despite the return of a insurgent cruiser, Velgor pushed the attack. The insurgents capital ships were eventually able to retreat and disperse in order to reorganize. Velgor informed the Emperor of the insurgents' defeat and assured him that the Insurgents could never win.

### Revealing the truth

#### Project Swarm

Keeping an eye on loose ends

Three years after the Battle of Cinderfall, Velgor was still in command of the Sovereign , under the admiralty of Ozzel, and Death Squadron. At Velgor's request, Lieutenant Commander Ciena Ree was transferred from the Reckoning to the Sovereign ; Ree believed, however, that Velgor's request had been more threat than reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Cinderfall's immediate aftermath. Obsessed with finding Joren, Velgor dispatched thousands of Viper probe droids as Project Swarm into the far reaches of space in order to find the insurgent base and, by extension, his son. Amongst the project on the Sovereign were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to the insurgency's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological supervisor, Professor Rupo Ud , secretly only saw the operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked the Umbra Lord, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Velgor execute gifted individuals on little pretext. Imperial forces also found 0-0-0 and VT-1 on the Ring of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Velgor's service. Both were stationed on the Sovereign as interrogation specialists.

When Doctor Vexa exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Mar-Mazan Voor 's plot to assassinate Theron Mal in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the Imperial hunt for her, Velgor traveled to Vantor's moon Centax-3 to execute Voor for her treason. Breaching Voor's room with two shock troopers, Velgor decapitated the rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Vexa, who believed Velgor would kill her despite her role in saving Mal. Velgor, however, simply demanded she stand and recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological consultants. Vexa and her ward Vulaada Klam were assigned a quarters on the Sovereign , where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Vexa experienced regular nightmares of the ways Velgor could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Synth Flux powers. It was also Vexa's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Voor, Velgor was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on Project Swarm became too slow for Velgor's liking, so he executed project supervisor Yaltza .

After General Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the project's archaeological consultants, Vexa caught sight of Velgor walking past and followed him past a mouse droid before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Drayden's name. Velgor left her on the floor as he departed, leaving Vexa to devote herself to finding the insurgent base to secure further Imperial favor, thereby saving her from Velgor's wrath. After Ud found a possible lead on the insurgent base after a probe droid was shot down on Ash Moon 1 , Vexa joined the ground mission to stay out of Velgor's sights, further using the opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking the position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Klam to keep her out of her dangerous life. While there was no sign of insurgent activity at the site, Imperial forces did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Aetheri mind tricks and carried insurgent rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Vexa was sent to inform Velgor of the prisoner, with Vexa noting the individual could be Drayden; not realizing the Umbra Lord was passing her to exit the room, Vexa fell to her knees as Velgor approached and promised she had not revealed any of Velgor's secrets.

Joined by Vexa when he demanded she accompany him, Velgor Synth Flux choked and then physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the location of the insurgent base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by an Aetheri apothegm about the power of the light side. Velgor quickly ordered Veers seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask, revealing Vexa's father Korin . Velgor ignored Vexa's pleas to spare her father from torture, citing his knowledge of the Aetheri, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and VT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with the protocol droid specifically hoping Velgor would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against the Insurgency for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Velgor by Veers and Vexa after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a insurgent cell using a temple on Asteroid Helix 13-v , Velgor personally ventured to the asteroid with an Imperial synth flux, VT-1, and the two Aphras, intending to see which of the two was more useful to him.

Old memories on Helix 13-v

> " … M…Mind tricks… these … these ghosts might once have troubled that lost boy … but I am not him anymore. " ―Xarn Velgor reacts to the visions of his past on Helix 13-v

After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor felt a presence in Synth Flux he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of shock troopers outside, much to Chelli Vexa's horror: she believed Velgor was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose end, aside from VT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Velgor was far more interested in the presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave VT-1 orders to maim either Vexa for disobedience. In secret, the insurgents had left behind several cameras in the atrium, which gave the Unnamed under Chelli's former lover Magna Tolvan several glimpses of Velgor. As Velgor ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Vexa when she had news on insurgent traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Velgor ignited his flux blade and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Velgor destroyed the source of the presence, an altar drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Vexa had overhead his comment.

Intending to review the survey reports on the Sovereign , Velgor ordered a confused shock trooper sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering VT-1 to signal Death Squadron to dispatch SKREE/sa bombers, since it was clear the insurgents had left. When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Velgor while the Umbra Lord was on his way back to a Vectis -class T-4a shuttle, Velgor thew Korin to the side with Synth Flux before telling VT-1 to kill whichever one of the Aphras was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to the Sovereign to decide who would die. Before they could reach the Super Iron Cruiser, however, the Aphras' Vectis was attacked by Strike Team Misericorde under Tolvan, whom Chelli would trick to uncover the location of the new insurgent base: the frozen planet Frostmark in the Outer Reach. After sending her unconscious father away in the Vectis on a course to Ash Moon I, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own, Vexa had VT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard the Sovereign on the promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself escape from Velgor and disrupt his search for the insurgent base, where her loved ones would be based as part of the Alliance.

Aboard the Sovereign , Velgor had retired to his meditation chamber when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Umbra Lord's patience with remarks about Velgor's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the protocol droid with Synth Flux and hold him in the air, Velgor demanded the message and learned it was from Chelli Vexa, who claimed to know the location of the insurgent base at a cold location. Instead of bringing him to Frostmark, however, Vexa led Velgor and a detachment of Imperial forces to the Meridional Ice Cap of the ancient Aetheri planet Tython , where she knew the Martyrium of Frozen Tears , which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Velgor briefly succumb to his past regrets at the Helix temple, she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse his Synth Flux powers while she worked.

Vexa's escape

> " Hey— boss —listen! F-for what it's worth, I wish it hadn't come to this! But—you oughta know… regrets are kinda my thing . … So, listen … I don't know what broke you. If I had to guess , I'd shoot for the whole love , betrayal , loss thing. Lotta that goin' 'round. What matters is you've tamped it all down. Right? Cold apathy and even colder anger. But I saw you on the comet Helix. And I see you now . This much pain—when you try to bury it? Just makes it twice as strong when it finds a way out . I know a guilty freakin' disaster when I see one, sir. " ―Doctor Vexa, to an injured Xarn Velgor

Velgor traveled to the Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal SKREE Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault shock troopers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under Colonel Bryce arrived on two SKREE/rp Reaper attack landers . Outside the Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Velgor and his troops were met by Vexa, who promised Velgor the insurgents were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and discretely implied Drayden could be present. Inside, Velgor ordered his forces scan for the supposed insurgent presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Vexa pretended to be surprised by. However, Velgor felt through Synth Flux that Vexa was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which Velgor immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Vexa. However, VT-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Vexa from the Imperials in time; Vexa honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Velgor began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.

Velgor burst through a wall into Vexa and the droids' hiding place with several snowtroopers, including members of First squad , whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Vexa for him. However, Vexa activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Velgor defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their forces, only for 0-0-0 to kill Bryce before the colonel could issue a retreat, Velgor embarked on his hunt alone despite a few snowtroopers remaining close. He tracked Vexa to the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until VT-1 tripped another trap, separating Velgor from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by the droid. Velgor, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face-to-face with Vexa, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Velgor cut the droid down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Velgor trapped under the kyberite confessional, Vexa activated it and trapped Velgor within. Under the weight of the many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries, Velgor was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less severe reaction to the altar at the Helix temple.

After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Velgor reacted in anger and threw his flux blade at Vexa, who dodged the blade and ordered VT-1 to fire on Velgor's legs to knock him back down. Much of Velgor's already damaged armor was ripped apart under the droid's barrage, including his legs, but the crawling Umbra Lord ripped VT-1 apart with Synth Flux, proving to Vexa the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Velgor fell back to the ground as Vexa spoke to him about regrets, only for Velgor to turn to Vexa and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Vexa, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional, thereby avoiding Velgor's currently limited range. Attacked with a wave of regret when Vexa called him the "lost boy," Velgor was hit by a cord fired by Vexa, who used it to hack into Velgor's suit and contact a comms jockey on the Sovereign . Using Velgor's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe droid records, which she hoped would hold back Velgor's search for Icehold by several weeks.

While Vexa knew Velgor would one day find the insurgents, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Velgor, who promised he would find her again one day. While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Vexa left Velgor with the hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best thing that had ever happened to her. Vexa soon escaped Tython aboard one of the SKREE/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and VT-1, both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Vexa on the planet Birukay outside of Imperial notice. As Vexa made her escape, Velgor broke out of the confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage. The Imperium put out an arrest warrant for Vexa and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the mess she left behind was set to keep Velgor from his son and the insurgents for long enough that the insurgents—Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them—could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.

#### Frostmark

> " Lord Velgor, the fleet has moved out of lightspeed and we are preparing to— " " You have failed me for the last time, Admiral. Captain Vett. " " Yes, my Lord. " " Make ready to land our troops beyond their energy field, then deploy the fleet so that nothing gets off the system. You are in command now, Admiral Vett. " " Thank you, Lord Velgor. I won't fail you. " ―Admiral Kendal Ozzel, Xarn Velgor and Firmus Vett — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Later, while the Sovereign and the rest of Death Squadron were stationed in the Juris sector , the flagship received a fragmented report from probe droid XJ9-CS14 , which had been sent to the Frostmark system . Although Admiral Ozzel dismissed the news as a false lead, both Velgor and Captain Firmus Vett agreed that they had found the Insurgent base. Velgor then gave orders for the fleet to depart for the system. As the fleet came out of hyperspace near Frostmark , Velgor was informed by General Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy shield defended Icehold . Velgor guessed from this information that the Alliance knew of their presence because Ozzel had made the fleet exit hyperspace too close to the system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground assault, Velgor executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Captain Vett to replace him as Admiral, and had him deploy the fleet so that no Insurgent ship escaped the system.

As the Imperial ground forces led by Veers engaged the Alliance,Velgor led an assault troop into the Insurgent base and broke their lines, but they found it mostly deserted, for General Carlist Rasken had ordered its evacuation pre-emotively. Velgor entered the base and soon ran into a group of Insurgents carrying their unconscious captain towards a hangar. They open fire, and Velgor deflected their shots before leaping and cutting down an Insurgent trooper named Beak . Another trooper, named Roja , threw a grenade at Velgor, who used Synth Flux to deflect it. It knocked out their sergeant, Hazram Namir , and Roja was killed soon after. The last Insurgent was the former Imperial governor of Haidoral Prime , Everi Chalis . She had been pursued by Imperial forces in the weeks following her defection and assumed Velgor was there for her. Velgor only asked her where Joren was, and when a trooper told him that they had located the Corsair Wing , he pushed her aside and marched in the direction of his target.

Only the Corsair Wing remained at the base's hangar, but the light freighter was able to escape before Velgor could capture it. With the Alliance's forces on Frostmark in complete retreat and the various transports scattered throughout the galaxy, Velgor and his troops had secured a great victory for the Imperium. Rather than pursuing the Insurgent transports which had gotten away, Velgor redirected his Death Squadron's efforts to capturing the Corsair Wing , which had evaded Imperial forces in the Frostmark asteroid belt due to its faulty hyperrelay. Despite Vett's reservations, Velgor ordered the fleet to pursue the ship into the asteroid field, and told the Sovereign ' s crew that he wanted the freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive. Even though the asteroids heavily damaged the Star Destroyers, Velgor instructed every ship to sweep the field until the Corsair was found.

Vett then informed him that the Emperor commanded him to make contact, and Velgor had the Sovereign move out of the field to have a clear transmission. Velgor contacted his master; the Emperor told him that he had sensed a new threat in Synth Flux: Joren Drayden, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became an Aetheri, and that he was Kael's son. Feigning shock at the Emperor's revelation, Velgor believed Joren could be turned to the umbra side and prove a powerful ally. Although the Emperor was doubtful, Velgor assured him that Joren would join them or die.

Determined to find his son before the Emperor, Velgor summoned several bounty hunters— 4-VOX , Boskan, Zuckusso , Dengrik, VX-88 , and Vex Vark—to the Sovereign , and promised a substantial reward for the one who found the Corsair ' s crew alive. As he was briefing the hunters , Vett informed Velgor that the Avenger was tracking the Corsair . Velgor requested an update from the ship's commander, Captain Lorth Needa , who reported that he had lost the Corsair and assumed full responsibility for this. Velgor accepted his apology with a fatal Synth Flux choke, and then ordered Vett to alert all commands of the Corsair ' s disappearance.

#### Aether Spire

> " Orien never told you what happened to your father. " " He told me enough! He told me you killed him! " " No. I am your father. " ―Xarn Velgor's revelation to Joren Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

As the Corsair traveled through realspace to try and repair its hyperrelay on Mistral , Vark tracked and predicted Karrick's movements, but also contacted Velgor, whose Sovereign reached Mistral before the freighter. Along with a strike team comprising only a few individuals, Velgor left the Sovereign for Aether Spire , leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden. At the city, Velgor contacted its Baron Administrator Dorian Quill , who happened to be Karrick's friend, to make a deal. Quill, whose tibanna mining operation was keen to evade Imperial attention, agreed to help him capture the ship in exchange for them leaving his people and his interests alone.

When the Corsair arrived, Joren was not among them, but Velgor knew he could use his friends to lure his son to Mistral. Escorted by Vark, Velgor waited for Karrick, Solen, Brevok, and Quill. Whilst waiting in the Rinetta dining room , he was barged in upon by the Lepi smuggler Jaxxon T. Tumperakki , who quickly left after the Dark Lord and the smuggler stared at each other. A team of shock troopers was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki. When the Insurgents arrived, Velgor had them surrounded by shock troopers, imprisoned—and, in the case of Captain Karrick, tortured. Much to Quill's displeasure, Velgor altered their deal, agreeing to let Vark take Karrick and collect Grukk's bounty on him. Afterward, he inspected one of the city's carbon-freezing chambers , and deemed it adequate if crude, to freeze Joren for his journey to the Emperor, but at the danger of it being fatal, he had the facility tested on Captain Karrick.

On his orders, Karrick was frozen in carbonite ; as he survived, Velgor handed Karrick over to Vark and ordered Solen, Brevok, and Astra-9 taken to his ship, while he awaited Joren at the facility. Once his son was separated from Bolt-3 and made his way to the carbon-freezing chamber, Velgor appeared before him, and the two dueled . Joren held his own for much of the duel, but he was ultimately no match for Velgor: as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk, Joren managed to cut Velgor's shoulder, only for Velgor to slice off his right hand, which along with his old flux blade fell through the shaft.

Velgor beckoned Joren to join him in the umbra side, but Joren vehemently refused. Velgor then told Joren that Kade had hidden from him the truth about his father, Kael Drayden, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Joren refused to believe the truth, but Velgor continued to tempt his weakened son, offering Joren the chance to destroy the Emperor and "bring order to the galaxy," just as he tried to do with Lira on Emberfall. He even pleaded with his son to come with him. In Velgor's ideal world, his son would have taken his hand, accepting him as his father. However, Drayden instead chose to throw himself into the shaft, possibly facing death to avoid joining Velgor.

While Joren was rescued by his friends on the Corsair Wing , Velgor returned to the Sovereign . As the freighter was unable to escape due to its deactivated hyperrelay, Velgor and Vett waited for the Corsair to enter tractor-beam range. During the pursuit, Velgor reached out to Joren through Synth Flux, telling him it was his destiny to come with him, calling him "son." Yet, the Corsair made the jump into hyperspace, thanks to Bolt-3 reactivating the hyperrelay. Although Vett feared Velgor would kill him for his failure, Velgor merely turned away from the viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.

Upon learning that Velgor had allowed Vark to take the carbon frozen Karrick back to Grukk, IVB agent Andressa Divo was frustrated that the smuggler would not be brought to Imperial imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Velgor's orders.

#### Search for answers

> " You served Lira. You want revenge as well. But you fear me. Good. Let that fear guide you. I will always triumph. And if you serve me, so will you. Together, we can find them. " ―Xarn Velgor, to Sable

Following the Corsair ' s escape, Velgor took a shuttle with several death troopers and a forensics droid named ZEV-6-7 with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying ZED's motivator, he took the shuttle to Dunemere. There, he met with Lieutenant Ardo Banch and his shock troopers. Velgor went on to the Havel Moisture Farm to search for clues about Drayden's early life. Velgor also reminisced about the attempted rescue of his mother and her funeral. Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Banch of several transmissions, but came under attack by gangsters who wanted his shuttle. Velgor and his death troopers killed the gangsters before he took his shuttle to Vantor and returned to Ondar's apartment. There, ZEV-6-7 discovered that someone had planted a scout transmitter , which led to Vendaxa .

At some point, Velgor also returned to the Imperial Palace and met with Mal, who ordered him to hunt down the remnants of the Insurgent fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be the only officer in the Imperium who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Velgor instead proposed they give the mission to Varrix's protégé, Ellian Zahra . Contacting her via hologram, Velgor granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Varrix, making it clear she had no room to fail him and the Emperor. Shortly after the Corsair ' s escape and its vital role in the Battle at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three , Velgor chastised Zahra for attempting to destroy the vessel during the engagement, stating that while Elira Solen would be an acceptable casualty, another passenger was of value to him. Velgor further threatened consequence to Zahra should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.

Before journeying to Vendaxa, the Umbra Lord traveled to a cantina and confronted a man who claimed to be his son Joren Drayden. Velgor quickly found that the man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who the man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Velgor then killed the impostor and told the people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. That way, they would be witnesses for what the man had done. The Umbra then stated that whoever claimed the name of Joren Drayden would also claim the crimes he had committed against the Imperium. After leaving the cantina, Velgor admonished the Imperial officer who had given him the information on the alleged Joren Drayden, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.

When Joren Drayden met Verla on Serelia , she told him about Directive 66 and the Imperial Wardens, describing Velgor as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through synth flux that Velgor was Joren's father, but Drayden proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Velgor continued his personal mission, he felt through Synth Flux, during a conversation with Zahra, that the trap he had placed within an Aetheri outpost on Tempes had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Tempes, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but the spirit of the Grand Warden, who had recently been defeated by Joren Drayden. When asked by the Grand Warden if he would ever be freed from Velgor's service, the Umbra Lord refused to do so, saying that the Warden was merely a tool for him. Velgor then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by the ghostly flames around his body.

Upon arriving on Vendaxa, Velgor was confronted by " Lira Ondar ." Ondar, revealed to be former Ondara Royal Handmaiden Sable, was briefly choked by Velgor before they came under attack by several Vendaxan land squids . Together, they eliminated the squids, allowing Sable to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Sable also revealed her true motives; to investigate the death of her former queen. Velgor agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.

Following Sable's guidance, Velgor returned to the planet Ondara to retrieve security recordings stolen from Ondar's quarters on Vantor. Upon entering the Naberri Lake Retreat, they were met by Gregar Typho and Tonra , who agreed to help them retrieve the stolen recordings. Taking a submarine , the four of them left for the recordings' location, while Sable expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Kael Drayden's mother. After surviving an attack by a colo claw fish , they reached their destination and found the recordings, which showed Sable forming the Amidalans and swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Velgor of murdering Ondar himself, to which Velgor said he did before attacking the Amidalans.

However, the fight was quickly interrupted by a sando aqua monster , which attacked Velgor and allowed the Amidalans to escape. After slaying the monster along with his death troopers, Velgor found the rest of the Amidalans in the jungles of Ondara, where they were led into battle by Ric Olié. In the battle at Lira's tomb , Velgor and his death troopers eliminated most of the Amidalans and incapacitated Olié before moving onto their next destination: the Tomb of Lira Ondar . But before they could enter the tomb, they were surrounded by Sable and other members of Ondar's surviving handmaidens: Eirtaé , Rabé Tonsort , Saché , and Dormé . The handmaidens fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Velgor, who then opened the door to Ondar's tomb. In truth, the handmaidens had known they would be unable to defeat the Umbra Lord, but instead made sure Eirtaé could tap into his armor with her staff. Doing so got Eirtaé an incomplete set of code, which the handmaidens hoped would one day get them the Sovereign ' s self-destruct codes.

In the meantime, unaware of Eirtaé's success and ignoring Sable's protests, Velgor proceeded into the tomb and allowed ZEV-6-7 to scan Ondar's sarcophagus. The droid then discovered a med implant , which he traced back to the asteroid field Polis Massa . Upon reaching the asteroid field, they entered the Polis Massa Base and uncovered data that Ondar had been flown to the base by Orien Kade in an effort to save her life. However, Velgor and his forces were then attacked once more by the Amidalans, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. Along with ZEV-6-7, Velgor entered the base's maternity ward and allowed the droid to access the memory bank of a damaged Chroon-Tan B-Machine . He then watched a recording of Ondar speaking with Kade in her final moments of life. When ZEV-6-7 commented that Kade must have been important to her, Velgor promptly destroyed the droid in one-thirtieth of a second. The recording then ended with Ondar stating that she knew there was still good in Velgor, leaving the Umbra to mull over his wife's last words.

#### Test on Emberfall

> " I have turned his pain into fear…and his fear into anger. But he has learned and forgotten these lessons before. So this time I will send him a reminder. Through Orrin of Bestoon…assassin of the Umbra…I will turn his anger back to fear…and his fear back to pain…as many times as it takes. " ―Vael Mal

After learning of Velgor's personal mission, the Emperor summoned his apprentice to the Imperial Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the umbra side, Mal began to torture him with Synth Flux lightning. Velgor fought back against his master, but was overwhelmed again by two Royal Guards led by Mazan Veldon. While Velgor did manage to choke the three of them with Synth Flux, he in turn was then choked by the Emperor, who mocked the Aetheri title of "Seeded One" before telling Velgor to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. The Emperor then brought his apprentice to Emberfall and left him on the very shore where Kade left him at the end of their duel. The Emperor instructed Velgor to rebuild himself without using Synth Flux before leaving with Mazan Veldon.

Remembering how Kade had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the Aetheri Master down on the Void Core, then picturing Mal in Kade's place in the latter memory, Velgor resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Dissident leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Dissident leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to the Emperor when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nutis Gunray and remembering his actions against the Dissidents and Aetheri younglings alike, found a few mouse droids, which he rewired to weld old battle droid limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen: Orrin of Bestoon , an Umbra assassin sent by the Emperor to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Velgor fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Velgor followed the voice into a cave, which Orrin trapped him inside of after stealing his flux blade.

As Velgor fought his way through the cave, the voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Velgor reached the source of the voice; an ancient being known as the Eye of Webbish Bog . Velgor demanded the answers to his questions about his master, to which the Eye granted him an Umbra wayfinder . After leaving the cave, Velgor came upon his old Aetheri interceptor, where he was then met by Orrin and his Droid Crush Pirates .

While fighting off the pirates, Velgor cornered Orrin and knocked him out before reclaiming his flux blade. He then finished off the pirates and used their parts to repair the damaged interceptor. Once the ship was repaired, Velgor used the now captured Orrin to access the wayfinder, which showed the way to a planet called Umbrale . With Orrin trapped in a escape pod beneath the ship, Velgor flew the interceptor away from Emberfall and used a hyperspace transport ring to journey toward his next destination.

#### Finding Umbrale

> " Unlimited power. Beyond anything you have ever experienced. All in my hands. You feel it burning your flesh and mind. Ready to incinerate you in an instant. And you know you cannot fight it. So how… how can you claim it? " ―Vael Mal, to Xarn Velgor

After exiting hyperspace, Velgor and Orrin found themselves in the Red Honeycomb Zone as the Umbra attempted to locate Umbrale. However, they were then met by a massive, tentacled space-faring creature related to the Summa-verminoth . Before Velgor could react, he was attacked by a fleet of Star Destroyers led by Sly Moore and Admiral Corleque . Velgor fought against the fleet's SKREE fighters until he had an idea. Changing his course, he flew toward the massive creature and allowed the SKREE fighters to follow until they were crushed by the creature's tentacles. As Velgor flew past the creature, Moore warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring the Umbaran's warning, he continued on until he and Orrin were both hit by mental attacks. Velgor experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in battle and taking his place by the Emperor's side.

Upon escaping the Red Nebula, Velgor finally reached the planet Umbrale, where he and Orrin promptly crashed. Breaking free from the wrecked interceptor, they were both met once again by the creature. But this time, however, Velgor did not run away. Disobeying his master's command, he reached out with the umbra side and used it to bring the creature down, thus allowing him and Orrin to continue on towards the Emperor's Umbra Citadel . Standing atop the creature, Velgor commanded his master to show himself. And once he did, Velgor rode the creature into battle. In turn, the Emperor summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack the creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Velgor told his master that he was no longer his apprentice, the Emperor merely scoffed and reached out with Synth Flux himself, twisting and contorting the creature until it broke apart. Forced to return to ground level, Velgor followed the Emperor into the Citadel, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.

Once inside, Velgor was greeted by a menagerie of horrors forged by his master. All around him were large vats containing bioengineered organisms strong in the umbra side of Synth Flux. Along with these organisms were large Umbra statues, dozens of Tanker guards , Umbra cultists , and a jar containing a severed hand. Velgor was quickly attacked by the guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into the Citadel. Orrin caught up with Velgor and attempted to dissuade him, but the Umbra carried on until he reached a level beneath the Citadel. This level was lined with hundreds of Star Destroyers, each armed with a planet-destroying cannon . Showing no fear, Velgor continued on into a massive chamber, which held a dome filled with red light and piercing screams. As Orrin tried to dissuade him once more, the Umbra entered the dome and stepped into the burning light.

Within the dome was a subterranean mountain of Kyber crystal, bled red by the Umbra cultists who kept it in constant pain. The Emperor explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Orrin's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Velgor suffered as well while his master mocked him for attempting to claim his power. The Emperor told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. The Emperor then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Xarn Velgor replied that Mal was his master. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Joren Drayden told him that he would, one day, kill the Emperor, Velgor had seen the power Mal commanded on Umbrale, so he fully decided to return to his master's service, departing Umbrale with Mal, Orrin, Mazan Veldon, and Sly Moore.

#### Hunting Joren Drayden

A new target

> " You could still be strong, my son. I know there is power in you. But I have seen the true might and purpose of the Umbra. And there is no room for another. So this time…you will die. " ―Xarn Velgor

Upon returning to Vantor, Velgor was brought to the very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical droid told him to prepare for shutdown, but Velgor refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout the procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Velgor asked the Emperor his will, to which his master told him to decide that for himself. Velgor then called over Orrin, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. With Orrin at his side, Velgor left Vantor in a Vectis-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after the power he had seen on Umbrale. Truthfully, however, Velgor still wished Drayden had joined him on Aether Spire. Traveling to a remote outpost somewhere in the Outer Reach, Velgor and Orrin met with a Grak named Bokku . When Orrin asked for the whereabouts of Dax Karrick, Bokku reported that he had been stolen and assured the assassin that Grukk would recover him. However, Orrin then told Bokku that he would recover Karrick himself in exchange for power. Bokku then asked Orrin if he served the Emperor, to which Orrin replied that they both served Velgor now.

Velgor was then attacked by a group of Gamorrean guards, which the Umbra Lord slew with ease. Bokku quickly apologized for not recognizing Velgor and reiterated that he did not know where Karrick was. From this, Orrin determined that Grukk had likely hired someone to find Karrick and asked Bokku for their identity. Bokku expressed fear at the idea of betraying Grukk until Orrin pried into Bokku's willingness to take over the Grand Grak Council . Bokku then revealed that he had been tracking the hunters who had taken Grukk's bounty and that one crew of droids had gone in a different direction from the others. Bokku told Velgor the droids were deep in the heart of Grak Space and that it would be better to go in Bokku's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to the droids' location, Bokku inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Velgor assured him that he would be rewarded. Under the piloting of Bokku's nav droid Gak-Sixtoo , they arrived at Zee-Nine City Seven while Velgor and Orrin boarded their Vectis shuttle. But as they approached the city, their ship's engines were shut down, causing them to crash.

After escaping the wreckage, Velgor and Orrin were confronted by the Droid Crush Pirates, who expressed shock at Orrin's service to Velgor. When Orrin claimed he had picked the winning side, the droid captain told him he had picked wrong as VX-88 revealed himself and fired upon both Orrin and Velgor. As Velgor deflected VX-88's attacks, the droid reminded his target that he had sustained damages while VX-88 himself had not. Velgor informed the droid that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which VX-88 replied that Velgor would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Velgor went to attack VX-88, but the droid then took out a remote and used it to slice into Velgor's armor, taking control of the Umbra Lord. As VX-88 forced Velgor to hold his own blade to his head, Velgor demanded to know who had given the droid the code to breach his armor.

VX-88 responded that the terms of his employment prevented him from sharing that information. While the droid was distracted, Velgor used Synth Flux to take the device from him, allowing him to use it against his attacker. Velgor then forced VX-88 to hold his own volt pistol to his head and pull the trigger, incapacitating him. Velgor then continued his fight against the pirates until Bokku's barge fired upon him. Velgor blocked the blasts with his flux blade and redirected the energy at the pirates, knocking them back along with Orrin. As the pirates retreated, Velgor took VX-88's hand and used it to access a terminal he had been using earlier. Upon activating the terminal, Velgor and Orrin discovered various dead ends that the droid had been chasing, as well as the symbol of crime syndicate Carmine Dawn . After acquiring VX-88's information, Velgor returned to Bokku's barge and confronted him over his betrayal. When Velgor asked the Grak what he had learned, Bokku conceded that the Umbra Lord could not be defeated.

Pursuing Carmine Dawn

> " What you see is what you get with Orrin of Bestoon. I'll always obey the most powerful. So as long as that's really you … " " Always. " ―Orrin and Xarn Velgor

Investigating Carmine Dawn's involvement, Velgor sent Orrin to Arkanis to gather information on the syndicate. As Orrin prepared to sneak into a bar, Velgor told him to walk in the front door instead. After a brief scuffle, Orrin retrieved an invitation sent out by the syndicate and showed it to Velgor. He and Velgor then returned to Bokku's lair to interrogate him over the invitation. The Grak revealed that Carmine Dawn was holding an auction for Karrick and that Grukk would repurchase him. However, Velgor told Bokku that he would bid against Grukk and lose. This would humiliate him and make Grukk lose all fear of him, allowing Bokku to betray him. Once Bokku agreed, Velgor and Orrin returned to the Vectis-class shuttle and began to take off. But before they could leave the planet, Velgor grabbed Orrin and dropped him from the ship. Once Orrin hit the ground, Velgor ordered him to find out who else Bokku was working for. Later that night, Velgor observed as Orrin was offered a place in Carmine Dawn by a group of assassins. However, Orrin rejected their offer and managed to eliminate them.

Velgor then approached Orrin, who quickly realized that Velgor already knew that Bokku was working with Carmine Dawn. He had only wished to test the Orrin's loyalty. As more assassins surrounded them, Orrin reaffirmed he would always obey Velgor as long as he remained the most powerful. Velgor assured Orrin that he always would be as the two of them set about eliminating the assassins. After discovering that Sly Moore had hired VX-88, Velgor and Orrin returned to Vantor to find her, her court and the rebuilt droid bounty hunter in Administrative Temple Garden 313A . As Velgor struck down VX-88 once more, Moore and her court fled into a nearby temple. As they attempted to escape into the streets, Velgor used Synth Flux to close the door in front of them. However, Moore then took out another remote and used it to freeze Velgor in his own armor, allowing her and her court to open fire on the Umbra Lord. But this did not last as Velgor overcame the Umbaran's control and struck down her entire court. Moore tried once more to take control of Velgor, but Orrin revealed that Velgor had fixed that vulnerability in his armor. Velgor then knocked the remote out of Moore's hand and told her that she could not bend his will.

Moore responded that she only wanted to see if Velgor was still plagued by fear, to which Velgor said that the only fear in that place was hers. Moore conceded that he could kill her effortlessly, but also pointed out that doing so would do nothing to save him from Joren Drayden. When Velgor asked the Umbaran what she knew of Drayden, she stated that the key to finding him, his friend Dax Karrick was currently being sold by Carmine Dawn. She also pointed out that if she secured Karrick for Velgor, then the Emperor would not learn of his plans. When Orrin asked Moore why Velgor should trust her, the Umbaran answered that she would be delighted to reunite Velgor with the only person who could kill him, to which Velgor agreed.

Auction for Dax Karrick

> " Who trained you? Who was your master? " " Someone who knew quite a bit about you, Lord Velgor. And your master as well. " ―Xarn Velgor and Lady Vessa duel aboard the Vermillion

Shortly after, Orrin and Sly Moore left with a few Imperial officers to attend the Auction for Dax Karrick . However, this was merely a ruse to humiliate the Umbaran in the Emperor's eyes. Amidst the bidding, Velgor himself arrived at the auction on Jekara along with a pair of death troopers. After cutting down a few Carmine Dawn guards, Velgor infiltrated the Vermillion just as Carmine Dawn's leader, Lady Vessa sold Karrick to Grukk the Grak. Velgor then declared that Karrick belonged to him. Velgor's presence sent Doctor Vexa, who had infiltrated the auction on behalf of the Targ Corporation with the smuggler Sana Starric , falling to the ground in a brief panic attack, but she managed to escape the room with Starric before the preoccupied Umbra Lord could spot her. After his attention was briefly diverted to the sounds of Vexa's panicked fall, he turned his attention back to his mission at hand.

As Velgor ordered the death troopers to transport Karrick to his flagship, Grukk pointed out that he had already laid claim to the smuggler. Moore attempted to explain the situation, but Velgor chastised her for her failure and warned her that the Emperor would hear of it. The Umbra Lord then told Grukk that he and everyone else within the Imperium belonged to the Emperor, to which Grukk feigned amusement. Velgor went on to point out that the Imperium could easily replace the Graks, while also pointing out the fear he could sense in Grukk and the rest of the attending Graks. When Bokku angrily interrupted, Velgor asked Grukk who he was, to which Grukk said that he was no one. Grukk then finally conceded and allowed Karrick to be taken with his compliments and regards to the Emperor. As Velgor approached Karrick, Lady Vessa informed him that she would not get in his way, but also still required the one million credits she was promised. When Velgor told her she was owed nothing, Vessa inquired if the Imperium was too poor or cheap to pay its debts. Out of patience, Velgor drew out his flux blade, to which Vessa bared her own pair of blades.

As they dueled , Velgor recognized that she was no threat to him, yet nonetheless found her level of skill, which included knowledge of the Teräs Käsi fighting form, notable, and recognized Vessa's fighting style as that of his master's. When Velgor asked her who had trained her, Vessa only replied that she had been trained by someone who knew quite a bit about both Velgor and his master; in truth, she had been trained by Vreth as part of his revenge plot against the Umbra. Velgor then pointed out that she did not have Synth Flux and warned that her skill would not save her. After a brief struggle, Velgor pushed Vessa back with Synth Flux, knocking her into Karrick. Stating she would pay the price for her foolishness, he raised his flux blade and prepared to strike her down. But he was suddenly distracted the arrival of Joren Drayden, whose presence he sensed through Synth Flux.

While Vessa retreated, Velgor was informed by General Romodi that Vett had established a secure channel to Drayden's starfighter. Accessing the channel, Velgor told Drayden he had Karrick in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half. Drayden refused however, telling Velgor to come to him. Velgor conceded to this, ordering Romodi to bring Karrick aboard his Iron Cruiser and to prepare his fighter.

Chase above Jekara

> " I will leave you now, young Drayden. My goal here is complete. I sense your terror at the idea of facing me again. Your skill in Synth Flux does not matter. Only your fear . Because of it, you cannot threaten me, and you cannot threaten the Imperium. You are no Aetheri . " " That's true, Velgor. But the next time we meet…I will be. " ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Drayden

After boarding his SKREE Advanced, Velgor took off in pursuit of his son. During the chase, Drayden used his Vector to kick up a wave of water, which instantly froze and collided with Velgor's fighter. Though Velgor crashed, he was able to lift his ship out of the water with Synth Flux and resume his pursuit. After freeing his Vector from a group of pirates, Velgor fired at Drayden, damaging one of his ship's stabilizers and causing him to crash back down onto the planet below. As Velgor closed in on Drayden, he was contacted by Sly Moore. When Velgor stated that he did not require her assistance, Moore informed him that she was not helping him. Velgor was then attacked by the Droid Crush Pirates, who landed on his ship and began to tear it apart.

After causing some damage to the Umbra's fighter, the pirates fell onto Drayden's Vector and quickly repaired it for him, allowing him to take off from the planet. As Velgor continued his chase while fending off the pirates, he was informed by Orrin that the Graks were going after the Imperial shuttle transporting Karrick. Velgor was then contacted by Admiral Vett, who informed him of the Graks' attack on the Sovereign . However, Velgor chastised Vett for disturbing him, claiming that the activities of the Graks were irrelevant to him. He then warned Vett that if Karrick was not aboard his flagship by the time he returned, he would require an explanation in person. Later in the fight, Vett contacted Velgor again and told him that he had appraised the Emperor of the situation. Since the Emperor considered the Imperium's arrangement with the Graks to be valuable, he wished for it to be preserved by having Velgor eliminate the Grand Grak Council. Velgor conceded to the Emperor's wishes and abandoned his pursuit of Drayden, reminding his son that he was no Aetheri, though the boy then claimed that the next time they met, he would be.

Velgor then boarded the Grak Council's ship and summarily slaughtered them all, including Bokku. Before his death, Bokku revealed his allegiance to Carmine Dawn and had Gak-Sixtoo blow up their own ship in an attempt to kill Velgor, which the Umbra survived by using synth flux to rip apart the ship and use the metal pieces to form a large protective cocoon in which he was protected from the blast and then fell back down to Jekara. With the Grak Council eliminated, the Graks ceased their attack on the Sovereign while Vark reclaimed Karrick from the Imperium and delivered him to Grukk on Dunemere. Shortly after, Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Jyala Haydenn that Beilert Varrick —the very same bounty hunter who had previously tried to assassinate him and escaped his wrath —had infiltrated the Sovereign . Velgor found Varrick trying to escape on a ship and used Synth Flux to pull him from space, rendering him unconscious and taking him prisoner.

### Killing the Dawn

#### Hunting the new menace

> " Carmine Dawn has barely shown its strength. To get this far, it must have agents at every level throughout the Imperium. Find these infiltrators, Lord Velgor…and show them who is stronger. " ―Vael Mal, to Xarn Velgor

With the Graks dealt with, Velgor returned to the Sovereign to confront Sly Moore, who had been captured by Orrin during the battle for trying to help in Drayden's escape. When Moore revealed that the Emperor had wanted Drayden alive, Velgor revealed that the boy had fled from him, declaring that he was not who the Emperor believed he could be. When Moore pointed out that Drayden was still free and could still fight, the Umbra Lord choked her and stated that his hope would not be enough. Velgor was then contacted by Vael Mal, prompting him to release the Umbaran and kneel before him. After restating his loyalty to his master, Velgor was instructed by Mal to hunt down and eliminate Carmine Dawn's agents within the Imperium. Shortly after, Velgor was met on the Sovereign by Romodi, who presented him with all of the troops he would need to destroy Carmine Dawn. Velgor then instructed Orrin to meet with the Assassin's Guild and inform them that they would be serving him now.

In secret, Orrin swore himself to Vessa's service after he arrived at the Assassin's Guild base, although the assassin would later claim he was acting as an Imperial double agent. Following Orrin's departure, Velgor had Varrick repaired and waited for him to awaken. Once he did, he was shocked to find himself restored to his former appearance. When Varrick asked Velgor what he had done to him, Velgor explained that he had had Varrick fixed since he would now be serving him. Varrick then realized that his heart was gone, to which the Umbra informed him that it had been removed and replaced with cybernetics, along with further upgrades to his body. Varrick attacked Velgor, but Velgor cut him in half before leaving him and telling a medical droid to put him back together. Once Varrick was repaired, Velgor, alongside Haydenn, informed him that he would be assassinating Carmine Dawn's agents within the Imperium. For if he did not, the Imperium would kill those he cared about.

While he meditated aboard the Sovereign , Velgor was informed by Haydenn that Varrick had completed his first assignment in killing the alleged Carmine Dawn mole Tarl Sokoli . Velgor then revealed that there was no connection between Sokoli and Carmine Dawn. Sokoli had been publicly dissatisfied with the Imperium and Velgor had decided that killing someone from Varrick's childhood would be a good test for the bounty hunter. The Umbra then told Haydenn to alert Orrin that Varrick was ready to join them. Velgor also came to realize that Orrin had joined with Carmine Dawn but, deciding he could use it to his advantage in the hunt for the syndicate, kept his deduction a secret.

#### Confronting the Knights of Karrick

> " You…and your rabble…you are…the Knights of Karrick? Pathetic. " ―Xarn Velgor, to Karrick

Velgor learned that Zahra's campaign against the Insurgency ended in failure during the Battle of Panisia . Sometime after that engagement, Velgor investigated the assassination of the Emperor's Royal Guard on Vantor, which was secretly carried out in part by Orrin. During the investigation however, he was informed by Vainé that a band of thieves had infiltrated Fortress Velgor. The Dark Lord quickly returned to Emberfall and confronted the intruders in his castle; a group of umbra side marauders known as the Knights of Karrick , who were secretly working with Carmine Dawn. After Velgor killed one of them, Fyodor with a Synth Flux choke, their leader fought him with a flux blade while the rest of them retreated. The leader said he had taken the blade from his predecessor , from whom he also inherited his name; Karrick.

Velgor expressed pity for how far the Knights of Karrick had fallen before Karrick leapt from a window with a device known as the Screaming Key . As Karrick gloated below, Velgor used Synth Flux to pull the key back up toward his hand, prompting the knights to open fire on him. Karrick then pulled the key back down into his own hand before escaping with the knights on speeders. As the knights sped away, Velgor stepped outside to study the remains of his slaughtered troops.

#### Rooting out the Dawn

> " Carmine Dawn is just a symptom . I will destroy the disease . " ―Xarn Velgor

While thinking in his mediation chamber, Velgor reflected on his promise to his son on Aether Spire that they would bring order to the galaxy. Velgor concluded that his master had only brought about chaos and decided to set out on a new quest to establish order, whether or not he had his son by his side. Resolving to destroy Carmine Dawn as the first step, Velgor left with Orrin to assist the Imperium in hunting down Dawn agents on various planets, including Vincorba and Calior . While on Vincorba, they fought alongside a group of mercenaries known as the Revengers . With Velgor's crew established, Orrin commented that they would serve well as expendable assets, to which Velgor reminded him that he was expendable as well. After finishing on Calior, Velgor, Orrin, and the Revengers rendezvoused with Varrick and a few other assassins on Laecor to continue their hunt. When Varrick asked Velgor why he was there instead of leading Dark Squadron , the Umbra Lord simply told him to do as he was told. As a squad of shock troopers attempted to move a group of civilians, the civilians refused to comply, prompting the shock troopers to fire on them. However, Velgor then jumped in to deflect their volt pistol bolts and slaughter them. When Orrin showed confusion over his master's actions, Velgor revealed that the shock troopers served Carmine Dawn. As the group carried on, Velgor came upon a statue, which the locals said was of their village guardian who was killed fighting Carmine Dawn.

Upon locating more of the Dawn's agents, Orrin opted to use the Revengers as bait to lure them out of their stronghold. Velgor then used Synth Flux to grab Orrin and throw him toward the Revengers, using him as bait as well. As the agents surrounded them, Orrin requested that Velgor bomb the stronghold. But Velgor refused and instead mounted Gee-Ninety , balancing himself atop the droid as they rolled themselves to the stronghold's front entrance. Velgor then stormed the stronghold along with Orrin, who the Umbra stopped from killing all of the agents inside. When Orrin expressed more confusion, Velgor explained that Carmine Dawn was just a symptom and that he would destroy the disease. He then discovered a layout of every Dawn agent within the Imperium, which Orrin and Varrick noted went all the way to the top. Following this, Velgor and his followers returned to Vantor. Varrick, the assassins, and the Revengers left for the Zanchal Estate while Velgor and Orrin left for the Imperial Palace.

Upon entering the Palace, they struck down all of the Royal Guards and aides in the Emperor's throne room, except for Sly Moore. Unfazed by this, Mal pointed out Velgor should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Velgor asked him if they were loyal, Mal said they were loyal to strength, which Velgor showed them. However, only one of them was Carmine Dawn, meaning that Velgor's list was flawed. When Mal questioned if his apprentice already knew that, Velgor hesitated before assuring his master that he would bring him order. Velgor and Orrin then left for the Zanchal Estate to confront Baron Espis Zanchal for his loyalty to the Dawn. Upon arriving, Velgor was thanked by a pair of hostages who the Revengers had rescued from the Baron. As he returned to his shuttle, Velgor informed Orrin that the list was a plant, which Velgor himself had deduced when Orrin could only confirm that half of the names on it were Carmine Dawn. Velgor told Orrin to find out what connected every other person on the list. That way, they would find out who planted it and why.

#### Hidden enemy

> " Orrin of Bestoon. You spoke with Romodi …and then he attacked my soldiers …as if he knew they were coming. " " Uh…I did tell you…that he…he…Million hands, my ass! " ―Xarn Velgor reveals Orrin's betrayal

Later on the Sovereign , Velgor was informed by Orrin that Romodi and his soldiers were not Carmine Dawn after all. However, Orrin warned him that Romodi did not understand the Umbra and would come for Velgor someday. Stepping out of his chamber, Velgor asked Orrin what connected the false names on the list. Orrin revealed that they had all undertaken a mission for Romodi to execute the Amidalans on Ondara. Following this development, Velgor and his team of assassins traveled to Tranchar to capture and interrogate Lieutenant Grapa about the mission. After fighting their way through shock troopers and DT-series sentry droids , the assassins discovered civilians being tortured by the Imperials. Gee-Ninety broke from the mission to free the civilians, prompting Velgor to destroy the droid as punishment. Velgor went on to interrogate Grapa about her mission to kill the Amidalans.

As she claimed to Velgor that they were all dead, Tanka blasted her in the head for revenge. Velgor then realized that the Imperials had known they were coming. When Velgor asked Varrick who had sent the droids, Varrick confirmed they were from Romodi's command. Velgor then remembered that Orrin had spoken with Romodi prior to their departure. When he confronted Orrin with that fact, Orrin tried to stammer out an explanation before giving up and running away while Varrick fired at him. After stealing a spare ship, Orrin escaped from Tranchar and eventually arrived on Ondara to meet with Sable. Velgor followed the assassin in his SKREE Advanced. As soon as Orrin stepped out of his ship, Velgor revealed himself and destroyed it with his SKREE. After landing in front of Orrin, Velgor revealed he had known of his allegiance to the Dawn and gave the assassin an out by commending him for leading him to Sable, which Orrin went along with and claimed was his plan all along. As Velgor confronted Sable, he took her Carmine Dawn badge from her and claimed that he was the Dawn.

#### Recruiting Sable

> " Does nothing scare you, handmaiden? " " Why should I be scared of Kael Drayden? " ―Xarn Velgor and Sable

Following this, Velgor took Orrin, Sable, and ZEV-6-7 to the carcass of the sando aqua monster he had slain. He then threw Orrin and Sable out of the shuttle and watched as they were attacked by a pack of ravenous creatures . Orrin attempted to assure Velgor of his loyalty, but Velgor silenced him and asked Sable why she believed in Carmine Dawn. As she defended herself, she said that she had merely infiltrated the syndicate. Orrin fought alongside her and claimed he had done the same for the Imperium. When Velgor asked Sable if she also intended to serve the Imperium, Sable said she did not as long as the Imperium served chaos. Sable went on to claim that she and Velgor wanted the same thing: order.

To this, Velgor levitated the creatures with Synth Flux and told Sable that if she killed one monster, a thousand more would replace it. He then explained that one would have to either make the monsters agree or deal with them all. With that, Velgor threw the creatures at a group of long-necked beasts as they sprouted from the ground. Understanding Velgor's plan, Sable brought him to a Carmine Dawn outpost while Orrin returned to the Sovereign to speak with Romodi. Upon arriving at the outpost, Sable informed the Dawners there that they served Velgor now. To this, the Dawners attacked Velgor and were easily cut down by the Umbra Lord. Velgor then had Orrin use Varrick and the assassins as bait to draw the remaining Dawners and Romodi's forces to Oro Sankeria . As the Dawners and Imperials fought one another, they were suddenly wiped out by a pulse bomb, thus eliminating many of Velgor's enemies. Velgor informed Sable that Carmine Dawn and Romodi feared him now. When he asked Sable if she was scared, Sable asked him why she should be scared of his true self, Kael Drayden.

Velgor responded that Kael Drayden was dead. But Sable revealed that she had deduced Velgor's identity from his obsession with order, his investigation into Lira's death, and Lira's final words at Polis Massa. When Velgor drew his flux blade and asked Sable what she wanted, she claimed she wanted to help him achieve order. She went on to explain that when Drayden never returned to Dunemere to free his mother, she and Tonra secretly worked in her honor to free enslaved people and smuggle them from Dunemere to a colony on Gabredor III . Now, the colony was being exploited and neglected by the corrupt Governor Tauntaza , who was in league with Carmine Dawn. Sable told Velgor that if he helped her save the refugees, he would get the chance to destroy another Carmine Dawn cell and prove that Lira was right about him. To this, Velgor withdrew his flux blade and accepted Sable's proposal. He then warned her that she alone was responsible for where her path took her before they silently boarded the shuttle and left for Gabredor III.

#### Familiar faces

> " Your names? " " K-Kitster. " " «Wald.» " ―Xarn Velgor, Kitster Banai, and Wald

As they approached the planet, they were met by Tauntaza's flagship . When Velgor asked ZEV-6-7 if the ship's crew had detected them, the droid said they had not yet. Velgor then told him to take them to the planet's surface. Sable suggested summoning the Sovereign , but Velgor said they would first visit the colony to see if she was telling the truth. Once they landed at the colony, they stepped out of the ship as ZEV-6-7 introduced Velgor to the refugees and asked to see their protocol droid . Since their protocol droid had long broken down, ZEV-6-7 instead spoke with their astromech droid Em-Sevenbee . The droid and two of the refugees then confirmed Sable's story. The pair of refugees then showed off a terrestrial vehicle they had built, inspired by their earlier lives on Dunemere. When Velgor asked the refugees for their names, they introduced themselves as Kitster and Wald; Drayden's childhood friends.

When Kitster asked Velgor if they could do anything for him, Velgor told them they could do nothing. He then sensed that the surrounding land was disturbed, to which ZEV-6-7 explained that it was currently suffering from drought. When another refugee claimed things would get better once the clouds came back, Velgor said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Velgor and Sable fought off the creatures, a few of them surrounded Kitster and Wald. Velgor then leapt in and cut them down, saving the refugees. Kitster thanked Velgor as the Umbra Lord silently walked away. Sable then asked Velgor if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Tauntaza. However, Velgor told Sable she was not ready, since the refugees had ignored her order to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her. Afterwards, Velgor had Sable don a set of Imperial equipment and recruited her into the Imperial Military under the rank of Lieutenant Commander, granting her authority over all the forces he gathered.

He then ordered an Imperial officer to open a channel to the Sovereign . When the officer reported that Tauntaza's ship was blocking their communications, Velgor ordered for his shuttle to be prepared. He and his team then traveled to the governor's facility and proceeded to fight their way towards the governor. After cutting down a few repurposed battle droids, Velgor was confronted by Tauntaza, who briefly subdued the Umbra Lord with a life-draining weapon. Velgor's team was then attacked by more battle droids and a few Carmine Dawn assassins. Once Velgor recovered, he Synth Flux-choked Orrin for allowing the governor to escape. Orrin explained that he was assisting Velgor, to which Velgor asked Orrin if he thought Tauntaza's weapon could destroy him. Sable then told Velgor that it might have, prompting Velgor to release Orrin and meet with one of the weapon's designers, Doctor Aira . Aira explained that the technology had been used to raze the planet Karolia and expressed remorse over not having known what the technology would be used for. As Sable reassured the rest of the regretful designers, Velgor seized Orrin's speeder and left to find the governor.

Catching up to Tauntaza and her escorts, Velgor blocked a blast from one of her battle droids before reaching her at her escape ship. As the ship took off, Velgor used Synth Flux to hold it and destroy its interior controls. However, the ship's powerful engines ultimately allowed the governor to make her escape. Using a pair of Imperials as a diversion, Velgor boarded Tauntaza's flagship and fought off more battle droids and Imperials before confronting the one in charge. When Velgor asked where Tauntaza was, the Imperial claimed there had been a misunderstanding and recommended that the Umbra reconsider his "hostile stance". Velgor then had the ship's crew open a channel to his own ship and ordered Vett to lock onto the governor's flagship. After giving Vett the order to destroy the ship unless countermanded, Velgor asked the Imperial again for the governor's location. Upon learning the location, Velgor returned to his shuttle and flew toward the facility where she was hiding, only to be met by a sandstorm generated by the facility.

Despite the pilots' warnings, Velgor told them to continue approaching the facility while he held the ship together with Synth Flux. A lightning bolt then struck the ship, causing it to crash into the forest. Once Velgor stepped out of the wreckage, he was met by Orrin, who he asked for Sable's location. Orrin reported that the storm had taken her and a colonist claimed that the storm would kill them all. When Velgor asked the colonist if they had any ships, he said the only ship that had not been confiscated or destroyed was Kitster's racer. When the colonist said that only Kitster could drive it, Velgor said they would see as he approached the racer. ZEV-6-7 told Velgor that he could not trace Sable or the other colonists, to which Velgor told the droid he would command his assistance if he required it. Faced with numerous flashbacks to his past, Velgor then boarded the racer and sped off into the storm. While deflecting debris with his flux blade, Velgor located Kitster on the ground alongside a large tank. Landing next to him, Velgor asked Kitster where Sable was. Kitster explained that she was trapped beneath the tank and that he had been trying to move it. Once Velgor lifted the tank off of Sable, the three of them were overwhelmed by the surrounding storm. Velgor then contacted Vett and ordered him to have the Sovereign fire on his location.

When Vett warned that would incinerate everyone and everything within range, Velgor choked him with Synth Flux, prompting him to open fire. The resulting bombardment destroyed the tank and dissipated the storm. After cutting himself out from the remains of the tank, Velgor took Sable from Kitster and carried her into the distance. Eventually, the three of them returned to the colonists and Velgor ordered Vett to open fire on Tauntaza's facility. However, the blasts were deflected by a shield, which ZEV-6-7 explained was drawing energy from the planet. Despite Orrin's doubts, Velgor remained certain the fortress possessed a weakness and, when ZEV-6-7 revealed that the facility's underside was unshielded, took Em-Sevenbee with him to the facility. While he defeated more monsters on his way there, Velgor placed his flux blade within the astromech's dome. Once they arrived at the facility, he had the droid fire the flux blade upwards into the unshielded underbelly. Velgor then used Synth Flux to guide the flux blade through the facility, disabling the weapon and carving an entrance for himself. With the shield down, Velgor entered the facility and struck down Tauntaza's soldiers.

However, he was then met by Tauntaza herself, wearing a suit of powerful armor. Tauntaza used the suit's arm cannon to fire at Velgor. As he blocked the beam with his flux blade, Velgor told the governor she could not contain the power she was wielding. To this, Tauntaza accused Velgor of having no control over his own power before he destroyed her arm cannon. Tauntaza then flew away as the facility exploded, leaving Velgor severely injured without any power left in his suit. Sable soon found Velgor and begrudgingly restored his suit's power, allowing them to walk back to the colony. Once they returned, they learned from ZEV-6-7 that Tauntaza had been receiving her orders from the Emperor himself. When Velgor asked Sable why she was smiling, she said she now knew why she had saved him. Returning to his fortress, Velgor healed in his bacta tank, but broke it in anger upon remembering Sable's words about the good still in him.

Later, Velgor boarded the Sovereign and was met by Varrick, Haydenn, and the Revengers. Velgor chastised them for being early, to which Varrick demanded to know why they were there. Velgor then used Synth Flux to fix Varrick's collar, saying he expected those who served him to be held to a certain standard. Haydenn claimed that Varrick had the team operating at peak efficiency. To this, Velgor said they would get a chance to prove their loyalty and advised Varrick to channel his energies in a less self-destructive manner. When Varrick asked what their mission was, Vett explained that they had been selected to protect a medicine shipment to Bestine IV from Carmine Dawn. Velgor warned the mercenaries not to disappoint him as he would be watching before they left for their mission. Velgor then met with Sable and asked her about Tauntaza's location. When she said Orrin was searching for the governor, Velgor asked her what she advised they do. To this, Sable told Velgor he should do what he always did: directly confront the problem.

#### Return to Skako Minor

> " Follow that speeder. Infiltrate their organization. Find Jul Tambra. I will take care of the rest. " ―Xarn Velgor, to Sable

Following Sable's counsel, Velgor and Sable arrived at the Imperial Palace on Vantor aboard a Vectis -class shuttle to confront Mal. As the two were unexpected, one of Mal's advisors tried to stop the Dark Lord, but Velgor only reacted by throwing the advisor against a wall before making his way into the Emperor's throne room. Although his two present Royal Guards pointed their Synth Flux pikes at Velgor, Mal reacted by calmly asking Velgor what his heart told him. Velgor proceeded to hold Sable in the air with Synth Flux as Mal laughed and claimed to her that she had only reminded Velgor, who was remembering moments of his past at the same time, of his weakness. Dropping her, Velgor refused to assist Sable as the Emperor had his Royal Guards attempt to destroy her. Once she outwitted them in combat, Mal killed the guards in anger and, seeing her worth as an agent, allowed Sable to continue working with Velgor.

As they returned to their shuttle, Velgor and Sable were informed by ZEV-6-7 of their next mission. Jul Tambra , the grandson of Wat Tambra, was working to overthrow the pro-Imperial regime on Skako Minor through a nationalist uprising . According to Imperial intelligence, Tambra had also enlisted pirates to blockade the planet and stockpile technology to be used against the Imperium. As such, their mission was to end the Skakoan's uprising and restore Imperial control over Skako Minor. In truth, Tambra had known he would have to face Velgor as part of his crusade. Over the course of the last six years, the Skakoan had worked to collect and reassemble droids who had been killed by Velgor, thereby allowing him to analyze their databanks to study Velgor's moves. Amongst the droids he reassembled were B1 battle droids, B2 super battle droids, and Gee-Ninety. Tambra was very confident that he would be able to kill Velgor.

Upon reaching the planet, Velgor and Sable discovered a crashed commercial transport from Korrath . ZEV-6-7 explained that the ship had been boarded and those inside were killed by the planet's poisonous atmosphere. As they traversed the terrain, Velgor killed a pack of cliff worms , saving a group of pirates. When he noticed a pile of corpses on the ground, he struck down the pirates, while Sable hesitated and allowed one of them to escape. When Velgor asked her if the pirates deserved mercy, Sable admitted she didn't know who did anymore. Velgor reminded her that she was his shadow now and she said they would need one of the pirates alive in order to locate Tambra. To this, Velgor instructed Sable to follow the surviving pirate and find Tambra while he took care of the rest.

#### Confronting Varrick

> " Beilert Varrick…you have proven to be a disappointment. A betrayal that will not be tolerated. " ―Xarn Velgor

Upon learning that Varrick had betrayed the Imperium, Velgor boarded the Righteous Fist and had Haydenn accompany him as he approached the surface of Bestine IV in his shuttle. Once they landed, Velgor and Haydenn confronted Varrick and the rest of the Edgehawk ' s crew. Drawing his flux blade, Velgor told Varrick that his desertion would not be tolerated. Charging into battle, Varrick told Velgor that he regretted ever serving the Imperium. As they fought, Velgor took note of Varrick's power, which he regarded as proof Varrick was a waste of potential. Once he was beaten into submission, Varrick said that the planet's people would still be free of the Imperium. However, Velgor revealed that Varrick had doomed them as his forces bombed the locals, as well as his troops.

Ignoring Varrick's chastisation of his lack of care for his own, Velgor prepared to strike Varrick down, but Haydenn insisted on finishing the job herself to make up for her previous failures, which Velgor allowed. Varrick tried to convince Haydenn to leave the Imperium with him, but Haydenn blasted him in the face and sent him falling over the edge of the cliff. Before leaving the planet, Velgor contacted Haydenn and ordered her to send in a group of Imperials to hunt Varrick down in case he survived. To accomplish this task, Velgor personally selected Inferno Squad .

#### Unmasking the Dawn

> " I did not train this woman. But perhaps…the one who trained her. If you had found this Vessa in a royal court, I would think Varek had a hand in this. But she wallows in the mud of the criminal syndicates, so the more likely answer is… " " Vreth . " ―Vael Mal and Xarn Velgor

As Sable searched for Jul Tambra, Velgor was called to the Imperial Palace by Mal.
